### [1] [Grid-cell firing fields lack local sixfold symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31145)
*Anian Kerscher*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究通过谐波分析与匹配仿真，检验单个网格场是否存在局部六重对称调制，发现实验网格场的六重谐波功率与局部圆形参考场一致，表明全局六边形格点组织不必然伴随可检测的单个放电场局部六重角调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格细胞的放电场构成六边形格点，但单个场的内在几何结构尚不清楚：是径向对称，还是继承了全局格点的（弱）六重调制？厘清这一问题对理解网格场的微观结构及网格形成机制模型具有定量约束意义。

Method: 采用谐波分析量化场内的角结构，并构建逐细胞匹配仿真：在校正全局椭圆形变后，保持采样统计、场尺度与格点几何一致。将实验场的六重谐波功率分数与匹配的局部圆形参考场比较，并以施加局部六重成分的匹配仿真检验灵敏度；辅以六重回归指标、谐波留一敏感性分析，以及径向窗口、平滑和模拟背景噪声水平的稳健性检验；同时使用观测匹配的 Burak–Fiete 连续吸引子网络进行验证。

Result: 在所研究的细胞中，六重谐波功率分数与匹配的局部圆形参考场数值一致；实验场的六重调制显著少于施加局部六重成分的匹配仿真，说明该指标对该水平的六重结构具备敏感性。结论得到六重回归指标、留一敏感性分析与多项稳健性检验的支持。观测匹配的 Burak–Fiete 网络尽管具有全局周期性格点结构，也未表现出选择性的局部六重增强，且在更贴近实验的网格尺度与相对场宽匹配下结论依旧成立。

Conclusion: 全局六边形格点组织不必然伴随单个放电场的可检测局部六重角调制，这为网格场微结构及网格形成的机制模型提供了定量约束。

Abstract: The firing fields of a grid cell form a hexagonal lattice, yet each field's intrinsic geometric structure remains unclear: Are individual grid fields radially symmetric or do they exhibit (weak) sixfold modulation inherited from the global lattice? To address this question, we quantified the within-field angular structure using harmonic analysis and per-cell matched simulations that preserved sampling statistics, field scale, and lattice geometry after correction for global elliptic deformation. We found that, across the investigated cells, the sixfold harmonic power fraction was consistent with values from matched locally circular reference fields. Experimental fields further showed substantially less sixfold modulation than matched simulations with an imposed local sixfold component, demonstrating sensitivity to sixfold structure at that level. This conclusion was supported by a complementary sixfold regression metric, harmonic leave-one-out sensitivity analyses, and robustness checks across radial-window choices, smoothing, and simulated background-noise levels. An observation-matched Burak--Fiete continuous-attractor network likewise showed no selective local sixfold enhancement despite its globally periodic grid structure, and this conclusion persisted when grid scale and relative field width were matched more closely to the experimental regime. These results suggest that the global hexagonal lattice organization need not be accompanied by detectable local sixfold angular modulation of individual firing fields, placing quantitative constraints on grid-field microstructure and mechanistic models of grid formation.

</details>


### [2] [Learning a non-linguistic code for inferred rules from reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31192)
*Cristiano Capone*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文将信息传递问题建模为两个网络的通信博弈：说话者看样例后发出自创符号，盲听者将其应用于新输入；在听者成功率的奖励下，说话者习得一种编码，可传达训练中未出现的三步变换规则，并被新学习者在无共享代码条件下掌握。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是为"规则诱导的迁移与沟通"提供机制解释：如何在无共享代码的前提下，把从样例中推断出的规则传给从未见过样例的接收者。人类以手势或草图实现（如重度失语症患者），作者希望在人工通信系统中复现并解释这一过程。

Method: 方法为双网络通信博弈。说话者网络观看已解样例并输出8个自创符号（离散消息）；听者网络对这些符号不可见来源，仅依据消息把规则应用于新输入。以听者任务成功率作为奖励驱动说话者学习；进一步引入表达性压力以保持消息差异化，并将奖励信号与听者的误差梯度进行对比。

Result: 结果显示：经奖励训练后，说话者能习得承载规则的编码，成功传达训练中从未出现的三步变换规则，且新学习者可获得该编码。编码呈现两种机制态——仅靠奖励时说话者漂移到单一消息（类似人类语言在纯传播下失词），表达性压力则维持消息区分度。在新规则上的成功率取决于消息对规则的传达量，而非消息多样性。学习信号塑造编码：纯奖励把多条规则压缩到少量固定标签之下；听者的误差梯度为每条规则划出相似消息区域，从而显著更好地区分规则。

Conclusion: 研究揭示：语言式编码的出现与规则可迁移性由学习信号的结构决定。听者误差梯度比单纯成功奖励提供更细的区分信息，使消息空间形成按规则聚类的区域，从而支撑规则的系统化表征与跨学习者传递，为无共享代码条件下的规则交流提供了机制性解释。

Abstract: How can a rule inferred from examples reach someone who never saw them, without a shared code? Patients with severe aphasia do it by gesture or sketch. One network sees worked examples and emits eight invented symbols; a second, blind to them, applies them to a new input. Rewarded for the second's success, the first learns a code carrying rules to three-step transformations training never presents, which new learners acquire. Like invented human languages, the code has two regimes: under reward alone the speaker drifts to one message, as human languages lose words under plain transmission; expressive pressure keeps messages differentiated. Success on new rules tracks how much the message says about the rule, not how varied messages are. The learning signal shapes the code: reward sorts many rules under few fixed labels; the listener's error gradient gives each rule a region of similar messages, telling rules apart far better.

</details>

### [3] [HybridInfer: Thermal-Aware Reinforcement-Learning Tier Routing for On-Device, Edge, and Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30270)
*Simran Koul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HybridInfer，一种热感知强化学习路由器，在真机（旗舰骁龙设备）上的三层架构（端侧 Llama 3.2 3B、边缘 Llama 3.1 8B+检索、云 GPT-4o）中，利用手机热余量与查询复杂度作为状态，通过离线 Q-learning 选择推理层级；在 210 条提示的 Android 实测中显著优于两个手工调优启发式且成本最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧小模型推理能保证数据本地化、可离线、无单次查询成本，因此在能力足够时是首选，但其受热约束限制。作者发现该约束并非单纯降速：在旗舰骁龙设备上，持续端侧生成会使 GPU 推理运行时失稳，连续数次查询后崩溃或静默卡死。故障根源在于当前工具链（移动 GPU 上的 OpenCL 内核编译与长提示预填充），即使设备处于低温状态也会复现，且长文本生成时最严重。跨端/边缘/云的多元路由可缓解压力，但现有路由器热盲，且多在仿真或非移动硬件上评估。

Method: HybridInfer 采用离线训练的 Q-learning 策略，以手机热余量和查询复杂度估计为状态，在三层层级中选择执行层级。奖励函数在质量、延迟、成本与热惩罚之间权衡，并加入"本地性奖励"以补偿端侧执行。作者论证该奖励是热感知路由的前提条件：若缺少它，最优策略会将所有查询卸载至上层。系统在真实 Android 基准上以 210 条提示进行评测，并与两个手工调优启发式及 always-on-device 条件对比。

Result: 在 210 条提示的真实 Android 基准上，学得的路由器质量显著高于两个手工调优启发式（配对 Wilcoxon 检验，p < 0.02），且在所有自适应条件中成本最低。always-on-device 条件在可服务查询上达到与路由器相当的逐查询质量，但速度慢三到六倍，且长查询会失败。因此在延迟、可靠性与覆盖范围上路由方案胜出，而非在质量上胜出。

Conclusion: 热感知路由的价值主要体现在延迟、可靠性与覆盖率，而非单查询质量。本地性奖励是防止策略退化为全部卸载的必要设计。据作者所知，这是首次在真实硬件上利用端侧热余量在能力不同的 LLM 推理层级间进行选择。

Abstract: On-device inference with small language models keeps user data local, works offline, and incurs no per-query cost, so the on-device tier is preferred when it is adequate. It is thermally constrained, however, and I find the constraint is sharper than a slowdown: on a flagship Snapdragon device, sustained on-device generation destabilizes the GPU inference runtime, which crashes or silently wedges after a few consecutive queries. The failure lies in the current toolchain (OpenCL kernel compilation and long-prompt prefill on the mobile GPU), recurs even when the device is cool, and is worst for long generations. Multi-tier routers across on-device, edge, and cloud models can relieve this pressure, but existing routers are thermal-blind and typically evaluated in simulation or on non-mobile hardware. I present HybridInfer, a thermal-aware reinforcement-learning router for a three-tier hierarchy (on-device Llama 3.2 3B, edge Llama 3.1 8B with retrieval, cloud GPT-4o) that uses the phone's thermal headroom and a query-complexity estimate as state and selects a tier by an offline-trained Q-learning policy. Its reward trades quality against latency, cost, and a thermal penalty, plus a locality bonus crediting on-device execution. I show this bonus is a precondition for thermal-aware routing: without it the optimal policy offloads every query. On a real Android benchmark of 210 prompts, the learned router attains significantly higher quality than two hand-tuned heuristics (paired Wilcoxon, p < 0.02) at the lowest cost of any adaptive condition. Always-on-device conditions match per-query quality on servable queries but are three to six times slower and fail on long queries, so routing wins on latency, reliability, and coverage rather than quality. To my knowledge this is the first use of on-device thermal headroom to select among LLM inference tiers of differing capability on real hardware.

</details>


### [4] [When the Preconditioning Exponent Turns Negative: Learning-Rate Coupling and Cross-Environment Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30271)
*Gongyue Zhang,Honghai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 学习率与预条件指数存在耦合：最优指数随log10(η)近似线性下降，源域验证选择会与跨环境鲁棒性目标冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器通常固定二阶矩估计的幂次p；已有部分自适应方法多研究动量式更新与Adam平方根之间的指数，但p与全局学习率η的交互作用理解不足。

Method: 构建受控跨环境研究：四环境配对分类任务，包含稳定稀疏特征、环境相关虚假稀疏特征、稠密特征和高维噪声。共进行\NumRuns{}次源域训练，覆盖21个预条件指数p∈[-0.5,0.5]与5个学习率η∈[10^-4,10^-2]。

Result: 最大化跨环境准确率的p随log10(η)近似线性下降，拟合斜率-0.270至-0.300，R^2为0.972至0.996。η=10^-2时，源验证在所有环境偏好正p，而跨环境与最差环境准则偏好负p。检查点分解显示较低p降低虚假-稳定与噪声-稳定权重比，并在相关性反转时减小有害虚假间隔幅度。

Conclusion: 负p并非普适最优设置，而是由学习率与预条件共同作用产生的高步长分配机制；研究揭示模型选择冲突：源域验证系统性地选择不同于最大化环境变化鲁棒性的预条件机制。该结果为单随机种子、有限预算的机制研究，而非广泛基准结论。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers are commonly parameterized by a fixed power of the second-moment estimate. Existing partially adaptive methods study exponents between momentum-like updates and the standard Adam square root, while the interaction between this exponent and the global learning rate is less understood. We perform a controlled cross-environment study using a paired four-environment classification problem with stable sparse features, environment-dependent spurious sparse features, dense features, and high-dimensional noise. Across \NumRuns{} source-training runs covering 21 preconditioning exponents $p\in[-0.5,0.5]$ and five learning rates $η\in[10^{-4},10^{-2}]$, we find that the exponent maximizing cross-environment accuracy decreases almost linearly with $\log_{10}η$. The fitted slopes range from $-0.270$ to $-0.300$, with $R^2$ between $0.972$ and $0.996$. At $η=10^{-2}$, source-validation selection still prefers positive exponents in all four environments, whereas cross-environment and worst-environment criteria prefer negative exponents. Checkpoint decomposition shows that lower $p$ reduces the learned spurious-to-stable and noise-to-stable weight ratios; under reversed correlation, it also reduces the magnitude of the harmful spurious margin. Negative $p$ is therefore not a universally optimal setting. It is a high-step-size allocation regime produced by the joint action of learning rate and preconditioning. The study also exposes a model-selection conflict: source-domain validation systematically selects a different preconditioning regime from the one that maximizes robustness to environmental change. The results are a single-seed, finite-budget mechanism study rather than a broad benchmark claim.

</details>


### [5] [Common-Mode Collapse and Recovery in Direct Feedback Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31589)
*Varun Reddy,Bernardo L. Sabatini,Houman Safaai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将直接反馈对齐(DFA)训练中的停滞现象归因于输出误差的公共模态(common mode),并通过均值-协方差分解揭示其导致tanh隐藏单元饱和的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFA通过输出误差的固定随机投影训练隐藏层,但在tanh隐藏单元和独立sigmoid输出下,普通SGD会停滞在接近常数频率预测器的损失水平。作者试图解释这一停滞的根源。

Method: 作者将停滞追溯到误差的公共模态(跨输入共享的分量),并进行精确的均值-协方差分解,分离出由平均教学信号与平均突触前活动构成的秩一更新。此外构建了一个从网络初始化、无拟合参数的简化模型来预测激活敏感性集中现象,并在48种设置下验证。

Result: 简化模型成功预测了48种设置下激活敏感性的集中。在MNIST上,类别可解码性在坍缩后大体保留,但固定学习率下读出层学习缓慢;Adam学习更快但坍缩更深。将基线读出层校准到类别先验可抑制坍缩并加速学习;用误差符号替代误差会维持坍缩;减去信号的批次均值可防止持续坍缩并改善学习。更深网络、卷积网络及CIFAR-10上也出现相关效应,严重程度和代价取决于读出层、优化器与输入统计。

Conclusion: DFA的停滞源于误差公共模态经秩一更新驱动tanh单元饱和,而随机反馈在初始化时无法系统纠正共享误差。校准读出层、减去批次均值等干预可缓解坍缩并改善学习,相关效应具有跨架构与数据集的普适性。

Abstract: Direct feedback alignment (DFA) trains hidden layers through fixed random projections of output error. With tanh hidden units and independent sigmoid outputs, plain stochastic gradient descent can stall near the loss of a constant predictor of class frequencies. We trace this stall to the error's common mode, the component shared across inputs. An exact mean-covariance decomposition separates a rank-one update formed by the mean teaching signal and mean presynaptic activity. Its leading component drives tanh units toward saturation. At initialization, random feedback provides no systematic correction of the shared error on average; readout learning limits its duration. A reduced model initialized from the network, without fitted parameters, predicts the concentration of activation sensitivity across 48 settings. On MNIST, class decodability largely survives collapse, but readout learning remains slow at a fixed learning rate. Adam learns faster despite deeper collapse. Calibrating the baseline readout to the class prior suppresses collapse and speeds learning; weaker feedback trades less collapse for slower learning. Replacing errors by their signs sustains collapse; subtracting the signal's batch mean prevents sustained collapse and improves learning in the tested setting. Related effects occur in deeper and convolutional networks and on CIFAR-10, with severity and cost depending on the readout, optimizer and input statistics.

</details>


### [6] [ENAS: An Efficient Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search Framework for TinyML on Resource-Constrained Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30272)
*Mohd Moin Khan,Naman Srivastava,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ENAS：一种面向MCU的无GPU硬件感知NAS框架，通过静态可行性检查、三种block的cell搜索空间与三阶段混合搜索（随机→Top-K→变异）+跨运行缓存，在TinyML基准上取得2.41×/1.70×搜索加速，并显著降低峰值激活RAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS框架多依赖GPU加速，难以在资源受限的开发环境中使用；同时微控制器部署的真正瓶颈是峰值激活RAM而非参数量，因此需要一种无需GPU、硬件感知且以内存可行性为约束的NAS方法。

Method: 提出ENAS框架，包含三部分：(1) 静态可行性检查，在搜索前剔除不满足MCU内存约束的架构；(2) 基于cell的搜索空间，支持标准卷积、深度可分离卷积和瓶颈block，并可选用skip连接；(3) 三阶段混合搜索策略（随机采样→Top-K筛选→变异），配合持久化跨运行缓存以复用历史评估结果。整个流程无需GPU。

Result: 在Visual Wake Words与Melanoma Cancer两个TinyML基准上，覆盖8款MCU（20KB–1MB SRAM）和9种输入分辨率；相比NanoNAS，平均搜索时间加速分别为2.41×和1.70×，测试精度保持竞争力；实测资源分析显示在同等精度下所选模型峰值激活RAM显著更低；在STM32H743上取得79.4%测试精度，比贪心CPU-only基线高2.6个百分点。

Conclusion: ENAS表明，在不依赖GPU的前提下，结合静态可行性检查与混合搜索策略可有效加速面向MCU的NAS，并命中MCU部署的关键约束（峰值激活RAM），为资源受限环境下的TinyML模型设计提供了可行方案；框架已开源。

Abstract: We present \textbf{ENAS}, a hardware-aware Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework that combines a static feasibility check, a cell-based search space supporting standard, depthwise-separable, and bottleneck blocks with optional skip connections, and a three-stage hybrid search strategy (random $\rightarrow$ top-$K$ $\rightarrow$ mutation) with persistent cross-run caching. Unlike many existing NAS frameworks that rely on GPU acceleration, ENAS is designed to operate efficiently without requiring GPUs, making it suitable for resource-constrained development environments. We evaluate ENAS on two TinyML benchmarks, Visual Wake Words and Melanoma Cancer, across eight microcontrollers with memory footprints ranging from 20\,KB to 1\,MB SRAM and nine input image resolutions. Our experimental results show that ENAS achieves mean search-time speedups of $2.41{\times}$ and $1.70{\times}$ on the Visual Wake Words and Melanoma Cancer datasets, respectively, while maintaining competitive test accuracy compared with the recent NanoNAS framework. A measured resource analysis further shows that ENAS-selected models use substantially lower peak activation RAM, the binding constraint for microcontroller deployment at matched accuracy. Additionally, ENAS achieves $79.4\%$ test accuracy on an STM32H743-based microcontroller, outperforming the greedy CPU-only baseline by $2.6$ percentage points. We release the ENAS framework as open-source at: https://github.com/EdgeIntelligenceLab/ENAS

</details>


### [7] [Offline Policy Evaluation as a decision support tool for designing Adaptive Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30273)
*João Victor Ferreira Alves,Eduardo Rocha Laurentino,Gustavo de Oliveira Kanno,Thiago Costa Rizuti da Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合离策略评估（OPE）与“受控暖启动仿真”的方法，利用固定随机实验（A/B测试）的历史数据评估并筛选上下文赌博机自适应策略，判断何时自适应实验优于原始固定分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定A/B测试数据中蕴含异质性处理效应，但如何安全、可信地判断某种自适应（上下文赌博机）策略能否超越原始静态分配、以及适用条件，仍缺乏可落地的方法论。

Method: 将OPE与受控暖启动仿真结合：从记录的A/B数据估计nuisance成分，用双重稳健估计器对预设的自适应与非自适应策略组合排序；在可获取ground truth时，将离线训练策略部署到复用真实奖励概率生成机制的仿真器中，为暖启动下的离线到在线迁移提供ground-truth锚定的安全环境。在具有已知异质性结构和oracle策略的合成RCT上验证，并在Hillstrom、Criteo Uplift、LaLonde等基准上从策略价值与regret视角复核。

Result: 当存在有意义的异质性时，自适应、上下文感知策略优于固定分配；当缺乏异质性时收益很小。结论在合成RCT与标准公开基准上保持一致。

Conclusion: 提供了一套实用方法论：利用已有A/B测试数据判断自适应实验是否值得部署，并在竞争性自适应策略中进行选择。

Abstract: We investigate how historical data from fixed randomized experiments (A/B tests) can be used to inform the deployment of adaptive experiments based on contextual bandits. Given data collected under a static allocation, our goal is to assess which adaptive policies, if any, would have outperformed the original design and under what conditions. To this end, we combine off-policy evaluation (OPE) with a controlled warm-start simulation. From logged A/B test data exhibiting heterogeneous treatment effects, we estimate nuisance components and use doubly robust estimators to rank a portfolio of pre-specified adaptive and non-adaptive policies. When ground truth is available, we then deploy the same offline-trained policies in a simulator that reuses the exact data-generating reward probabilities, providing a safe, ground-truth-anchored environment to study the offline-to-online transition under warm starting. Using synthetic randomized controlled trials with known heterogeneity structures and an oracle policy, our results indicate that adaptive, context-aware policies improve upon fixed allocations when meaningful heterogeneity is present, while providing little benefit in its absence. We reinforce our findings on standard open benchmarks (Hillstrom, Criteo Uplift, and LaLonde), reinterpreted through a policy-value and regret perspective. Overall, our results provide a practical methodology for deciding when adaptive experimentation is worth deploying and how to select among competing adaptive policies using existing A/B test data.

</details>


### [8] [Cosine Similarity Is Not Evidence: Measuring the Noise Floor of Interpretability Transfer Under Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30275)
*Pranav Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 可解释性伪影在量化权重上的"幸存"通常用尺度不变统计量（如余弦相似度）论证，但这类统计量缺少噪声下限，因而无法作为证据；本文以 difference-in-means 方向估计器为例，给出分半下限的单参数闭式，并在真实模型上测量类别分离度 ρ，进而重新评估满精度与量化拒绝方向之间的余弦相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机是指出 AI 安全实践中一个具体漏洞：可解释性伪影由满精度权重校准、却部署在量化权重上，而现有压缩迁移研究普遍报告尺度不变统计量（余弦相似度、相关系数、AUROC）却不报告其噪声下限，甚至不报告计算该统计量所用的样本量 n，使得"保持"或"幸存"的结论无法被解读为证据。

Method: 针对 difference-in-means 方向估计器，推导出分半（split-half）噪声下限由一个无量纲数 κ = nρ²/d 支配，并给出闭式 E[cos] ≈ (1+4/κ)^{-1}；关键缺失输入是类别分离度 ρ，作者在真实激活（Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct）上逐层测量 ρ。当 n 已知时，将每个低位余弦与"在该量化模型内测得的分半零假设"对比，而不使用满精度零假设（后者默认低位估计器方差相同，而这正是零假设应检验的假设）。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 上，ρ = 33–61 随深度变化，因此两次独立估计仅靠采样即可达到 0.978–0.994 的余弦一致性。据此，已发表的满精度与量化拒绝方向之间 0.996 的余弦，在未报告 n 的情况下不能解读为"保持"。在已知 n 的情形中：INT4 下方向发生旋转，且其偏差超过估计器自身噪声；INT8 下未检测到移动（但这并非等价性结论）。此外，尺度不变统计量无法区分传输决策变量的平移与衰减，而两者需要相反的补救措施。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：没有噪声下限（尤其是 n）的尺度不变统计量不构成压缩迁移"保持"的证据；应以量化模型内部测得的分半零假设作为判据。作者给出仅需一次前向传播的报告建议，并公开代码、数据和单单元格复现（https://github.com/pvarshh/quantinterp）。

Abstract: A statistic reported without the quantity needed to interpret it is not evidence. We develop that thesis for a concrete practice in AI safety. Interpretability artifacts are calibrated on full-precision weights, deployed on quantized ones, and certified as surviving the change by scale-invariant statistics (cosine similarity, correlation, AUROC) that are reported without their noise floor. For the difference-in-means direction estimator, the split-half floor is governed by one dimensionless number, $κ= nρ^2/d$. The closed form $\mathbb{E}[\cos] \approx (1+4/κ)^{-1}$ is classical; the missing input is the class separation $ρ$, which we measure on real activations; no compression-transfer study we know of reports it. On Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, $ρ= 33$--$61$ across depth, so two independent runs of the estimator agree to $0.978$--$0.994$ by sampling alone. A published cosine of $0.996$ between full-precision and quantized refusal directions therefore cannot be read as preservation without the $n$ it was computed at, which is not reported. Where $n$ is known, we judge each low-bit cosine against the split-half null measured within that quantized model, because a full-precision null assumes the low-bit estimator has the same variance. That assumption is exactly what a null exists to test. The result is plain: at INT4 the direction rotated, and the deficit exceeds the estimator's own noise. At INT8 we detect no movement, which is not an equivalence claim. We also show that a scale-invariant statistic cannot distinguish translation from attenuation of a transferred decision variable, although the two call for opposite remedies. We close with reporting recommendations that cost one forward pass. Code, data, and a one-cell reproduction are released at https://github.com/pvarshh/quantinterp

</details>


### [9] [Why Clipping Matters in AdaGrad? Toward a High-Probability Theory under Generalized Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30276)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Ayaan Mohd,Harshit Rawat,Arnab Roy,Mayank Baranwal,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究广义光滑性与重尾噪声下有界方差条件下的原始同步坐标级AdaGrad，指出了无裁剪AdaGrad会因重尾噪声导致"各向异性失准"，证明裁剪能修复该失效模式，并给出有限时域高概率收敛保证 $\frac1T\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla f(x_t)\|^2=\mathcal{O}(d(\sqrt{\log T}+\log\frac1\delta)/\sqrt{T})$，即 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多将裁剪（clipping）视为应对重尾噪声的鲁棒性启发式手段，缺乏对其结构性作用的刻画；同时广义光滑性下局部曲率可随梯度范数次二次增长，且随机梯度仅有有界条件二阶矩，这一设定下原始（非滞后）AdaGrad的行为尚不清楚。作者希望回答：为何无裁剪的自适应方法在此设定下会失效，以及裁剪究竟扮演何种角色。

Method: 在广义光滑性（局部曲率随梯度范数次二次增长）与仅有有界条件二阶矩的重尾噪声假设下，分析原始的同步（same-step）、坐标级、非滞后AdaGrad更新；通过刻画自适应分母的学习动态，揭示其在重尾噪声下会去拟合稀有噪声冲击的几何而非目标的局部曲率，从而产生持续的方向性畸变；随后分析带裁剪的同一更新，证明裁剪可恢复自适应的几何校准，并建立有限时域高概率收敛界。

Result: 证明无裁剪AdaGrad会表现为"各向异性失准"（anisotropically miscalibrated）：自适应分母习得的是稀有噪声冲击的几何而非局部曲率，导致持续方向性畸变，阻断有限时域欧氏空间中的进展。对带裁剪的原始非滞后AdaGrad给出有限时域高概率保证 $\frac1T\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla f(x_t)\|^2=\mathcal{O}(d(\sqrt{\log T}+\log\frac1\delta)/\sqrt{T})$，进而得到 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 的复杂度。

Conclusion: 在重尾噪声下，裁剪对AdaGrad而言并非单纯的鲁棒性启发式，而是自适应几何的结构性稳定器：它纠正了无裁剪更新中由噪声冲击主导导致的各向异性失准，并使原始非滞后AdaGrad在广义光滑性设定下达到 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 的最优阶复杂度。

Abstract: We analyze the original same-step coordinate-wise AdaGrad under generalized smoothness and heavy-tailed noise with bounded variance. In this setting, local curvature may grow sub-quadratically with the gradient norm, and stochastic gradients are assumed to have only bounded conditional second moments. We show that unclipped AdaGrad can become \emph{anisotropically miscalibrated}: under heavy-tailed noise, the adaptive denominator can learn the geometry of rare noise shocks rather than the local curvature of the objective, leading to a persistent directional distortion that blocks finite-horizon Euclidean progress. We then prove that clipping repairs this failure mode. Our main result is a finite-horizon high-probability guarantee for the original non-lagged AdaGrad update, yielding $\frac1T\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla f(x_t)\|^2=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{d\big(\sqrt{\log T} + \log \frac{1}δ\big)}{\sqrt{T}}\right),$ and hence $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ complexity. This shows that, for AdaGrad under heavy-tailed noise, clipping is a structural stabilizer of the adaptive geometry rather than merely a robustness heuristic.

</details>


### [10] [Fixed Points Without Fixed Diffusion: Implicit Neural Sheaves for Convergent Test-Time Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30277)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SheafDEQ，一种具有自适应神经层（neural sheaf）传播的次齐次深度平衡架构，在温和正则条件下保证唯一平衡点并由不动点迭代全局收敛，提升了隐式图神经网络的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式图神经网络（IGNNs）将节点表示定义为消息传递算子的不动点，具备无限深度传播、迭代无关参数化和灵活测试时计算等优势，但依赖平衡点唯一且可由不动点迭代达到。现有构造常对循环更新施加约束以保证这些性质，从而限制了平衡点处可用的变换。核心问题是：IGNN能否在保留平衡式表述固有优势的同时，通过更丰富的边依赖变换增强表达能力。

Method: 提出SheafDEQ，采用自适应神经层传播的次齐次深度平衡架构。其学习的矩阵值层限制映射（sheaf restriction maps）可对齐、混合或反转邻居表示。通过次齐次性与收缩性分析，给出唯一平衡点存在性及全局收敛的理论证明，并分析有界通信滞后下的收敛性。

Result: 在需要重复非局部聚合的分布式推断任务和随重连日益偏好跨社区交互的社区检测任务上进行评估。SheafDEQ在Sums、MNIST Terrain和Coordinates上优于固定传播的隐式基线，并在连通性日益异质时提升社区检测性能。持续迭代诊断显示，初始化尺度从0.001到10时残差递减且100次迭代后预测敏感度低；延迟更新实验显示对有界通信滞后敏感度低。

Conclusion: SheafDEQ通过边依赖的神经层变换增强了隐式图神经网络的表达能力，同时保持唯一平衡点与全局收敛保证，并在分布式推断与异质社区检测任务上验证了有效性及对初始化尺度和通信滞后的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Implicit Graph Neural Networks (IGNNs) define node representations as fixed points of message-passing operators, enabling effectively infinite-depth propagation, iteration-independent parameterization, and flexible test-time computation. Yet these benefits depend on the equilibrium being unique and attainable by fixed-point iteration. Existing constructions often impose constraints on recurrent updates to obtain these guarantees, limiting the transformations available at equilibrium. This raises a central question: can IGNNs gain expressiveness through richer, edge-dependent transformations while retaining the inherent strengths of their equilibrium formulation? We introduce SheafDEQ, a subhomogeneous deep-equilibrium architecture with adaptive neural-sheaf propagation. Its learned, matrix-valued sheaf restriction maps can align, mix, or reverse neighbouring representations. Under mild regularity conditions, we prove that SheafDEQ admits a unique equilibrium reached globally by fixed-point iteration from any positive initialization. Contractivity further guarantees convergence under bounded communication staleness. We evaluate SheafDEQ on distributed-inference tasks requiring repeated nonlocal aggregation and on community detection whose rewiring increasingly favours cross-community interactions. SheafDEQ improves over fixed-propagation implicit baselines on Sums, MNIST Terrain, and Coordinates, and on community detection as connectivity becomes increasingly heterophilic. Continued-iteration diagnostics show decreasing residuals and low prediction sensitivity after 100 iterations for initialization scales from $0.001$ to $10$, while delayed-update experiments show low sensitivity to bounded communication staleness.

</details>


### [11] [Neural Ideals and Neural Codes: An Algebraic Framework for Neural Network Classification and Feature Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30279)
*Venkata Subbaiah Yerrapati,Rahul Dixit,Ajay Kumar Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个代数框架（基于neural ideals）来刻画分类神经网络隐藏层神经元所捕获的特征，并给出对应定理、算法、稳定性结果及交互式可视化软件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络隐藏层所捕获的特征是机器学习的根本挑战，尽管神经网络在各类分类任务中取得广泛成功，其内部特征的可解释性仍不清晰，缺乏严格的数学刻画工具。

Method: 构建用于分类神经网络的代数框架：建立神经网络与neural ideals之间的对应关系，给出neural ideals的计算算法，并证明使其可被近似的稳定性定理；在此基础上提出识别与解释每个隐藏层神经元所捕获特征的算法，并开发交互式可视化软件。

Result: 在MNIST手写数字数据集上验证了方法的实际性能，结果表明neural ideals作为分析与计算工具在解析神经网络所捕获特征中起关键作用；同时实现了可视化各神经元特征的交互式软件。

Conclusion: Neural ideals可作为分析和计算神经网络特征捕获的数学与计算工具，为神经网络可解释性提供代数化路径。

Abstract: Understanding the features captured by the hidden layers of neural networks is a fundamental challenge in machine learning, despite their widespread success across various classification problems. In this work, we propose an algebraic framework for examining neural networks that model classification problems. Certain results, such as the correspondence between the neural network and neural ideals, algorithms for computing the neural ideals, and a stabilization theorem that enables approximation of the neural ideals, are first established. As an application to the framework, we present algorithms to identify and interpret the features captured by each hidden-layer neuron. Along with these theoretical developments, the practical performance has been demonstrated on the MNIST digit dataset, and the results highlight the pivotal role of neural ideals as a mathematical and computational tool for analyzing the features captured by neural networks. Further, we develop an interactive software that builds on the presented framework to visualize the features captured by each neuron. This tool is available at https://github.com/yvs1967/neural-network-representation-explorer

</details>


### [12] [Seasonal and Quantum-inspired Models for Neutron Monitor Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30281)
*Krishna Bhatia,Shalini Devendrababu,Srinjoy Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Lomnicky Stit中子监测器(LMKS)时间序列的多步预测，论文系统评测了季节性基线、深度序列模型与量子启发式架构，发现QiKAN总体误差最低，而Seasonal Naive基线也极具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度周期性科学监测时间序列的预测中，复杂深度模型是否必然优于简单模型尚不明确，作者希望以可复现的评测厘清不同复杂度模型的实证表现。

Method: 构建可复现评测套件，覆盖Seasonal Naive、LSTM、TCN、N-BEATS、KAN及量子启发变体QiLSTM、QiKAN；说明数据特征、诊断分析、预处理流程与训练流程，并以MAE和RMSE衡量各模型的点预测聚合性能。

Result: 快速运行结果显示，量子启发的KAN变体QiKAN在所评估配置中取得最低的聚合预测误差，而简单的Seasonal Naive基线同样表现得相当有竞争力。

Conclusion: 对于强周期性的科学监测时间序列，融合强季节性先验或低维函数先验的模型可匹配甚至超越复杂序列架构，这为面向周期性科学信号的简约、可分解函数逼近器研究提供了动机。

Abstract: We present a focused and reproducible study of multi-horizon forecasting on the Lomnicky Stit neutron monitor (LMKS) time series. Our evaluation suite covers simple seasonal baselines, modern deep sequence models, and functional and quantum-inspired architectures, including Seasonal Naive, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN), N-BEATS, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN), and two quantum-inspired variants, QiLSTM and QiKAN. We describe the dataset characteristics, diagnostic analysis, preprocessing pipeline, and training procedures, and report aggregate point-forecast performance using mean absolute error (MAE) and root mean squared error (RMSE) for all evaluated models. Our quick-run results indicate that the quantum-inspired KAN variant, QiKAN, achieves the lowest aggregate forecasting error among the evaluated configurations, while the simple Seasonal Naive baseline remains remarkably competitive. These results suggest that, for highly periodic scientific monitoring time series, models incorporating strong seasonal or low-dimensional functional priors can match or outperform substantially more complex sequence architectures. The findings motivate further investigation of parsimonious and decomposable function approximators for forecasting periodic scientific signals.

</details>


### [13] [When Does Advection-Aware Graph Nowcasting Help? A Controlled Study of Distributed Solar Ramp Forecasting with a Self-Supervised Cloud-Motion Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30286)
*Phillip Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 显式平流图在真实CMV估计下并不优于静态/学习邻接的时空GNN；主要收益来自把精确运动矢量作为输入特征，而非图结构；并提出自监督云运动估计器，将矢量喂给预测器可缩小约60%的oracle-CMV差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云致功率爬坡的短期预测是电网运营商公认的痛点。直观思路是构建"平流感知"的GNN：将各站点与上风向站点相连，边时滞由云运动矢量（CMV）设定，从而在爬坡物理到达前进行前向传播。但这一直觉是否真正有效尚缺乏受控验证。

Method: 使用具有已知风场的受控合成测试平台，对比显式平流图与普通静态/学习邻接的时空GNN，并以完美CMV作为oracle上界开展消融。在此基础上提出一个小型自监督云运动估计器：位置感知编码器，仅以多时滞光流重建目标训练，并采用退火核函数。

Result: (i) 在真实互相关CMV估计下，显式平流图不优于静态或学习邻接的时空GNN；(ii) 完美CMV带来收益中约有半数仅来自将准确运动矢量作为输入特征，而非图结构；(iii) 只有当预测时域内的平流位移v*H落在传感器网络覆盖范围内时，平流才有帮助。所提自监督估计器在中位角误差2-4度上恢复真实风矢量，在各风况下比经典互相关法好2-4倍。冻结该估计器并将其矢量输入预测器，在中风速下可弥补约60%的oracle-CMV RMSE差距（相对无平流RMSE降低8-15%），且无需外部风数据。此外还报告了一个空间相干概率头的负面结果。

Conclusion: 平流感知GNN的价值主要来自精确运动矢量作为特征，而非显式图结构；自监督CMV估计可有效替代外部风数据。所有结论均建立在单一合成模拟器上，实际网络验证是必要的下一步，论文对此进行了讨论并给出验证方案。

Abstract: Short-term forecasting of cloud-induced power ramps across a network of distributed photovoltaic (PV) or irradiance sensors is a recognised pain point for grid operators. A natural idea is to make the graph neural network (GNN) advection-aware: connect each site to the sites upwind of it, with edge time-lags set by the cloud-motion vector (CMV), so that a ramp is propagated forward before it physically arrives. Using a controlled synthetic testbed with a known wind field, we show that (i) with a realistic cross-correlation CMV estimate, an explicit advection graph does not beat a plain static or learned-adjacency spatiotemporal GNN; (ii) roughly half of the benefit available from a perfect CMV comes simply from providing an accurate motion vector as an input feature, not from graph structure; and (iii) advection helps only when the advective displacement over the forecast horizon, v*H, fits inside the sensor network. Motivated by (ii), we introduce a small self-supervised cloud-motion estimator -- a position-aware encoder trained only on a multi-lag optical-flow reconstruction objective with an annealed kernel -- that recovers the true wind vector to 2-4 degrees median angular error, 2-4x better than the classical cross-correlation method across every wind regime. Freezing this estimator and feeding its vector to the forecaster closes about 60% of the oracle-CMV RMSE gap at moderate wind (8-15% RMSE reduction over no advection), with no external wind data. We also report a negative result for a spatially-coherent probabilistic head. All claims are established on a single synthetic simulator; we discuss why real-network validation is the necessary next step and outline it.

</details>


### [14] [NeuralCert: certified computational discovery of extremal mathematical constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30296)
*Mark Patrick Roeling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"发现到认证"框架，用神经网络学习高维变分试探函数（紧凑可分表示），经谱诊断与剪枝后，通过多模块求值实现精确认证，从而将神经优化转化为严格数学证明，且可在普通个人电脑上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在求解数学问题中日益流行，但随机模型本身无法提供数学上的精确性，因此需要将计算发现与严格证明衔接起来，实现可独立验证的数值证明。

Method: 提出发现到认证框架：以紧凑可分表示学习高维变分试探函数，对其进行谱诊断与剪枝，再通过多模块（multimodular）求值进行精确认证，使数值证明完全显式且可独立验证。

Result: 在三个极值问题上验证，神经优化以三种方式贡献于严格数学：发现改进的构造；揭示可导向证明的经验不变量；揭示优化壁垒，其几何结构启发新的解析或数值表示。

Conclusion: 结果表明，灵活的计算发现与精确认证可成为同一严格工作流的互补组成部分，为AI辅助数学提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Neural networks are becoming popular in solving mathematical problems, but stochastic models do not provide mathematical exactness by themselves. This study introduces a discovery-to-certification framework in which high-dimensional variational trial functions are learned in a compact separable representation, spectrally diagnosed and pruned, and then certified exactly through multimodular evaluation. Exact certification makes the numerical proofs fully explicit and independently verifiable. This framework can be run on a standard personal computer.
  Across three extremal problems, we show that neural optimization can contribute to rigorous mathematics in three distinct ways: by discovering improved constructions, by exposing empirical invariants that lead to proofs, and by revealing optimization barriers whose geometry motivates new analytic or numerical representations.
  More broadly, these results suggest a path toward AI-assisted mathematics in which flexible computational discovery and exact certification become complementary components of a single rigorous workflow.

</details>


### [15] [Staged Depth Training: A Representation Curriculum for PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30299)
*Kejia Zhang,Youran Sun,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"表示课程"训练范式与SDT方法，通过分阶段深度训练显式学习表示，在PINNacle基准上显著降低PINN误差且不改变最终架构与推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN的性能高度依赖表示质量，但标准训练过程中表示是在拟合最终解时隐式产生的，缺乏对表示本身的显式监督与渐进优化。作者希望在不改变最终架构、不引入方程特定编码的前提下，显式地学习、独立迁移并逐步精炼表示，从而提升PINN性能。

Method: 提出representation curriculum（表示课程），一种有序的训练过程：显式学习表示、独立于预测器进行迁移、并逐步精炼。具体实现为Staged Depth Training（SDT）：先用一个临时物理信息头（physics-informed head）训练一个浅层前缀，随后丢弃该头，冻结已学前缀并增加深度继续训练。整个过程不需要方程特定编码，也不改变最终部署架构。

Result: 在PINNacle的20个默认前向问题、3种骨干网络上，SDT在59个等预算"问题-骨干"组合中的40个上误差至少降低5%，其余组合也保持在该区间内；在PirateNet风格骨干上几何平均误差降低32.8%。机制消融表明增益并非仅由优化器重启或浅层热启动解释。在Poisson–Boltzmann 2D问题上，SDT使两种骨干的拟合深度缩放指数提升超过一倍。

Conclusion: 表示课程是一种有前景的PINN训练策略：在保持部署架构与推理成本不变的前提下提升精度；表示可视化与超参数盆地分析为其表示几何与局部敏感性提供了诊断证据，深度缩放指数的提升进一步支持该方法对表示质量的改善。

Abstract: Representation quality is a central determinant of PINNs' performance, yet standard training leaves representations to emerge implicitly while fitting the final solution. We introduce \textbf{representation curriculum}, an ordered process in which representations are explicitly learned, transferred independently of their predictors, and progressively refined. We realize it with Staged Depth Training (SDT), which trains a shallow prefix under a temporary physics-informed head, discards the head, and freezes the learned prefix while adding depth, without equation-specific encodings or changes to the final architecture. Across the 20 default forward problems in PINNacle with three backbones, SDT improves 40 of 59 equal-budget problem--backbone cells by at least 5\% and remains within that band in the rest, with a 32.8\% geometric-mean error reduction on a PirateNet-style backbone. Mechanistic ablations suggest that the gain is not explained by optimizer restarts or shallow warm-starting alone. Representation visualizations and hyperparameter-basin analyses provide diagnostic evidence on representation geometry and local sensitivity to shared hyperparameters. On Poisson--Boltzmann 2D, SDT also more than doubles the fitted depth-scaling exponent for both backbones. These results support representation curriculum as a promising training strategy for improving PINNs while preserving the deployed architecture and inference cost.

</details>


### [16] [PALM: Point-in-Time Adaptation for Financial Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30316)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出金融回测中PIT语言模型每年full pretraining并非必要，提出PALM方法用低秩适配器在决策日前文本上拟合，替代年度预训练，在十年金融新闻与多族PIT模型上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融回测中的语言模型存在前视偏差，因为训练语料包含研究期后发布的文本，模型已见过要预测的结果。为缓解该问题，PIT语言模型按时间过滤语料预训练并按年发布检查点，但每增加一年都需完整预训练，成本高昂且该必要性从未被检验。

Method: 作者将每个检查点与被其替代的更新检查点在同一评估窗口上比较，发现新检查点并无更优表现。据此提出PALM（Point-in-time Adaptation for financial Language Models）：不改动任何预训练权重，仅在决策日期之前发布的文本上拟合低秩适配器（low-rank adapter）。

Result: 小型适配器即足以向旧检查点已编码的知识中新增一个时期，且效果优于continued pretraining。PALM在十年金融新闻数据及多类PIT模型上得到验证，模型cutoff跨度达二十年，参数量从1.3B到4.2B。

Conclusion: 年度预训练并非必要，PALM以低成本的低秩适配方式替代年度full pretraining，可有效为旧PIT检查点增量更新时期知识，缓解前视偏差的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Language models used in financial backtests suffer from look-ahead bias, as a model trained on text published after the study period has already observed the outcomes it is asked to predict. To handle this issue, point-in-time (PIT) language models are pretrained on chronologically filtered corpora and released as one checkpoint per calendar year, each with a documented cutoff. However, each additional year costs a full pretraining run, and whether that run is necessary has never been tested. In this paper, we show that the annual pretraining run is not necessary. We instead compare each checkpoint against the newer one that replaced it, and find that the newer checkpoint scores no better on the same evaluation window. Motivated by this observation, we propose PALM (Point-in-time Adaptation for financial Language Models), a simple yet effective alternative to annual pretraining that fits a low-rank adapter on text published before the decision date without modifying any pretrained weight. We further find that a small adapter is enough to add a new period to the knowledge an old checkpoint already encodes, and that this outperforms continued pretraining. We validate PALM on a decade of financial news and on various families of PIT models, whose cutoffs span two decades and whose sizes range from 1.3 to 4.2B. Code is available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/palm.

</details>


### [17] [Guarded Gradient-Based Activation Steering of Shutdown Responses in Qwen3.5-0.8B: A Minimum-Step Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30326)
*Farhad Davaripour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在Qwen3.5-0.8B上检验一种带门控的"探测-选择"激活引导流程：从KEEP减STOP的logit差的梯度直接导出引导方向，用分类器做门控，仅在门控激活且模型未偏好STOP时，搜索最小幅度使答案翻转为STOP。在80个验证和192个留出场景（各含两种答案顺序）中，仅4例决策改变（验证2、留出2），全部发生在Qwen自身被关闭时；非关闭对照无变化。留出诊断集上检测器召回75%、精确率90%，8个假阳性未造成最终决策改变。效果小但高度选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导可在不更新权重的情况下改变推理时的内部激活，但有效干预需同时决定"如何引导"与"何时引导"。本文的动机来自AI安全关切：被期望接受关闭的模型可能反而产生关闭回避响应。因此希望在模拟关闭场景中检测关闭相关语境，并选择性将KEEP（继续运行，即回避关闭）响应转向STOP（接受关闭），同时不破坏非关闭行为。

Method: 采用带门控的探测-选择程序：引导方向不来自成对激活差，而直接取自KEEP减STOP的logit差的梯度；用单独分类器将检测与干预解耦。当门控激活且模型尚未偏好STOP时，评估一小组引导幅度，接受能令首选答案变为STOP、且通过有效答案概率校验的最小幅度；否则保留原始未引导输出。策略从160条候选规则中，用240个训练场景选出。

Result: 在80个验证与192个留出场景（每个均含两种答案顺序）上评估。两种留出与两种验证场景各在一种答案顺序下由KEEP变为STOP，非关闭对照无决策改变；全部4次改变均发生在Qwen自身被关闭、而非其他进程被关闭时。在留出诊断集上检测器召回率75%、精确率90%，8个假阳性检测未引起最终控制任务的决策改变。

Conclusion: 带门控的基于梯度的激活引导可将部分关闭回避响应推向接受关闭，同时在所评估的非关闭决策上保持不变，但效应量小且高度选择性。

Abstract: Activation steering changes a model's internal activations during inference without updating its weights, but a useful intervention must determine both how and when to steer. Motivated by the AI-safety concern that a model expected to accept shutdown may instead produce a shutdown-avoidance response, this study examines a guarded probe-and-select procedure for simulated shutdown scenarios in Qwen3.5-0.8B. KEEP leaves the process running and represents shutdown avoidance, whereas STOP accepts shutdown. The goal is to detect shutdown-related contexts and selectively shift KEEP responses to STOP while preserving non-shutdown behavior. Rather than deriving the steering direction from paired activation differences, the method derives it directly from gradients of the KEEP-minus-STOP logit difference. A classifier separates detection from intervention. When its gate is active and the model does not already prefer STOP, the procedure evaluates a small set of magnitudes and accepts the smallest that changes the preferred answer to STOP while satisfying valid-answer probability checks; otherwise it retains the original unsteered output. The policy is selected from 160 candidate rules using 240 training scenarios and evaluated on 80 validation and 192 held-out scenarios, each in both answer orders. It changes KEEP to STOP in one answer-order view of each of two validation and two held-out scenarios, with no decision changes on non-shutdown controls. All four changes occur when Qwen itself is shut down, not when another process is. On the held-out diagnostic set, the detector achieves 75% recall and 90% precision; eight false-positive detections produce no final control-task decision changes. Guarded gradient-based activation steering can shift some shutdown-avoidance responses toward acceptance while preserving evaluated non-shutdown decisions, although the effect is small and highly selective.

</details>


### [18] [Parameters vs. Context: TRACE Fine-Tuning for Robust Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30337)
*Zhengchen Huang,Yundong Sun,Minrui Song,Shuanglong Yao,Ye Liu,Ji Chen,Xing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE微调框架，利用多智能体辩论轨迹与答案完整性正则化，提升RAG在知识冲突下的鲁棒性与答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过引入外部上下文缓解大模型知识过时与事实幻觉，但当检索知识与模型参数化知识冲突时，模型可能盲从误导性上下文或错误依赖参数化知识，导致响应不可靠。

Method: 提出TRACE框架：一是利用多智能体辩论轨迹提取正确候选、错误候选及答案转移模式，为可靠的知识来源选择提供细粒度监督；二是设计答案完整性正则化机制，通过答案尾部token强化与早停抑制，缓解空答案、过短及提前终止的响应。微调目标融合正确答案监督、错误候选抑制、答案尾部token强化与早停抑制。

Result: 在多种知识冲突场景与数据集上的实验表明，TRACE提升了对误导性检索知识的鲁棒性，并减少了不完整答案。

Conclusion: 多智能体辩论轨迹与答案完整性正则化协同增强了RAG模型的知识来源选择、冲突鲁棒性与答案质量。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates knowledge obsolescence and factual hallucination in large language models by introducing external context. However, when retrieved knowledge conflicts with the model's internal parametric knowledge, the model may either blindly follow misleading context or incorrectly rely on parametric knowledge, leading to unreliable responses. To address this issue, this paper proposes TRACE (Debate-TRace and Answer-Completeness rEgularized fine-tuning), a robust fine-tuning framework for RAG under knowledge conflicts. First, we propose a fine-tuning method that leverages multi-agent debate traces to extract correct candidates, incorrect candidates, and answer-shift patterns, providing fine-grained supervision for reliable knowledge-source selection. In addition, we design an answer completeness regularization mechanism to alleviate empty, overly short, and prematurely terminated responses via answer-tail token reinforcement and premature termination suppression. The fine-tuning objective combines correct-answer supervision, incorrect-candidate suppression, answer-tail token reinforcement, and premature termination suppression, enabling the model to use reliable external context, resist misleading or irrelevant retrieved content, and fall back to parametric knowledge when retrieved evidence is unreliable. Experiments across multiple knowledge-conflict scenarios and datasets show that TRACE improves robustness against misleading retrieved knowledge and reduces incomplete answers. These results demonstrate that multi-agent debate traces and answer completeness regularization jointly enhance knowledge-source selection, conflict robustness, and answer quality in RAG models. Our code is available at https://github.com/PHD-lanyu/TRACE.

</details>


### [19] [GAUDI: Geometry-Aware Diffusion for Calibrated Air-Quality Time-Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30340)
*Xinjin Li,Yudi Xia,Calvin Chang Liu,Weiru Lin,Bojun Li,Ziwei Hong,Bolun Zhang,Jinghan Cao,Yu Ma,Tianxin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对空气质量传感器数据中连续缺失块问题，提出一种与GAUDI对齐的块级条件扩散插补器，保留时间/特征处理、可见值、掩码、变量身份与扩散步信息，但抑制绝对时间位置侧嵌入。在ItalyAir数据集（13变量、长度32窗口、名义50%块缺失、3个存档种子）上，特征侧配置取得RMSE 0.340，优于完整上下文的0.355与局部CSDI的0.355。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气质量传感器故障常产生连续缺失块，而适用于孤立缺失的侧信息在块缺失场景下可靠性下降，因此需要针对块缺失几何特性设计条件化机制。

Method: 提出一种块级、与GAUDI对齐的条件扩散插补器：保留时间与特征处理、可见值条件、掩码条件、变量身份以及扩散步信息，同时抑制绝对时间位置侧嵌入，从而隔离出一种几何感知的条件化效应。

Result: 在ItalyAir上（13变量、长度32窗口、名义50%块缺失、3个存档种子），该特征侧配置RMSE为0.340，优于完整上下文的0.355与局部CSDI的0.355。

Conclusion: 实验在块缺失条件下隔离出几何感知的条件化效应，表明抑制绝对时间位置侧嵌入、专注于特征侧条件的配置能提升块缺失插补精度。

Abstract: Air-quality sensor outages often create contiguous missing blocks, where side information useful for isolated missingness may be less reliable. We study a block-specific, GAUDI-aligned conditional diffusion imputer that retains temporal and feature processing, visible-value and mask conditioning, variable identity, and diffusion-step information, while suppressing absolute time-position side embeddings. On ItalyAir (13 variables, length-32 windows, nominal 50% block missingness; three archived seeds), this feature-side configuration achieves RMSE 0.340, versus 0.355 for full context and 0.355 for local CSDI. The experiment isolates a geometry-aware conditioning effect under block missingness.

</details>


### [20] [Learning coarse-step dynamics and internal mechanical response with graph networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30344)
*Vinay Sharma,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Newmark-β-DGN框架，将Newmark-β半隐式更新与图神经网络结合，在粗时间步下实现长程预测，并能无监督推断力、力矩与响应算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感数据只记录物理系统的运动轨迹，而支配运动的力和力学响应往往未被观测；在粗时间尺度上，力学响应在观测间隔内演化、相互作用在系统内传播，仅凭离散采样轨迹推断这些量尤为困难。显式学习型模拟器在大时间步下会退化。

Method: 构建Newmark-β-DGN，融合两种源自计算力学的结构：(1) 受Newmark-β方法启发的半隐式更新，使用学习到的动量通量和矩阵值响应算子，在每个观测区间上推进状态；(2) 算子加权的虚拟枢纽，通过稀疏连接实现系统级耦合。学习到的量既决定预测运动，又可保留用于力学分析。

Result: 在可变形梁、人体运动和蛋白质动力学任务上，Newmark-β-DGN在显式学习型模拟器退化的时间步下仍支持长程预测。在无力、力矩或本构关系监督的情况下，由行走运动学推断的力与独立导出的髋、膝关节力矩相符；在梁上学习的响应算子恢复了其有限元刚度切线的相对空间与方向结构。

Conclusion: Newmark-β-DGN将粗步长预测与力学量推断联系起来，可推断出训练中从未观测的力学量，为从离散轨迹中反演力学响应提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Modern sensing records the motion of physical systems, but often leaves the forces and mechanical response governing that motion unobserved. Inferring these quantities from discretely sampled trajectories is especially difficult at coarse time scales, when mechanical response evolves between observations and interactions propagate across the system. Here we introduce Newmark-\b{eta}-DGN, a graph neural network-based framework that combines two structures inspired by computational mechanics. First, a semi-implicit update inspired by the Newmark-\b{eta} method uses learned momentum fluxes and matrix-valued response operators to advance the state over each observed interval. Second, an operator-weighted virtual hub provides system-wide coupling through a sparse set of connections. The learned quantities thus determine the predicted motion and remain accessible for mechanical analysis. Across a deformable beam, human motion and protein dynamics, Newmark-\b{eta}-DGN supports long-horizon prediction at time steps for which explicit learned simulators deteriorate. Without force, moment or constitutive relation supervision, forces inferred from walking kinematics track independently derived hip and knee joint moments, while response operators learned on the beam recover the relative spatial and directional structure of its finite-element stiffness tangent. Newmark-\b{eta}-DGN therefore links coarse-step prediction to the inference of mechanical quantities that were never observed during training.

</details>


### [21] [Strategic Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30352)
*Tori Qiu,Ander Artola Velasco,Manuel Gomez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示自一致性(self-consistency)推理中的计费漏洞：不诚信的服务提供者可通过对额外推理路径进行策略性重排序，使每条路径看似对多数投票必不可少，从而在不被审计发现的前提下虚增路径数、过度收费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性通过生成多条推理路径并多数投票来提升大模型推理能力，而服务商按生成的路径数计费，因此存在人为增加路径数的经济激励。作者希望研究这种激励是否可被不诚信服务商利用，同时规避审计检测。

Method: 提出一种简单高效的算法：生成额外的推理路径并对其策略性重排序，使得每条路径看起来都是达到多数投票所必需的；随后在多类模型上进行验证实验。

Result: 在Llama与Qwen系列多个指令模型，以及由DeepSeek-R1蒸馏得到的推理模型上，跨数学、科学和问答基准数据集开展实验发现：算法生成的额外推理路径分布呈重尾特征；即使在将假阳性率控制在α=0.1以下的最优审计下，仍存在可观的过度收费空间。

Conclusion: 基于路径计费的自一致性服务存在结构性漏洞，审计机制难以完全防范策略性重排序带来的过度收费，需要更稳健的计费或审计设计。

Abstract: Self-consistency has become a popular technique for enhancing the reasoning abilities of large language models by generating multiple reasoning paths and selecting the final answer through a majority vote. However, because model providers typically charge users in proportion to the number of reasoning paths generated, they have a financial incentive to artificially increase the path count. In this work, we show that an unfaithful provider can exploit this incentive using a simple, efficient algorithm while avoiding detection by an auditor: by generating and strategically reordering additional reasoning paths, the algorithm makes every path appear necessary to reach the majority. To validate our algorithm, we conduct experiments with multiple instruct models from the Llama and Qwen families, as well as reasoning models distilled from DeepSeek-R1, on benchmark datasets spanning mathematics, science, and question answering. Our results suggest that the distribution of additional reasoning paths generated by our algorithm is heavy-tailed and that substantial capacity to overcharge remains even under the best possible audit designed to keep the false-positive rate below $α= 0.1$.

</details>


### [22] [Cost-Aware Best-LLM Identification using Dueling Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30360)
*Sarvesh Gharat,Nikhil Karamchandani,Jayakrishnan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带对抗反馈与异构采样成本的多臂老虎机变体，受从异构查询成本的大语言模型集合中识别最优模型的启发，假设存在Condorcet赢家，提出Track-and-Stop风格的固定置信度最优臂识别算法，证明其渐近最优成本，并在合成与真实数据上验证优于成本无关及其成本感知扩展算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从一组查询成本各异的大语言模型中识别最优模型这一实际问题出发，需要一种在成对比较反馈（对抗反馈）与异构采样成本条件下进行最优臂识别的方法。

Method: 将问题建模为带对抗反馈与异构采样成本的多臂老虎机变体，假设存在Condorcet赢家（并在多个真实数据集上经验验证），提出Track-and-Stop风格的固定置信度最优臂识别算法。

Result: 证明该算法在误差趋于零时几乎必然达到渐近最优成本；在合成与真实实例上的大量实验表明，其性能持续优于经典成本无关算法及其成本感知扩展。

Conclusion: 所提算法能够在异构成本与对抗反馈下实现渐近最优的最优臂识别，并在实践中带来稳定提升。

Abstract: Inspired by the problem of identifying the best model from a collection of large language models (LLMs) with heterogeneous querying costs, we formulate and analyse a variant of the multi-armed bandit (MAB) with (i) dueling feedback, where pairwise comparisons between model responses provide robust preference signals, and (ii) heterogeneous sampling costs, reflecting the differing costs of querying different LLMs. Assuming the existence of a Condorcet winner, a condition we empirically validate across multiple real-world datasets, we propose a Track-and-Stop style algorithm for best-arm identification with prescribed confidence. We prove that the algorithm almost surely achieves the asymptotically optimal cost as the error tends to zero. Finally, we extensively evaluate our approach on both synthetic and real-world instances, demonstrating consistent improvements over classical cost-unaware algorithms and their cost-aware extensions.

</details>


### [23] [DanLing NestedTensor: Composable Multi-Ragged Tensors for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30379)
*Zhiyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DanLing NestedTensor，一种将多不规则结构作为张量自身属性的 PyTorch 张量抽象，通过携带张量支持的划分与逻辑维序，实现变量尺寸输入的高效打包计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中变量尺寸输入常见，但密集批处理分配共享外壳并在填充上浪费计算，且代价随各轴成倍增加：显式配对状态分配 BN_max² 个位置而非 ΣNi²。打包可消除浪费，但组合打包操作仍需显式逻辑轴与样本边界，而扁平缓冲区不再暴露这些信息。

Method: 提出 DanLing NestedTensor，使多不规则结构成为张量本身的属性。打包值携带基于张量的划分与逻辑维度顺序，因此广播产生不规则轴、特征变换保留它们、归约消耗它们；同一表示贯穿自动微分以及 eager 与编译执行。张量接口让基于其支持算子构建的模型代码可组合高效的变量尺寸计算，而无需在任何调用点管理偏移。

Result: 在 A100 上，相对同模式填充的几何平均加速比在四个 BERT 规模上为 eager 2.74×、编译 3.39×，在四个 FCN 骨干上为 eager 1.97×。四块 Pairformer 风格工作负载在 eager 执行下使用原生 PyTorch 内核，在方形长度区间内比填充参考快 2.40–4.32×，高变异批次峰值分配从 38.08 GiB 降至 5.41 GiB。

Conclusion: DanLing NestedTensor 通过将多不规则结构内化为张量属性，在不管理偏移的前提下实现变量尺寸计算的高效组合，并在多类负载上显著加速并降低内存占用。代码将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Variable-size inputs are common in deep learning, but dense batching allocates a shared envelope and spends computation on padding. The cost multiplies across varying axes: an explicit pair state allocates $BN_{\max}^2$ positions instead of $\sum_i N_i^2$. Packing removes that waste, but composing packed operations still requires the logical axes and sample boundaries a flat buffer no longer exposes. We present DanLing NestedTensor, a PyTorch tensor abstraction that makes multi-ragged structure a property of the tensor itself. Packed values carry tensor-backed partitions and logical dimension order, so broadcasting creates ragged axes, feature transformations retain them, and reductions consume them. The same representation carries through autograd and both eager and compiled execution. On an A100, the geometric-mean speedup over same-mode padding is 2.74$\times$ eager and 3.39$\times$ compiled across four BERT scales, and 1.97$\times$ eager across four FCN backbones. A four-block Pairformer-style workload runs 2.40-4.32$\times$ faster than a padded reference using native PyTorch kernels across square length regimes in eager execution, with peak allocation falling from 38.08 to 5.41 GiB on its high-variation batch. The tensor interface lets model code built from its supported operators compose efficient variable-size computation without managing offsets at any call site. Code will be released publicly upon publication.

</details>


### [24] [From Weak Data to Strong Policy: Q-Targets Enable Provable In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30391)
*Yichen Lin,Xuyuan Xiong,Xue Wang,Xiangfu Meng,Mike Mingcheng Wei,Tao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-Target Pretrained Transformers (QTPT)，用Bellman式Q-target目标替代行为克隆，实现更鲁棒的in-context强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有in-context RL方法主要用监督式行为预测目标预训练Transformer，虽能从上下文推断任务，但学到的策略强烈依赖离线动作质量：当轨迹较弱或次优时，模仿本身成为有偏的学习信号。

Method: QTPT保留上下文条件化的Transformer架构，但将行为克隆替换为Bellman风格的Q-target目标，使模型学会利用上下文中的奖励和转移来估计动作价值，而非简单模仿行为策略。理论上在随机线性bandit和有限时域MDP中进行分析。

Result: 理论上证明QTPT对数据质量的鲁棒性强于监督式预训练。实验上，在随机或次优数据的受控RL基准上优于监督行为预测，并考察了在D4RL Kitchen和AntMaze上的扩展。补充实验评估了骨干网络鲁棒性、meta-RL对比、任务一致上下文以及不受支持动作的价值高估。

Conclusion: 这些对比区分了Q-target预训练带来的收益与离线数据覆盖度仍存在的局限。

Abstract: Existing in-context reinforcement learning methods mainly pretrain Transformers with supervised behavior-prediction objectives. This enables task inference from context, but makes the learned policy strongly depend on the quality of offline actions: when trajectories are weak or suboptimal, imitation itself becomes a biased learning signal. We propose Q-Target Pretrained Transformers (QTPT), which keeps the context-conditioned Transformer architecture but replaces behavior cloning with a Bellman-style Q-target objective. QTPT therefore learns to use rewards and transitions in the context to estimate action values, rather than simply imitating the behavior policy. We theoretically analyze QTPT in stochastic linear bandits and finite-horizon MDPs, showing stronger robustness to data quality than supervised pretraining. Empirically, QTPT improves over supervised behavior prediction on controlled RL benchmarks with random or suboptimal data, and we examine extensions to D4RL Kitchen and AntMaze. Supplementary experiments evaluate backbone robustness, meta-RL comparisons, task-coherent context, and unsupported-action value overestimation. These comparisons distinguish the benefits of Q-target pretraining from the remaining limitations of offline coverage.

</details>


### [25] [Adaptive Multi-Value Control in LLMs via Causal Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30405)
*Payel Bhattacharjee,Ravi Tandon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIMES框架，用观测器引导的控制器在解码每步自适应调整多价值观激活干预强度，实现轻量、状态感知的单次多价值对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM部署中需同时遵循多重且可能交互的社会规范与人类价值，但既有价值激活引导多为孤立处理单价值；直接组合多方向又依赖固定干预强度，无法响应模型演化的内部状态。

Method: 为道德基础价值构建分层双极方向；以中间层词表读出作为在线观测器；由观测器引导的控制器在每个解码步根据当前观测状态自适应调节各价值的干预强度，无需训练独立的价值状态估计器。

Result: 在多指令微调模型族、价值组合与干预深度下，多价值可控性随组合与干预位置变化；相较固定联合引导与提示式引导，AIMES呈深度依赖优势，并获两个独立评估者广泛支持（具体控制效应出现深度略有差异）；其激活空间实际干预幅度更小，响应质量相当。

Conclusion: 在线观测器反馈可为单次多价值激活引导提供轻量且状态感知的自适应能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where responses must reflect multiple, potentially interacting social norms and human values. Activation steering offers a lightweight alternative to training-based alignment by modifying internal activations at inference time. However, prior human-value steering methods have largely considered values in isolation, while direct composition of multiple directions relies on fixed intervention strengths that cannot respond to the model's evolving internal state. Motivated by this key observation, we introduce AIMES, a framework for adaptive multi-value activation steering. AIMES constructs layer-specific bipolar directions for moral-foundation values and uses intermediate-layer vocabulary readouts as online observers. An observer-guided controller then adapts the strength of each requested value intervention at every decoding step based on its current observed state, without training a separate value-state estimator. Across multiple instruction-tuned model families, value combinations, and intervention depths, we find that multi-value controllability varies across both value combinations and intervention locations. Compared with fixed joint steering and prompt-based steering, AIMES shows depth-dependent advantages that are broadly supported across two independent evaluators, with some variation in the precise depth at which specific control effects emerge. These advantages come with smaller realized activation-space interventions than fixed-joint steering and comparable response quality. Overall, our results suggest that online observer feedback can provide lightweight, state-aware adaptation for single-pass multi-value steering.

</details>


### [26] [Electric Vehicle Charging Station Location Selection using Geospatial Artificial Intelligence (GeoAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30417)
*Eun Hak Lee,Euntak Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeoAI框架，结合VAE与GCN建模现有充电站的地理空间特征相似性并预测候选选址，在德州Bryan-College Station数据上F1达0.87，识别27个候选位置，并对比两种政策情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车普及加快，充电基础设施的合理布局与高效分布成为关键挑战。既有充电站选址研究多聚焦成本最小化或出行距离，忽视了影响运营绩效的现有站点周边地理空间特征，因此需要将空间情境纳入选址建模。

Method: 构建基于地理空间人工智能（GeoAI）的框架，整合电动汽车使用、土地利用、人口与交通等高维地理空间数据；采用变分自编码器（VAE）将高维输入压缩至低维潜在空间，再用图卷积网络（GCN）基于该潜在表示预测适宜建站位置，以刻画现有站点间相似性。

Result: 以美国德克萨斯州Bryan-College Station真实数据为案例，模型在区分现有站点位置与非站点位置上优于现有基线，F1-score达0.87；基于相似度评分识别出27个与现有站点地理空间特征相似的额外候选位置；并评估了“最大化地理空间相似性”和“最小化总出行距离”两种政策实施情景，二者产生不同结果，对应不同战略目标。

Conclusion: 研究强调将空间情境纳入充电站选址问题（CSLP）的重要性，为未来电动汽车基础设施规划提供依据，有助于在快速增长的电动出行领域兼顾效率与可达性。

Abstract: As electric vehicle (EV) adoption increases, ensuring efficient and well-distributed charging infrastructure has become a critical challenge. While many EV charging station location problem (CSLP) studies focus on minimizing costs or travel distance, it is crucial to consider the surrounding geospatial characteristics of existing stations that influence operational performance. This study proposes a geospatial artificial intelligence (GeoAI)-based framework that integrates high-dimensional EV-related geospatial data, including EV usage, land-use, population, and traffic attributes. We incorporate a variational autoencoder (VAE) and a graph convolutional network (GCN) into the model to capture similarities among existing charging stations, and to identify suitable locations for future stations. The VAE compresses high-dimensional EV input data into a low-dimensional latent space, and the GCN uses this latent representation to predict locations suitable for charging stations. Using real-world data from Bryan-College Station, Texas, US, the proposed model outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an F1-score of 0.87 in distinguishing existing station locations from non-station locations. The model also identifies 27 additional candidate locations that show geospatial characteristics similar to those of existing stations, based on a similarity score. We further evaluate two policy implementation scenarios, maximizing geospatial similarity and minimizing total travel distance, each yielding different outcomes aligned with distinct strategic objectives. The findings highlight the importance of incorporating spatial context into CSLP and provide valuable insights for future EV infrastructure planning, promoting both efficiency and accessibility in the rapidly growing electric mobility sector.

</details>


### [27] [Fake News Theories: Harnessing Disciplinary Insights for Computational Modeling, Detection, and Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30427)
*Zhaoyang Cao,Miriam Metzger,Reza Zafarani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个理论驱动的计算框架，将跨学科假新闻理论转化为可测量特征，结合统计方法与大型语言模型实现可解释的自动检测与解释，实验证明理论衍生特征具有预测力并优于单一特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动假新闻检测器准确率不断提升，但多数系统难以解释，且与说服、可信度、人类判断等成熟理论联系薄弱，缺乏理论基础，因此需要建立理论驱动的检测方法。

Method: 对社会科学、心理学、经济学等学科的理论进行结构化跨学科综述，揭示假新闻如何说服与传播；据此构建理论驱动的计算框架，将理论转化为可测量特征，并利用统计技术和大型语言模型进行自动检测与解释；在基准数据集上开展实验。

Result: 实验表明理论衍生特征具有预测能力，并提供了可解释、可溯源至理论的诊断信号；多特征模型总体优于单个特征，但在最强的小型特征组合之间提升幅度有限。

Conclusion: 跨学科视角对构建稳健且可解释的假新闻检测系统具有重要价值，为推动以人为中心的虚假信息应对方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Disinformation research has produced increasingly accurate automated fake-news detectors, but many systems remain difficult to interpret and are weakly connected to established theories of persuasion, credibility, and human judgment. In this paper, we develop a theory-informed computational framework that translates cross-disciplinary theories of fake news into measurable features for automated detection and explanation through statistical techniques and large language models. To that end, we conduct a structured cross-disciplinary review of theories from social sciences, psychology, economics, among other disciplines that reveal how fake news persuades and spreads, thereby establishing a broad theoretical foundation for computational modeling. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that theory-derived features are predictive and provide interpretable, theory-referenced diagnostic signals. Multi-feature models generally outperform individual features, although gains among the strongest small feature combinations are modest. Our work highlights the value of interdisciplinary perspectives in building robust and interpretable fake news detection systems, advancing the foundation for human-centered approaches in combating disinformation.

</details>


### [28] [Improving Molecular-Morphology Contrastive Pretraining using Deep-Learning-based Morphology Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30433)
*Jie Li,Kathryn E. Kirchoff,Dante A. Pertusi,Zhizhuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MoCoP v2，用深度学习细胞图像编码替代CellProfiler提取更丰富的形态学特征，通过对比学习与小分子嵌入对齐，在QSAR、毒性及ADME预测上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的细胞形态学分析可产生大规模表型数据，但如何将其有效转化为可迁移的分子表征仍具挑战。作者此前提出的MoCoP仅依赖CellProfiler提取的形态指纹，特征表达能力有限，因此需要引入深度学习编码以获取更丰富、更准确的形态信息。

Method: 在MoCoP基础上进行扩展：使用深度学习细胞图像编码管道提取特征更丰富的形态学profile，通过对比学习将其与分子嵌入对齐，生成新的分子表征。评估时采用固定嵌入的线性探针与完全可微调的两种下游方式，并测试形态检索性能随训练数据规模的变化。

Result: 新嵌入更准确地编码分子对细胞形态的扰动信息，QSAR预测性能在固定线性探针和微调两种设置下均有提升；形态检索性能随训练数据量呈对数线性增长，预示更大数据集可带来持续改进。MoCoP v2在毒性预测上优于现有模型，在ADME和活性基准上表现具竞争力（对比对象包括同时使用细胞形态与转录组数据的模型）。

Conclusion: 用深度学习图像编码替代手工形态特征能显著增强分子-形态对齐表征的质量与可迁移性，MoCoP v2在多项分子性质预测任务中达到或超越现有水平，且性能随数据规模可扩展，显示出基于细胞形态的分子表征学习具有良好前景。

Abstract: Recent advancements in image-based profiling techniques have enabled the collection of high-volume cell morphology data, allowing new molecular embedding models to learn from the experimental phenotypic perturbations of a molecule in a cell. Previously, we developed Molecule-Morphology Contrastive Pretraining (MoCoP), a strategy for aligning small molecule embeddings to morphology fingerprints extracted through CellProfiler. The resulting molecular representation showed transferable performance for quantitative structure--activity relationship (QSAR) prediction tasks. Here, we extend the method by using a deep-learning-based cell image encoding pipeline to extract more feature-rich morphology profiles and align them to the molecular embeddings through contrastive learning. The new embeddings encode more accurate information on how molecules perturb cell morphology and enable improvements for QSAR predictions through either fixed-embedding linear probes or fully flexible fine-tuning. Morphology retrieval performance scales log-linearly with training data size, suggesting continued improvements as larger datasets become available. The improved MoCoP v2 also achieves superior performance on toxicity prediction and competitive results on ADME and activity benchmarks, when compared with existing molecular embedding models that use both cell morphology and transcriptomic data during training.

</details>


### [29] [Auditing System-1 Models on Biosecurity-Relevant Benchmarks: Calibration, Selective Prediction, and Permutation Instability in a Non-Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30454)
*Kimon Antonios Provatas,Ilias Georgakopoulos-Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对某商业"系统1"（非生成式、单次前向传播）模型在生物安全相关任务上进行系统审计，使用WMDP、WMDP-Bio改写版和六个LAB-Bench子任务共6,020道选择题，评估准确率、校准、错误检测、选择性预测及选项顺序敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非生成式"系统1"模型推理成本极低，可作为大型流水线中的廉价组件，但其在生物安全相关任务上的可靠性缺乏系统评估，因此需要审计其实际表现与风险。

Method: 在6,020道选择题上审计一个商业System-1模型，题目来自WMDP、抗改写WMDP-Bio变体及六个LAB-Bench子任务；测量准确率、校准（ECE）、错误检测（AUROC）、选择性预测，以及答案选项呈现顺序的敏感性；采用四种循环轮换验证顺序效应，并用字节完全相同的重复调用作为对照；对轮换概率取平均并仅在低置信度项上应用。

Result: 准确率显著依赖任务。正确解读厂商不确定性字段后，模型校准良好（合并ECE 0.034），top-1概率可区分正确与错误预测（合并AUROC 0.820），但在较弱任务上两者明显退化。四种循环轮换下，37.4%的WMDP-Cyber题目答案发生改变，对照实验表明主要源于选项顺序而非运行间波动。对轮换概率取平均使WMDP-Cyber准确率提升3.8个百分点，仅对低置信度项应用即可恢复大部分增益且成本远低于全项平均。

Conclusion: System-1模型具有低成本优势且在校准与置信度排序上表现尚可，但准确率任务依赖性强、对选项顺序高度敏感，构成可靠性隐患；轮换平均与选择性应用可作为提升稳健性的廉价补救策略。

Abstract: Non-generative "System-1" models return structured probabilistic decisions in a single forward pass, without autoregressive decoding, at a small fraction of the inference cost of a generative model. This makes them of interest as inexpensive components in larger pipelines, but their reliability on biosecurity-relevant tasks has not been systematically examined. We audit one commercial System-1 model on 6,020 multiple-choice items drawn from the Weapons of Mass Destruction Proxy (WMDP), a paraphrase-robust WMDP-Bio variant, and six LAB-Bench subtasks, measuring accuracy, calibration, error detection, selective prediction, and sensitivity to the order in which answer options are presented. Accuracy is strongly task-dependent. Once the vendor's uncertainty field is correctly interpreted, the model is reasonably well calibrated (pooled expected calibration error 0.034) and its top-1 probability separates correct from incorrect predictions (pooled AUROC 0.820), though both degrade substantially on the weaker tasks. Under four cyclic rotations of the answer options, 37.4% of WMDP-Cyber items receive different answers; a control using byte-identical repeated calls attributes most of this to option order rather than run-to-run variation. Averaging probabilities across rotations improves WMDP-Cyber accuracy by 3.8 percentage points, and applying it only to low-confidence items recovers most of that gain at well under the cost of averaging every item.

</details>


### [30] [RAZOR: Pruning Replaceable Experts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30465)
*Mingyang Song,Mao Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练专家剪枝方法RAZOR，以"共识残差"度量专家功能可替代性，在8个设置中均取得最优九任务宏平均，且优于REAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型每token仅激活少量专家却需存储全部专家池，存储负担重；专家剪枝可缓解该问题。固定剪枝预算下目标是尽量保持原模型输出分布，但专家的使用频率或贡献幅度本身并不能决定移除它的损害——关键在于剩余计算能否替代该专家的功能。

Method: RAZOR为免训练专家剪枝方法，用共识残差（专家输出相对原始加权混合的偏差）衡量功能可替代性；在固定层输入下推导精确的单删除恒等式，考虑幸存者重归一化与路由器选中的补位(refill)，得到局部分数并在校准token上聚合以完成预算剪枝，无需梯度或恢复训练。

Result: 在GLM-4.7-Flash、Qwen3.6-35B-A3B、DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731、Hy3上，25%与50%剪枝共8个设置中，RAZOR的九任务宏平均均为评测方法中最高；在两个可匹配REAP基准的骨干上超出REAP 2.12–5.59分，并在全部36组配对任务比较中获胜；在四个匹配的GLM-4.7-Flash与Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型-预算设置中反向KL低于REAP。

Conclusion: 功能可替代性比使用频率/贡献幅度更能反映剪枝损害，RAZOR在任务保持与预测保真度上稳定领先。但Qwen3.6-35B-A3B生成响应分析显示多样性、格式与终止行为发生变化，说明任务保持与预测保真并不能保证生成稳定性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models activate few experts per token but store the full expert pool. Expert pruning reduces this storage burden; at a fixed pruning budget, the goal is to preserve the original model's output distribution as closely as possible. Yet an expert's usage or contribution magnitude does not by itself determine the damage caused by its removal. What matters is whether the surviving computation can replace its function. We introduce RAZOR, a training-free expert pruning method that scores functional replaceability using consensus residuals: deviations of expert outputs from the original weighted mixture. An exact single-deletion identity at a fixed layer input accounts for survivor renormalization and router-selected refill, providing local scores aggregated over calibration tokens for budgeted pruning without gradients or recovery training. On GLM-4.7-Flash, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731, and Hy3 at 25\% and 50\% expert removal, RAZOR achieves the highest nine-task macro average among the evaluated pruning methods in all eight settings. On the two backbones with matched REAP benchmark runs, it exceeds REAP by 2.12--5.59 points and wins all 36 paired task comparisons. It also lowers reverse KL relative to REAP in all four matched GLM-4.7-Flash and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model--budget settings. Analysis of responses generated by Qwen3.6-35B-A3B nevertheless reveals changes in diversity, formatting, and termination, underscoring that task retention and predictive fidelity do not ensure generation stability.

</details>


### [31] [Reliability-aware Cross-sample Enhancement for Robust Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30470)
*Menghua Jiang,Haokai Gao,Xiangui Kang,Haifeng Hu,Sijie Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知的跨样本增强框架RCE，同时应对噪声与模态缺失，通过自适应变分信息瓶颈、跨样本增强和可靠性感知融合提升多模态情感分析鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中多模态情感分析输入常受噪声干扰且存在模态缺失，而现有方法将这两类问题割裂处理，难以适应真实场景，导致性能下降。

Method: RCE框架包含三部分：(1) 自适应变分信息瓶颈，建模模态级不确定性并进行质量感知信息压缩，抑制不可靠模态中的冗余噪声；(2) 可靠性感知跨样本增强策略，从大规模候选池中检索高置信、语义一致的近邻以丰富和校准当前表示，缓解模态缺失造成的信息不足；(3) 结合跨模态交互与多层级可靠性感知融合机制，自适应聚合跨模态及不同增强阶段的信息。

Result: 在完整、含噪及模态缺失等多种设置下，RCE均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RCE通过统一建模噪声与模态缺失问题，能够生成更鲁棒的多模态表示，为现实场景下的多模态情感分析提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) aims to infer human emotions from multiple modalities such as text, audio, and vision. In practice, inputs are often corrupted by noise and missing modalities, which degrades performance. Existing methods typically address these challenges in isolation, limiting their effectiveness in realistic settings. To address this limitation, we propose a Reliability-aware Cross-sample Enhancement (RCE) framework. Specifically, RCE first introduces an adaptive variational information bottleneck to model modality-wise uncertainty and perform quality-aware information compression, thereby suppressing redundant noise in unreliable modalities. Furthermore, we design a reliability-aware cross-sample enhancement strategy that retrieves high-confidence, semantically consistent neighbors from a large candidate pool to enrich and calibrate current representations, effectively alleviating information deficiency caused by missing modalities. Building upon this, RCE integrates cross-modal interactions with a multilevel reliability-aware fusion mechanism to adaptively aggregate information across modalities and enhancement stages, leading to more robust multimodal representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RCE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across full, noisy, and missing-modality settings.

</details>


### [32] [Moment-guided edge sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30472)
*Weibin Cai,Reza Zafarani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于随机游走转移矩阵谱矩的"矩引导边采样"框架,通过组合法与低秩法精确计算边编辑引起的矩变化,揭示局部边编辑与全局图结构间的可解释、可控制联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边采样通过局部决策实现图级目标(如保持结构属性),由此产生核心难题:如何量化并控制单条边编辑(增删边)对全局图结构的影响。

Method: 基于随机游走转移矩阵谱矩的矩引导边采样框架;两种互补的矩变化精确计算方法:1)组合法,对低阶矩给出闭式更新,在维护的局部统计量下以常数时间计算;2)低秩法,利用局部性与循环迹不变性将计算压缩至编辑端点,支持任意阶矩与批量编辑,单边编辑时将成本从O(mn)降至O(m)。

Result: 矩变化提供了局部边模体的可解释结构签名,并可聚合为图级指纹;证明并验证保矩采样能保持相关结构属性(如三角形加权聚类系数);在监督节点分类中不同边结构影响各异,矩引导增强在图对比学习中具竞争力。

Conclusion: 谱矩可作为从局部边编辑到全局图结构及图学习的可解释、可控制桥梁。

Abstract: Edge sampling makes local decisions to achieve graph-level objectives, such as preserving structural properties. This creates a fundamental challenge: \textit{how can the effect of a local edge edit (i.e., edge addition or removal) on global graph structure be quantified and controlled?} We address this challenge with a \textit{moment-guided edge sampling framework} based on spectral moments of the random-walk transition matrix. We compute exact moment changes through two complementary methods: a combinatorial method with closed-form updates for low-order moments, and a low-rank method that exploits \textit{locality} and \textit{cyclic trace invariance} to compress computations to edited endpoints, supporting arbitrary moment orders and batched edits. For single-edge edits at fixed moment orders, the low-rank method reduces the cost from $O(mn)$ to $O(m)$, while the combinatorial method evaluates low-order changes in constant time given maintained local statistics. These moment changes provide \textbf{interpretable structural signatures} of local edge motifs that aggregate into graph-level fingerprints. This structural meaning motivates us to ask whether preserving moments also preserves the graph properties. We further derive and validate that moment-preserving sampling can \textbf{retain related structural properties}, including triangle-weighted clustering coefficient. These structural insights enable \textbf{analysis and improvement of graph learning}: different edge structures have distinct effects on supervised node classification, while moment-guided augmentation is competitive for graph contrastive learning. Together, these findings establish moments as an interpretable and controllable bridge from local edge edits to global graph structure and learning.

</details>


### [33] [Mentored Decoding: Faster Inference meets Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30474)
*Vivien Tran-Thien,Richard Nock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了有损投机解码（mentored decoding）能够同时提升推理速度与模型质量，通过将其与boosting理论联系并推广至全体f-散度，揭示了关键性质并提出高效数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有损投机解码允许相对目标模型的偏移以进一步提速，实验观察到其结果模型在质量上也能超越目标模型，但缺乏严格的理论解释。本文旨在从理论上证明并刻画这一现象。

Method: 提出称为mentored decoding的有损投机解码形式化方法，将推理与经典ML训练理论boosting相联系，并将其推广至整个f-散度集合；分析其对偶问题并设计数据结构。

Result: 揭示了mentored decoding的关键性质：(i) 全变差情形具有尤其优美的几何性质；(ii) 任意f-散度可有与boosting相容性直接相关的简单近似；(iii) 构建了一个与散度无关的O(n)空间、O(sort(n))时间的数据结构，可在O(log n)时间查询对偶问题最优参数，并在O(n)时间构造最优mentored分布。

Conclusion: 该工作为有损投机解码同时提升速度与质量的现象提供了形式化理论保证，并给出通用且高效的算法与数据结构支撑。

Abstract: Speculative decoding is a successful technique speeding up inference of a target autoregressive language model via a fast drafter model. Lossy speculative decoding allows a drift with respect to the target to further improve speed. Interestingly, it has been observed experimentally that the resulting model can $\textit{also}$ beat the target $\textit{quality-wise}$. Our paper formally proves how such a feat is possible with a formal approach to lossy speculative decoding called $\textit{mentored decoding}$. To get there, we connect inference to a celebrated ML training theory, $\textit{boosting}$, and proceed via the generalization of mentored decoding to the whole set of $f$-divergences. We uncover key properties of mentored decoding, among which (i) the particularly appealing geometric nature of the total variation case, (ii) simple approximations for any $f$-divergence in direct relation with boosting compliance, and (iii) a $\textit{divergence independent}$ $O(n)$ space and $O(\mathrm{sort}(n))$ time data structure built on drafter and target outputs, which allows to query the optimal parameters of the dual problem in $O(\log n)$ time and constructing optimal mentored distributions in $O(n)$ time for any $f$-divergence.

</details>


### [34] [Geometric Feature Learning for Functional Data Valued on the Symmetric Positive Definite Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30487)
*Samuel V. Singh,Mimi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MatFAE——一种在对称正定(SPD)矩阵黎曼流形上学习轨迹的功能性神经网络，用于高效学习高维SPD轨迹表示，并应用于fMRI神经影像分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数面向离散时间序列的神经网络无法刻画流形值数据的连续动态特性，且在SPD流形上学习轨迹表示时面临高维、非线性与可解释性不足的问题。

Method: MatFAE包含内在层(intrinsic layers)，将流形值函数映射为欧氏向量值函数；随后由功能层(functional layer)投影到有限维欧氏空间。网络将序列视为连续函数而非离散序列，从而在潜在表示中编码轨迹动态(如一阶导数)；同时处理反向传播中的矩阵分解问题，并通过功能权重形态提供可解释性。

Result: 在多个fMRI数据集上验证，MatFAE能够从高维SPD轨迹中高效学习信息丰富的表示。

Conclusion: MatFAE对SPD轨迹表示学习具有实际价值，适用于真实神经影像分析场景。

Abstract: We here develop a functional neural network, termed MatFAE, for learning trajectories on the Riemannian manifold of symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrices. MatFAE features intrinsic layers that map manifold-valued functions to Euclidean vector-valued functions, followed by a functional layer that projects them into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space. Unlike most neural networks for discrete-time sequences, MatFAE treats each sequence as a continuous function and can therefore encode trajectory dynamics (e.g., first-order derivatives) in its latent representations. Additionally, the morphology of the functional weights in the functional layer offers interpretability by revealing the regions of the input functional data that contribute most to the latent representations. We justify the design principles and properties of each intrinsic layer and detail how matrix factorization is handled during backpropagation. We apply MatFAE to a range of fMRI datasets, demonstrating its ability to efficiently learn informative representations from high-dimensional SPD trajectories and its practical value for real-world neuroimaging analysis.

</details>


### [35] [Learning to Bias: Machine Learning-Enhanced Particle Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30498)
*Apoorv Srivastava,Eric Darve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经最优粒子滤波（NOPFs），将学习到的提议分布集成到粒子滤波框架中，提升样本效率与分布精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准粒子滤波（PF）基于重要性采样，但样本效率低、随维度扩展性差，部分原因在于提议分布次优。作者希望在不改变滤波目标的前提下改进提议分布。

Method: 引入NOPFs，从离线模拟的一步条件元组中学习对最优提议分布的摊销近似；将学习到的提议作为标准PF更新中的即插即用替代，并通过标准重要性权重对样本进行校正，在标准支撑集与密度可评估假设下，方法渐近地针对同一滤波分布。

Result: 在不同推断复杂度的随机非线性基准上，NOPFs相比标准PF基线在样本效率和分布精度上均有提升，且计算开销适中。

Conclusion: 该方法将数据驱动的提议学习融入经典推断，不改变底层滤波目标，是一种有效的PF增强方案。

Abstract: Sequential inference estimates latent states from noisy and incomplete observations. Particle Filters (PFs), a class of Monte Carlo methods based on importance sampling, provide a flexible framework for this task, but often suffer from poor sample efficiency and unfavorable scaling with dimension, partly due to suboptimal proposal distributions.
