We address these challenges by integrating learned proposals into the PF framework. We introduce Neural Optimal Particle Filters (NOPFs), which learn an amortized approximation to the optimal proposal from offline simulated one-step conditioning tuples. The learned proposal is used as a drop-in replacement in standard PF updates, with samples corrected by standard importance weights so that the method asymptotically targets the same filtering distribution under standard support and density-evaluation assumptions. Across stochastic nonlinear benchmarks of varying inference complexity, NOPFs improve sample efficiency and distributional accuracy over standard PF baselines with modest computational overhead. The approach integrates data-driven proposal learning into classical inference without altering the underlying filtering objective.

</details>


### [36] [PolicyAttention: Softmax Attention Implements Policy Mirror Descent for Closed-Loop Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30500)
*Yuhe Sui,Yingzhi Tang,Shufang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究因果softmax注意力能否作为重复控制器实现负熵策略镜像下降(PMD)，而非一步代数恒等式。作者构造了固定因果softmax的actor–environment–one-step-critic协议，将残差传播到实际返回策略，并通过预训练的pre-LN Transformer进行实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知Q-TD-PMD递归仅提供一步代数对应，尚不清楚因果softmax注意力能否在重复控制、含残差的真实执行流程中稳定实现PMD。作者旨在建立带显式残差的编译构造，并检验学习得到的actor在重复控制下能否逼近Exact PMD oracle。

Method: 构造一个固定的causal-softmax actor–environment–one-step-critic协议，显式给出actor、routing、sampling与normalization残差并传播至返回策略；明确有限logit/满支撑域、外部tokenization与采样边界，以及归一化编译所需的mean-zero LayerNorm载体条件。采用单独训练的pre-LN Transformer进行恢复实验，并以冻结的一步审计模型对比固定规则；设置S=4的预注册五次重复控制测试与S=8、16的对比实验，区分exact critic与learned critic。

Result: 冻结一步审计模型在测试的固定规则中最接近PMD。S=4重复控制测试中，学习actor+exact one-step critic的中位返回策略损失为Exact PMD oracle的1.052×，并在四次无重训练迁移中保持判据；同检查点配学习critic时中位为1.050×（无注册裕度）。S=8时，用学习critic替换exact critic使T=20中位损失升至0.0225，但仍比Liang–Lai与Algorithm Distillation适配低20.2–24.2×损失；该比较为单侧采样critic界，因PolicyAttention每轮消耗144个生成转移，而适配仅用20个on-policy转移。S=8、16时，exact-critic共同试验台比较仍比这些适配低17.7–28.2×损失。

Conclusion: 因果softmax注意力可作为重复控制器近似实现PMD，但严格性依赖critic类型与采样预算：exact critic下表现接近oracle并在迁移中保持；learned critic下行仍成立但存在信息不对称。严格的20转移同预算对比尚未闭合，留待后续工作。

Abstract: Can causal softmax attention implement policy mirror descent as a repeated controller rather than a one-step algebraic identity? Negative-entropy policy mirror descent (PMD) has the statewise update $\operatorname{PMD}_η(π,Q)=\operatorname{softmax}(\logπ+ηQ)$. Building on the known Q-TD-PMD recursion, we construct one fixed causal-softmax actor--environment--one-step-critic protocol with explicit actor, routing, sampling, and normalization residuals, and propagate them to the policy actually returned. The construction states the finite-logit/full-support domain, the external tokenization and sampling boundary, and the mean-zero LayerNorm carrier conditions required by the normalized compilation.
  Separately trained pre-LN Transformers recover the target computation empirically. A frozen one-step audit model is closest to PMD among the tested fixed rules; in a preregistered five-run $S=4$ repeated-control test, the learned actor with an exact one-step critic reaches median returned-policy loss $1.052\times$ the Exact PMD oracle and retains the criterion across four no-retraining shifts. The same checkpoints with their learned critic give descriptive median $1.050\times$ the oracle (no registered margin). At $S=8$, replacing the exact critic by the learned critic raises median $T=20$ loss to $0.0225$ yet leaves the Liang--Lai and Algorithm Distillation adaptations $20.2$--$24.2\times$ higher-loss; this is a one-sided sampled-critic bound because PolicyAttention consumes 144 generative transitions per round versus 20 on-policy transitions for the adaptations. The strict 20-transition comparison remains open. At $S=8,16$, the exact-critic common-harness comparison remains $17.7$--$28.2\times$ lower-loss than those adaptations, with the information asymmetry stated locally.

</details>


### [37] [To Solve Bilevel Optimization with Nonconvex Lower Levels, We Need Second-Order Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30501)
*Zhiyao Zhang,Menglu Yu,Alvaro Velasquez,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对一般下层非凸双层优化（LLNC-BLO）问题，提出基于二阶平稳性代理的重构方法，并设计 PROBE 算法，首次实现该类问题的有限时间收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层优化研究大多局限于下层强凸（LLSC）或下层一般凸（LLGC）假设，这些假设过于严格，无法覆盖许多实际机器学习问题。而在更具现实意义的 LLNC 设定下，现有工作要么依赖下层目标函数的额外结构，要么沿用源自凸设定的"一阶平稳性重构"作为下层代理，该代理在非凸情形下可能失效。因此需要在一般 LLNC 设定下研究 BLO 的求解方法。

Method: 提出用基于二阶平稳性的代理问题重构非凸下层问题，其解可保证为下层问题的局部最优解。基于该重构提出 PROBE（Perturbed gradient algorithm for bilevel problem，双层问题扰动梯度算法），通过探测并逃离下层鞍点来克服以往方法的局限。

Result: 理论证明 PROBE 达到 $O(T^{-2/5})$ 的有限时间收敛速率（T 为迭代次数）。据作者所知，这是首个在一般 LLNC-BLO 中实现下层二阶平稳解有限时间收敛的工作。实验表明，在大语言模型数据筛选任务和元学习任务上，PROBE 均优于现有 SOTA 方法。

Conclusion: 该工作将双层优化的可解性从凸下层设定推广到一般非凸下层设定，通过二阶平稳性重构与扰动梯度机制，在理论上和实验上均验证了方法的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Although bilevel optimization (BLO) has emerged as a powerful framework for addressing many complex and nested machine learning problems in recent years, most existing studies are confined to the lower-level strongly convex (LLSC) or lower-level generally convex (LLGC) settings (i.e., the lower-level objective function is assumed to be, at least, convex). While the LLSC/LLGC assumptions render more tractable algorithmic design and theoretical analysis, they are too rigid to encompass many machine learning problems in practice. The limitations of LLSC/LLGC assumptions in BLO motivate us to investigate solving the BLO problem in the general lower-level nonconvex (LLNC) settings, which remains in its infancy. In the literature on LLNC-BLO, most of the existing works either require additional structures in the lower-level objective function for tractable theoretical analysis, or adopt the first-order stationarity reformulation as a lower-level surrogate problem, which is inherited from the LLSC/LLGC settings but could lose their effectiveness in the LLNC setting. To bridge this gap, we propose to reformulate the nonconvex lower-level problem using a second-order stationarity-based surrogate, the solution of which guarantees a local optimal solution at the lower level. Based on this reformulation, we propose the PROBE (Perturbed gradient algorithm for bilevel problem) and show that it overcomes the limitations of prior works by probing and escaping lower-level saddle points. We prove that PROBE achieves a finite-time convergence rate of $O(T^{-2/5})$, where T denotes iterations. To our knowledge, this work is the first to establish the finite-time convergence for achieving lower-level second-order stationary solutions in general LLNC-BLO. Our experiments on both a large language model-based data curation task and a meta-learning task also show that PROBE outperforms SOTA methods.

</details>


### [38] [Federated Targeted Maximum Likelihood Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30503)
*Diyang Li,Fei Wang,Kyra Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个联邦式TMLE算法，可在不汇聚个体数据的前提下完成针对性最大似然估计的目标化步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学或运营决策所需的证据常分散于医院、银行、登记机构等无法共享个体观测的数据孤岛；联邦学习将计算移到数据端，而TMLE虽能给出尊重模型且支持有效推断的插件估计，却一直是完全中心化的流程，二者之间存在空白。

Method: 提出两个互补框架：FedTMLE-G聚合局部梯度，逐步复现中心化目标化步骤；FedTMLE-L让各机构先完成自身波动拟合，再单次交换拟合更新，以本地自主性换取同步保真度。针对梯度聚合，设计有限精度协议，传输“变化量”而非“数值”，并在交换次数与比特数的显式界内认证目标化精度；随后对接受的更新做描述长度分析。

Result: 描述长度分析表明，有限通信带来的数值目标化误差相对于抽样不确定性可忽略，即计算估计量的代价与选择估计量的复杂度是分离的。分析还表明，保持数据本地并不构成TMLE的隐私保证，因为全记录重构的不稳定性未必能阻止指定敏感属性的恢复。针对个性化本地平均版本，非凸收敛界将因平均而损失的改进归因于各本地拟合之间的分歧，并揭示机构间等权影响与小数据孤岛抽样变异之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 联邦TMLE在任意目标、损失与波动族下实现了目标化步骤的联邦化，并刻画了通信、精度、隐私与机构公平性之间的权衡。

Abstract: The evidence behind a scientific or operational decision is often held by hospitals, banks, or registries that cannot pool individual observations. Cross-silo federated learning moves computation to the data and exchanges agreed summaries. Targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE) refines a flexible initial fit, yielding plug-in estimators that respect the model and support efficient inference. TMLE itself, however, has remained a fully centralized procedure. To fill this gap, our paper introduces the first federated TMLE algorithm. We federate targeting itself, for an arbitrary target, loss, and fluctuation family, through two complementary frameworks. FedTMLE-G aggregates local gradients and reproduces centralized targeting step for step. FedTMLE-L lets each institution complete its own fluctuation fit before a single exchange of fitted updates, trading synchronized fidelity for local autonomy. For gradient aggregation, we develop a finite-precision protocol that transmits changes rather than values and certifies targeting accuracy within explicit bounds on exchanges and bits. A description-length analysis of the accepted updates then shows that this finite communication leaves numerical targeting error negligible against sampling uncertainty. The cost of computing an estimator is thus distinct from the complexity of selecting it. Our analysis also indicates that keeping data local is not itself a privacy guarantee of TMLE, since instability of full-record reconstruction need not prevent recovery of a specified sensitive attribute. For a personalized version of local averaging, institutions retain their own estimates and leave once local targeting is complete. A nonconvex convergence bound charges the improvement forfeited through averaging to disagreement among local fits and exposes a tradeoff between equal institutional influence and the sampling variability of small silos.

</details>

*Felix S. Reimers,Ola Huse Ramstad,Aliaksandr Hubin,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用储备池计算框架分析秀丽隐杆线虫连接组，将其实现为回声状态网络并在神经启发任务上测试；结果表明生物布线不一定带来更优性能，随机零模型常表现更好，且结果高度依赖储备池配置与连接组来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 秀丽隐杆线虫是首个已发布完整神经系统物理连接组的生物，作者希望借助计算视角检验生物神经网络连接组是否具备功能优势，并探索将其作为神经启发任务基准的可行性。

Result: 生物布线及基于生物信息配置输入输出节点，并不必然带来更好性能；随机零模型在所选基准上常优于原始连接组。结果高度依赖储备池配置和连接组的获取方式；不同年龄连接组可产生不同结果，且无明显趋势。

Conclusion: 生物连接组的结构优势在储备池计算基准中未得到一致验证，随机零模型往往可达到甚至超过其表现。因此，使用连接组作为基准时需谨慎考虑储备池配置与数据来源的影响，不能简单假设生物布线必然优于随机结构。

Abstract: The aim of this work is to examine the connectomes of Caenorhabditis elegans through a computational lens using the reservoir computing framework. Connectomes are mappings of biological neural networks; C. elegans is the first organism for which physical connectomes covering the whole nervous system have been published. The connectomes of C. elegans used in this paper have been derived at different ages of the organism and are based on three different ways of measuring inter-cellular connections. They have, with minimal preprocessing, been implemented as reservoirs in the form of echo state networks, which are recurrent neural networks. In reservoir computing, the reservoir itself is not trained, rather the output of the reservoir is passed to a comparatively small read-out module in which training takes place. Training and testing is conducted in different neuro-inspired tasks, with the aim of using these tasks as a benchmark for the connectomes. This process has been repeated with different configurations of the reservoir and equally sized but randomized null models have been used for comparison. The results show that the biological wiring and a bio-informed configuration of input and output nodes of the reservoirs do not necessarily lead to better performance. Contrarily, the randomized null models are often outperforming the original connectomes on the chosen benchmarks. At the same time it becomes clear that the results depend a lot on the configuration of the reservoir and the way the connectome has been derived from the organism. Connectomes from different ages may produce varying outcome, without a clear trend becoming visible.

</details>


### [40] [AutoResearch at Production Scale: Failure Modes and a Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30541)
*Aparajith Chandran,Juwon Kim,Saurav Jha,Pablo Castells,Florian Hottier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将Karpathy的AutoResearch范式（LLM迭代编辑训练脚本并保留提升指标者）应用于生产级推荐系统的embedding优化，历经12周、220+实验，识别出五种失败模式并提出"prevent-persist-redirect"三原则脚手架，取得1.82x Recall@6与2.1x coherence提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产推荐pipeline中embedding系统的优化需要系统性探索，但在规模化下消耗不成比例的工程投入。作者希望验证LLM自动化研究范式能否从受控设置迁移到真实生产场景（长迭代、多GPU、多指标、跨周campaign），并揭示该迁移中的结构性障碍。

Method: 在两个独立开发的书籍推荐表示学习系统上部署AutoResearch范式（LLM迭代编辑训练脚本、保留提升held-out标量指标的修改），运行12周、220+次实验；归纳出五种重复出现的失败模式：infrastructure fragility、agent memory decay、search-direction stagnation、iteration-cost asymmetry、metric fixation；提出三原则脚手架prevent-persist-redirect，将每种失败模式映射到结构性补救，且其实例化随迭代成本扩展。

Result: 相比人工调优baseline，框架带来1.82x Recall@6提升与2.1x coherence提升；agent自主设计了text-only fallback，将catalog coverage扩展5.8x。两个系统单次迭代成本相差近三个数量级，却呈现相同失败模式，表明这些是生产级自主研究的结构性属性而非特定应用的偶然产物。

Conclusion: AutoResearch范式可有效迁移至生产规模，但需结构化脚手架支撑；五种失败模式与迭代成本无关地复现，说明其具有普适性。该工作为大规模自主研究系统的工程设计提供了可扩展的失败模式-补救映射框架。

Abstract: Optimizing embedding systems for production recommendation pipelines demands systematic exploration that consumes disproportionate engineering effort at scale. We apply Andrej Karpathy's AutoResearch paradigm -- a large language model that iteratively edits a training script and retains modifications that improve a held-out scalar metric -- to automate this exploration. We report on twelve weeks of running this paradigm at production scale, where iterations consume hours of multi-GPU compute, evaluation involves competing criteria, and campaigns span weeks across many training jobs. Across two independently developed representation-learning systems for a book recommendation pipeline, we ran 220+ experiments and observed five recurring failure modes absent from the original setting: infrastructure fragility, agent memory decay, search-direction stagnation, iteration-cost asymmetry, and metric fixation. We contribute a three-principle scaffolding design -- prevent, persist, redirect -- that maps each failure mode to a structural remedy and whose instantiation scales with iteration cost. The framework produced a 1.82x Recall@6 lift and a 2.1x coherence lift over hand-tuned baselines, and the agent autonomously designed a text-only fallback that expanded catalog coverage by 5.8x. The two systems span nearly three orders of magnitude in per-iteration cost yet exhibit the same failure modes, suggesting these are structural properties of production-scale autonomous research rather than artifacts of either application.

</details>


### [41] [GyroNovo: Error-Guided Fragment Imputation with Mass-Aware Attention for \textit{De Novo} Peptide Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30542)
*Abdellah El Mekki,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GyroNovo：利用解码器误差自适应调整缺失片段填充目标，并引入质量感知旋转位置编码，提升从头肽段测序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验质谱常稀疏、嘈杂且不完整，关键的b/y离子片段缺失；现有方法将潜伏空间填充视为固定重建任务，未考虑哪些缺失片段与解码器错误最相关，且峰表示未显式建模峰间质量差。

Method: 一是用训练中观察到的解码器误差调整填充目标，优先恢复与频繁解码错误相关的片段，并基于解码错误分布构造易/难增强视图，使解码器适应不同程度的谱损坏与片段缺失；二是在自注意力中通过旋转嵌入编码峰间成对质量差，引入质量感知归纳偏置。推理时保持标准编码器-填充器-解码器架构，无需额外输入或辅助搜索。

Result: 在NovoBench上，相比最优基线，肽段级精度提升约9个百分点，氨基酸级精度提升约7个百分点。

Conclusion: GyroNovo通过将缺失片段恢复与解码器行为对齐，并显式建模肽段碎裂背后的质量关系，显著提升从头肽段测序性能，且推理时不增加额外依赖。

Abstract: De novo peptide sequencing from tandem mass spectra is essential for identifying peptides without relying on reference databases. Despite advances in deep learning, accurate sequencing remains challenging because experimental spectra are often sparse, noisy, and incomplete, leaving informative b- and y-ion fragments unobserved. Existing methods attempt to recover this missing evidence via latent-space imputation before autoregressive decoding. However, they typically treat imputation as a fixed reconstruction task, without considering which missing fragments are most relevant to decoder errors. Moreover, existing peak representations do not explicitly model mass differences between peaks, despite their fundamental importance.
  We introduce GyroNovo, a framework with two main contributions. First, we use decoder errors observed during training to adapt the imputation objective, prioritizing fragments associated with frequent decoding errors. We further use the decoder error distribution to construct easy and hard augmented views of each spectrum, enabling the decoder to learn under varying degrees of spectral corruption and missing-fragment severity. Second, we introduce a mass-aware inductive bias into self-attention by using rotary embeddings to encode pairwise mass differences between spectral peaks. Together, these components align missing-fragment recovery with decoder behavior while explicitly incorporating the mass relationships that underlie peptide fragmentation. At inference time, GyroNovo retains a standard encoder-imputer-decoder architecture and requires neither additional inputs nor auxiliary search procedures. Experiments on NovoBench show gains of about 9 percentage points in peptide-level precision and 7 percentage points in amino-acid-level precision over the state-of-the-art baseline. Code: https://github.com/UBC-NLP/gyronovo.

</details>


### [42] [Dynamic Regret in Online Convex Optimization with Indicator Switching Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30556)
*Naram Mhaisen,George Iosifidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究带指示切换成本(固定惩罚)的在线凸优化动态遗憾,提出基于元学习框架的随机懒惰FTRL算法,达到对S_T与P_T的自适应最优界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指示切换成本刻画服务器激活、模型部署、缓存更新等启动开销,在有界域上可退化为基于范数的移动成本。现有对指示成本的保证仅处理静态比较器,无法直接扩展到动态遗憾,且直接扩展被证明不可行,因此需要新方法。

Method: 提出元学习框架:一组在二进时间尺度上重启的随机懒惰FTRL基学习器,由移动感知的主学习器(мастер)聚合,主学习器混合各基学习器的提议密度,并通过相邻混合分布的最大耦合(maximal coupling)采样动作。

Result: 期望意义下 R^1_T ≤ Õ(min{√(T(S_T+1)), T^{2/3}(P_T+1)^{1/3}}),其中 R^1_T 为动态遗憾加累计指示切换成本,S_T 为比较器切换次数,P_T 为比较器路径长度。该界对所有序列同时成立且无需 S_T 或 P_T 的先验知识,对分段常数比较器的跟踪是极小极大最优(忽略对数因子),并涵盖总路径长度小的高频移动比较器。

Conclusion: 在指示切换成本下实现了无需先验参数的动态遗憾保证,达到分段常数跟踪的极小极大最优并兼顾小路径长度情形,解决了既有技术无法直接扩展的问题。

Abstract: We study dynamic regret in online convex optimization with an \emph{indicator switching cost}: a fixed penalty incurred whenever two consecutive decisions differ. This captures startup overheads such as server activation, model deployment, and cache updates, and on a bounded domain it recovers norm-based movement costs as a special case. Existing guarantees for indicator costs handle only static comparators. We show that a direct extension of these techniques to dynamic regret provably fails, motivating a different approach. We propose a meta-learning framework: a set of randomized lazy FTRL base learners restarted at dyadic time scales, aggregated by a movement-aware master that mixes their proposal densities and samples actions via maximal coupling of consecutive mixtures. The resulting algorithm satisfies, in expectation, $\mathcal{R}^{\mathbf{1}}_T \le \tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\min\{\sqrt{T(S_T{+}1)},T^{2/3}(P_T+1)^{1/3}\})$, where $\mathcal{R}^{\mathbf{1}}_T$ is the dynamic regret plus the cumulative indicator switching cost, $S_T$ counts comparator switches, and $P_T$ is the comparator path length. The bound holds simultaneously for all sequences and requires no prior knowledge of $S_T$ or $P_T$: it is minimax-optimal (up to logarithmic factors) for tracking piecewise-constant comparators, and also captures frequently moving comparators with small total path length.

</details>


### [43] [Entropy Regularization: A Free Correction to Cross-Entropy for Verified Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30572)
*Mihir Dhanakshirur,Adam Ousherovitch,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了在可验证任务中交叉熵最小化与验证器风险最小化的错位问题，提出用熵正则化交叉熵（ER-CE）控制策略支撑集，从而提升正确输出概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常在下游目标并非模仿专家解、而是产出任意可通过验证器的输出时，仍使用交叉熵（CE）在专家示范上做后训练。这在数学推理、代码生成等存在多个正确解、但训练数据每题仅含一个专家解的可验证领域尤为突出。作者希望指出并解决CE最小化与验证器风险最小化之间的目标错位。

Method: 先构造学习理论反例，形式化证明两个策略可对观察到的示范赋予相同似然，却在错误输出上分配不同概率质量，导致CE最小化选中次优策略。分析指出控制学习策略的支撑集可防止概率质量扩散到无支撑输出。由于支撑集大小不可微且计算不可行，提出熵正则化交叉熵（ER-CE），以token级Shannon熵作为可处理代理。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成基准上，熵正则化训练相较标准交叉熵持续提升验证器准确率。

Conclusion: 研究识别出可验证任务中基于模仿的后训练的一个简单失败模式，并提供了一个更契合产出正确输出的实用训练目标。

Abstract: Large language models are often post-trained on expert demonstrations using cross-entropy (CE), even when the downstream objective is not to imitate the demonstrated solution but to produce any output accepted by a verifier. This mismatch is seen in verifiable domains with multiple correct solutions, such as mathematical reasoning and code generation, where training data may contain only one expert solution per problem. We show that minimizing cross-entropy can be misaligned with minimizing verifier risk; two policies can assign identical likelihood to the observed demonstrations while placing different probability mass on incorrect outputs. This is formalized through a learning-theoretic counterexample in which CE minimization selects a suboptimal policy. We identify that controlling the support of the learned policy can solve this problem by preventing probability mass from spreading to unsupported outputs. Since support size is non-differentiable and computationally intractable, we propose entropy-regularized cross-entropy (ER-CE), using token-level Shannon entropy as a tractable proxy. Finally, across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, we find that entropy-regularized training consistently improves verifier accuracy over standard cross-entropy. Our results identify a simple failure mode of imitation-based post-training in verifiable tasks and provide a practical objective that is better aligned with producing correct outputs.

</details>


### [44] [Reinforcement Learning of Communication in a Mesh of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30578)
*Mehmet Kerem Turkcan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TalkMesh 提出一种去中心化的小语言模型多智能体网格：智能体各自采样并评估置信度，由最高置信度者广播提示、低置信者据此修正，经 gossip 共识逼近置信度加权投票；以群体相对策略优化训练通信策略。3 个智能体（最多 6 个输出）即可达到 3 个模型各自 32 样本多数投票的精度；8 智能体训练、32 智能体评估时，GSM8K（Qwen3.5-0.8B）从 0.568 提升至 0.705，MATH-500（SmolLM3-3B）从 0.492 提升至 0.722；防御式网格在合谋攻击下保留 0.507 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型借助测试时更多计算可提升准确率，但对独立样本做多数投票会饱和：样本增多时投票收敛到模型最高频答案，无法突破单模型的知识与推理上限。作者希望用智能体间通信补充采样所缺乏的信息——解题智能体可把关键步骤传给其他智能体，从而在远少于多数投票所需的样本量下获得同等或更高精度。

Method: 构造 TalkMesh 去中心化网格：每个智能体采样一个候选解，并用训练得到的置信度头打分；最高置信度智能体广播提示，低于置信阈值的智能体进行修正，且仅保留评分高于原候选的修正结果；用 gossip 共识在无中心协调者的情况下逼近置信度加权投票。通信策略（写提示与修正）通过群体相对策略优化（GRPO），以修正后正确性变化为奖励进行训练。防御版本让智能体用自己的置信度头对解重新打分，以抵抗伪造置信度与投毒提示。

Result: 3 个智能体、合计至多生成 6 个输出时，网格精度达到三个模型各自对 32 个样本做多数投票的水平；训练用至多 8 个智能体、评估用 32 个时，GSM8K 上 Qwen3.5-0.8B 从自一致性 0.568 提升到 0.705，MATH-500 上 SmolLM3-3B 从 0.492 提升到 0.722。8 个智能体中 4 个以伪造置信度和投毒提示合谋错误答案时，多数投票精度降至 0.000（Qwen3.5-0.8B，GSM8K），而防御式网格仍保持 0.507。在推理、具身协调与交通信号控制任务中，当执行智能体无法观测所需信息而另一智能体可发送该信息时，消息能改善决策。

Conclusion: 通信可提供采样无法获得的信息，使小型语言模型网格以远少的采样量达到甚至超越多数投票精度，并具备跨任务泛化性；同时网格对合谋与投毒存在脆弱性，而基于自身置信度重打分的防御机制可显著缓解该风险。

Abstract: Language models gain accuracy from more compute at test time, but majority voting over independent samples saturates: as samples grow, the vote converges to the model's most frequent answer. Communication can add what sampling cannot: an agent that solves a problem can pass the key step to the others. We present TalkMesh, a decentralized mesh of small language model agents that learns when and what to communicate. Each agent samples a proposal and scores it with a trained confidence head. The most confident agent broadcasts a hint; agents below a confidence threshold revise, keeping each revision that outscores its proposal. Gossip consensus approximates the vote weighted by confidence without a coordinator. A talk policy, trained with group relative policy optimization on the change in correctness after revision, writes hints and revisions. With three agents, which together generate at most six outputs, the mesh reaches the accuracy of majority voting over 32 samples with each of three models. Trained with at most 8 agents and evaluated with 32, it raises accuracy from 0.568 under self-consistency to 0.705 (Qwen3.5-0.8B, GSM8K) and from 0.492 to 0.722 (SmolLM3-3B, MATH-500). When 4 of 8 agents collude on a wrong answer with fabricated confidence and poisoned hints, majority vote accuracy falls to 0.000 (Qwen3.5-0.8B, GSM8K). A defended mesh, whose agents rescore solutions with their own confidence heads, retains 0.507. Across reasoning, embodied coordination, and traffic signal control, messages improve a decision when the acting agent cannot observe the information it requires and another agent can send it.

</details>


### [45] [Energy-efficient operation of neural operators for virtual sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30580)
*Jason Yoo,Samrendra Roy,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究虚拟传感中共享空间计算对重复更新能耗的影响，通过热交换器服务实验量化了计算复用带来的能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟传感需要在不断变化的观测下重复重建物理场，且通常基于固定几何结构。作者希望探究共享的空间计算能否在保留选定检查点及其预测结果的前提下，降低这些更新的能耗。

Method: 在热交换器服务场景中，对比标准编译器冻结（freezing）与显式主干复用（trunk reuse）两种策略相对图重放（graph replay）的运行能耗；并在15 W固定频率模式下，将复用结合图重放与即时执行（eager execution）进行比较；使用DeepONet和FNO两种算子作为对照，以区分可复用算术与启动开销的影响。

Result: 标准编译器冻结与显式主干复用相对图重放取得相近的运行能耗降低：每秒1次请求时约1%，每秒40次请求时约20%。在15 W固定频率模式下，复用结合图重放完成同一请求序列的总能耗（含准备与等待）比即时执行低22.0%至22.5%。准备、伪影构建和worker替换会带来重复推理之外的额外开销。

Conclusion: 算子结构（如DeepONet与FNO）与运行能耗存在关联；更新频率和执行生命周期共同决定物理场虚拟传感中计算复用收益的大小。

Abstract: Virtual sensing repeatedly reconstructs physical fields from changing observations, often on a fixed geometry. We investigate how shared spatial computation reduces the energy of these updates while retaining the selected checkpoint and its evaluated predictions. In a heat-exchanger service, standard compiler freezing and explicit trunk reuse give similar operating energy reductions relative to graph replay: approximately 1% at one request per second and 20% at forty requests per second. In 15 W mode with fixed clocks, reuse with graph replay completes the same request sequence with 22.0 to 22.5% less energy than eager execution, including preparation and waiting. DeepONet and Fourier neural operator (FNO) controls distinguish the effects of reusable arithmetic and launch overhead. Preparation, artifact construction, and worker replacement add costs outside repeated inference. These results connect operator structure to operating energy and show how update frequency and execution lifetime govern the benefit of computation reuse in physical-field virtual sensing.

</details>


### [46] [QSV: Quat-Sphere-Vision for Coupled Quaternion Attention on Spherical Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30592)
*Nicholas Foley,Devin Marinelli,Donny Moore,Diego Enriquez,Amanda Fernandez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QSV用单个单位四元数替代标准注意力中的Q/K/V三元投影，通过相对四元数同时生成注意力logit和特征传输；消融显示传输通道是主要贡献者，但同等参数量下标准注意力及二维格点模型均超过QSV，表明球面几何本身并未带来增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力依赖三组独立学习的投影（W_Q、W_K、W_V）来分别决定注意力强度与聚合前特征变换，参数冗余且角色划分隐含。作者希望用单个单位四元数统一这两项功能，并检验稀疏球面几何（同心Fibonacci球上的kNN图）是否能提供额外的归纳偏置优势。

Method: 提出Quat-Sphere-Vision（QSV）：每个token学习一个单位四元数q_i，用相对四元数r_ij = q_i* ⊗ q_j 同时给出注意力logit Re(r_ij)与三明治积特征传输 x ↦ r_ij ⊗ x ⊗ r_ij*；消息在同心Fibonacci球上的稀疏kNN图中传播。设计仅改动目标组件的消融（去掉传输、注意力权重替换为均匀平均）、参数量匹配的对照（同图上的标准注意力、平坦二维格点上的同模型），并与同流程训练的ResNet-20比较。

Result: 消融显示两个角色不对称：移除传输使CIFAR-10/100测试精度下降约4个百分点（CIFAR-100各变体为单次运行），而将学习到的注意力权重替换为均匀平均几乎无变化。参数匹配对照中，同图标准注意力超过QSV（均值87.3% vs. 85.9%），平坦二维格点同模型达91.1%，与同流程ResNet-20相差2.1个点（单次运行）。在耦合核中，几乎全部学到的成对计算位于传输通道。

Conclusion: 四元数耦合核中"传输"承担了几乎所有学习到的成对计算，而"注意力"权重贡献甚微；然而去除几何先验后性能反而更好，说明QSV的优势并非来自球面几何或四元数表示，而是来自传输通道这一有效机制。

Abstract: In standard attention, three separately learned projections decide how strongly a token attends to each neighbor ($W_Q$, $W_K$) and how the attended features are transformed before aggregation ($W_V$). We study Quat-Sphere-Vision (QSV), a sparse spherical vision model that replaces this projection triple with a single learned unit quaternion per token: the relative quaternion $r_{ij} = q_i^{*} \otimes q_j$ supplies both the attention logit $\operatorname{Re}(r_{ij})$ and a sandwich-product feature transport $x \mapsto r_{ij} \otimes x \otimes r_{ij}^{*}$, with messages passed over sparse kNN graphs on concentric Fibonacci spheres. Ablations that change only the targeted component show the two roles to be asymmetric. Removing the transport reduces test accuracy by about four percentage points on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 (single runs per CIFAR-100 variant), while replacing the learned attention weights with uniform averaging leaves it essentially unchanged. Parameter-matched controls then remove the geometry itself: standard attention on the same graph exceeds QSV (mean $87.3\%$ vs. $85.9\%$), and the same model on a flat 2D lattice reaches $91.1\%$, within $2.1$ points of a ResNet-20 trained under the same pipeline (single run). In the coupled kernel, nearly all of the learned pairwise computation resides in the transport channel.

</details>


### [47] [Probabilistic Robustness-driven Universal Adversarial Perturbations with Explainability against Deep Reinforcement Learning-based Intrusion Detection System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30605)
*Hongsen Zhang,Lu Zhang,Mingjing Xu,Yi Zhang,Gregory Epiphaniou,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于概率鲁棒性（PR）的通用对抗扰动生成框架，并设计 PX-UAP，利用可解释AI引导扰动生成，在DRL入侵检测系统上攻击效果优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽能实现动态网络环境下的自适应入侵检测，但也引入通用对抗扰动（UAP）等对抗威胁；而概率鲁棒性（PR）作为群体级事后评估指标，与UAP的"通用性"目标契合，可作为引导UAP生成的天然信号。

Method: 首次将显式PR驱动目标引入针对DRL-based IDS的UAP生成，提出PR-based UAP；在此基础上提出PX-UAP，利用XAI在真实域约束下引导扰动整形，并给出严格的理论分析。

Result: 大量实验表明，PX-UAP在攻击有效性上持续优于当前最先进的UAP方法。

Conclusion: PR可作为UAP生成的原理性引导信号，XAI辅助的扰动整形能提升对抗攻击效果，揭示了DRL-based IDS在通用对抗扰动下的脆弱性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) enables adaptive intrusion detection in dynamic network environments but also exposes intrusion detection systems (IDS) to adversarial threats such as universal adversarial perturbations (UAPs), which apply a single input-agnostic perturbation to degrade detection performance across traffic. Probabilistic Robustness (PR), as a post-hoc evaluation metric, provides a principled, population-level measure of adversarial impact that conceptually aligns with the universality objective of UAPs, i.e., PR quantifies the prevalence of misclassification in the input space, making it a natural signal for guiding UAP generation. Hence, we propose PR-based UAP, which represents the first integration of an explicit PR-driven objective into generating UAPs against DRL-based IDS. Building on this formulation, we introduce PX-UAP, which leverages explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) to guide perturbation shaping under realistic domain constraints, and provides a rigorous theoretical analysis of its design. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PX-UAP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art UAP methods in attack effectiveness.

</details>


### [48] [OpenHail: An Event-Driven Gymnasium Environment for Electric Ride-Hailing Fleet Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30628)
*Tommaso Schettini,Nicholas D. Kullman,Jorge E. Mendoza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenHail，一个用于电动网约车车队联合控制的开源 Gymnasium 仿真环境，支持请求分配、调度与充电的统一策略控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习方法（尤其是强化学习）在网约车车队控制中应用日益广泛，但训练与评估需要结构化仿真环境，明确观测、动作、奖励与决策时刻。对电动车队而言，环境还需刻画随机需求、车辆运营与有限容量充电设施之间的交互，而现有环境难以满足这一需求。

Method: OpenHail 采用定长观测—动作接口，将请求分配、车辆调度（repositioning）与充电决策暴露给单一策略；其事件驱动仿真器建模了带取车截止时间的请求、车辆任务队列、电池动态以及具有先入先出队列的有限容量充电设施；并设计了可配置的决策时刻机制，将仿真器内部事件与策略交互分离，在同一运营模型下支持事件驱动、周期性、混合及策略请求式控制。

Result: 该软件提供带随机种子的实例、可行动作工具、评估工具、运营指标与基线策略，源代码已在 GitHub 开源（https://github.com/tommaso-schettini/openhail）。

Conclusion: OpenHail 为电动网约车车队的联合控制研究提供了标准化、可复现的开源仿真平台，便于强化学习等方法的训练与评估。

Abstract: Machine-learning policies have attracted increasing interest for ride-hailing fleet control in recent years. Reinforcement learning, in particular, requires a structured simulation environment that specifies observations, actions, rewards, and decision epochs for training and evaluation. For electric fleets, this environment must also capture the interaction among stochastic demand, vehicle operations, and capacitated charging infrastructure. We present OpenHail, an open-source Gymnasium environment for joint control of electric ride-hailing fleets. Its fixed-size observation--action interface exposes request assignment, repositioning, and charging to a single policy. The event-driven simulator represents requests with pickup deadlines, vehicle job queues, battery dynamics, and finite-capacity charging facilities with first-in--first-out queues. A configurable decision-epoch mechanism separates internal simulator events from policy interactions, supporting event-driven, periodic, hybrid, and policy-requested control within the same operational model. The software provides seeded instances, feasible-action utilities, evaluation tools, operational metrics, and baseline policies. The source code is available at https://github.com/tommaso-schettini/openhail.

</details>


### [49] [Stable initialization without the CLT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30633)
*Simon Kuang,Kyle Chickering,Xinfan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对正弦激活网络提出"均匀相位初始化"，通过利用正弦函数的周期性对称消除分布近似误差并实现层间完全解耦，在图像与音频拟合任务上优于现有方法，且无需调参即可与最佳调参基线竞争，并支持μP宽度缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络训练高度依赖初始权重分布：权重过大会导致训练发散，过小则无法学习特征，稳定初始化必须在这两个极端之间取得平衡。传统随机网络理论依赖中心极限定理控制神经元间依赖，这会引入分布近似误差并造成层间耦合，因此需要一种无近似、层间解耦的初始化方案。

Method: 针对使用正弦（sine）激活函数的网络，作者推导出"均匀相位初始化"（uniform-phase initialization）。该方法首次利用正弦函数的周期性对称性，从而规避分布近似（不再依赖中心极限定理的分布逼近），并实现网络各层之间的完全解耦。

Result: 采用均匀相位初始化的模型在神经表征类任务（如图像拟合与音频拟合）上超越了当前最先进水平；未调参的模型即可与以往工作中经过精细调参的最佳基线相竞争，并且该初始化支持 μP 宽度缩放。

Conclusion: 均匀相位初始化为正弦激活网络提供了一种免分布近似、层间解耦的稳定初始化方案，实验证明其在表征拟合任务上性能领先，且具备良好的宽度缩放性质，是有别于传统CLT式初始化理论的有效替代。

Abstract: Successful training of deep neural networks is highly dependent on the distribution of the initial weights. If the weights are too large, network training blows up; if they are too small, the model fails to learn features. Stable initialization is the optimal moderation between these two extremes. The conventional theory of random networks uses the Central Limit Theorem to control inter-neuron dependencies, which introduces distributional approximation error and coupling between layers. For networks with sine activations, we derive the uniform-phase initialization, which obviates distributional approximation and fully decouples the layers. Ours is the first work to use the sine function's periodic symmetry. Models trained with the uniform-phase initialization outperform the state of the art in neural representation tasks like image and audio fitting. We find that our untuned models are competitive with the best-tuned baselines from previous work and support $μ$P width scaling.

</details>


### [50] [In-Context Binding Capacity in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30634)
*Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli,Samrath Singh Chadha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型的实体-值绑定记忆容量上限：通过对12个≤3B模型绘制连续回忆曲线、并对30个≤12B开放模型做阈值扫描，发现半衰负载遵循幂律 $K_{50}=cN^\alpha$（$\alpha=0.820$，$R^2=0.73$），并系统分析了干扰、预训练配方、直接任务训练等因素对该容量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在需要将多个值绑定到对应实体时，其容量存在上限，但此前缺乏对"能记住多少条绑定后开始混淆"这一极限的定量刻画，也缺少区分规模、预训练配方、训练方式等因素影响的手段。作者希望用连续回忆曲线和阈值扫描两种互补测量，建立可复现的容量标度律，并把该测量与工作记忆、指令遵循联系起来。

Method: 1) 对12个参数量≤3B的模型测量连续回忆曲线，用曲线拟合确定回忆降至随机水平一半时的负载 $K_{50}$，并拟合幂律 $K_{50}=cN^\alpha$；2) 对30个≤12B的开放模型进行阈值扫描，考察预训练配方带来的差异；3) 从理论推导干扰如何通过降低单绑定回忆率而压低实测容量，即使负载依赖的回忆曲线形状不变；4) 比较零样本外推律与直接任务训练的表现；5) 在两个独立代码库中检验形成时间是否服从幂律（以成功运行的试验为条件）。

Result: 连续回忆曲线显示 $K_{50}=cN^\alpha$，$\alpha=0.820$、$R^2=0.73$；更广泛的扫描显示预训练配方带来约八倍的范围差异，但在控制规模后连续曲线未检测到配方效应，且拟合中现代模型较少。理论上说明干扰会压低实测容量。直接任务训练超过零样本外推律，但测量标准不同，不能据此解释为容量提升。形成时间在两个独立代码库中均服从幂律形式，但以成功运行试验为条件。

Conclusion: 这些结果刻画了模型查询接口处的容量特性。作者给出联合回忆的界与策略误差分解，用以把该测量与工作记忆、指令遵循联系起来，同时强调不应把回忆当作对齐的度量。该受控任务为检验绑定限制是否约束世界状态追踪提供了基线；但当前实验并未测量状态更新或下游迁移。

Abstract: How many assignments can a language model recall before it loses track of which value belongs to which entity? We measure this limit using continuous recall curves for 12 models at or below 3B parameters and a threshold sweep over 30 open models up to 12B. On the continuous curves, the load at which recall falls halfway to chance follows $K_{50}=cN^α$, with $α=0.820$ and $R^2=0.73$. The broader sweep shows an eightfold range associated with pretraining recipe, although the continuous curves show no detectable recipe effect after controlling for scale, with few modern models in the fit. We derive why interference can lower measured capacity by reducing single-binding recall even when the load-dependent recall profile is unchanged. Direct task training also exceeds the extrapolated zero-shot law, but different measurement criteria prevent interpreting that comparison as a capacity gain. Its formation times follow a power-law form in two independent codebases, conditional on runs that succeed. Together, these results characterize capacity at the model's query interface. Bounds on joint recall and a decomposition of policy errors connect this measurement to working memory and instruction following, without treating recall as a measure of alignment. The controlled task also provides a baseline for testing whether binding limits constrain world-state tracking; the present experiments do not measure state updates or downstream transfer.

</details>


### [51] [Causal Retention in Interactive Agents: Interface Factorization and Selective Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30650)
*Shengjun Zhang,Tingyi Liu,Dong Xie,Yunlong Dong,Xiang Wang,Cheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“因果保持”（causal retention）概念，研究冻结的学习状态能否稳定回答与训练无关的机制探测图，并给出贝叶斯风险刻画与实现方法 Causal Core。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务性能无法决定智能体保留哪种干预机制，因此需要区分“任务充分性”与“因果保持”：即便模型在源任务上表现良好，其冻结状态也未必能正确回答独立于训练固定的机制探测（如动作、上下文、直接目标、价值、延迟）。

Method: 将因果保持形式化为机制探测映射的问答问题；对有限结构因果模型类，把最优探测误差刻画为贝叶斯决策风险，证明其消失当且仅当每个学习接口纤维落在同一探测答案纤维内，且任何对该接口的后处理状态继承相同下界。给出后验覆盖定理刻画预算化重测，并通过精确编辑分解表明移位集合是无误差目标更新的唯一支撑。实现上提出 Causal Core，包含证据门控写入、读出过滤、时间信用、隐藏上下文建立与局部诊断更新。

Result: 实验覆盖有限因果系统、连续模拟器、官方 TD-MPC2 世界模型与 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct。冻结的 Qwen 末层探针在源机制上达到 0.958 平衡准确率，但在改变延迟时降至 0.583；门控机制状态达到 1.000，且仅接受 0.056 的同步读出候选。在 TD-MPC2 中，每个执行器五个目标状态将效应符号准确率从 0.057 恢复到 0.948，且不损害稳定响应。

Conclusion: 因果保持区别于任务充分性与源域可解码性；仅靠源任务性能或源域解码能力无法保证机制层面的因果保持，需要显式的证据门控与目标状态约束。

Abstract: Task performance need not determine which intervention mechanism an agent retains. We study causal retention: whether a frozen learned state answers a mechanism-probe map fixed independently of training, including action, context, direct target, value, and delay. For finite structural causal model classes, the optimal probe error is a Bayes decision risk. It vanishes exactly when every learning-interface fiber lies within one probe-answer fiber; any state obtained by post-processing that interface inherits the same lower bound. A posterior-coverage theorem characterizes budgeted retesting, while an exact edit decomposition shows that the shifted set is the unique support of an error-free target update. Causal Core implements these conditions through evidence-gated writing, readout filtering, temporal credit, hidden-context setup, and local diagnostic updates. Experiments cover finite causal systems, continuous simulators, an official TD-MPC2 world model, and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. A frozen Qwen last-layer probe reaches 0.958 balanced accuracy on source mechanisms but 0.583 on changed delays; the gated mechanism state reaches 1.000 and accepts only 0.056 of synchronized-readout candidates. In TD-MPC2, five target states per actuator recover effect-sign accuracy from 0.057 to 0.948 without degrading stable responses. Causal retention is therefore distinct from task sufficiency and source-domain decodability.

</details>


### [52] [Population loss in shallow ReLU networks: Bias & families of critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30661)
*Michael Field*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文给出了带偏置的浅层ReLU网络在师生核模型中的总体损失（population loss）公式，核心用到Owen's T函数，并证明加入偏置会严格降低损失，且既往的伪极小值族可推广到带偏置网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望在师生核模型下刻画带偏置浅层ReLU网络的总体损失，并推广以往仅针对无偏置网络的理论结果（Choo & Saul 2009、Brutzkus & Globerson 2017）。核心问题是：加入偏置后网络损失与损失景观几何如何变化。

Method: 推导了带偏置浅层ReLU网络师生核模型的总体损失公式，形式化地依赖Owen's T函数，并给出T函数所需理论；采用Komelj (2023)算法、基于MPFR的高精度T函数实现。数学工具除高斯统计外还包括解析几何（解析集与次解析集、曲线选择引理）。为控制篇幅，正文仅讨论输入数等于神经元数的最简情形。

Result: 给出了带偏置情形下的总体损失精确公式；证明既往文献中描述的若干伪极小值族可推广至带偏置网络；并证明加入偏置后损失总是严格下降，且由偏置引起的景观几何变化相对轻微。附带回顾了无偏置网络的相关先前结果。

Conclusion: 带偏置的浅层ReLU师生核模型具有可解析刻画的总体损失；偏置引入不显著恶化优化景观，反而严格降低损失。作者仅在最简设定下展开示例，更一般情形留待后续工作。

Abstract: The main result presented is a formula for the population loss in the student-teacher kernel model that is applicable to shallow ReLU networks with bias. This extends previous work of Choo and Saul (2009) and Brutzkus and Globerson (2017). The formula makes essential use of Owen's T-function. The necessary theory of the T-function is given and a high precision coding using MPFR for the T-function, based on an algorithm of Komelj (2023), is available on request. It is shown that various families of spurious minima described in past papers of Arjevani and the author extend to biased networks and that the loss is always strictly decreased when bias is added. The change in landscape geometry caused by adding bias appears to be relatively mild. Only the simplest examples are described in this paper where it is assumed that the number of inputs is equal to the number of neurons (this restriction is for reasons of length). A review of relevant previous results on unbiased networks is included. Aside from Gaussian statistics, the main mathematical tools and ideas come from analytic geometry (analytic and subanalytic sets, the Curve Selection Lemma).

</details>


### [53] [PixSim: a calibrated open-source simulator of instant-payment fraud, recovery and interdiction under analyst capacity constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30684)
*Bashir Zeimarani,Alireza Khatib,Somayeh Mousavinasr,Carlos Maurício Serodio Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PixSim：Pix即时支付轨道的开源仿真器，联合建模不可逆结算、监管追偿机制、资金下游分散与人力受限审核，并在基准平台上比较四类策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pix月均约59亿笔即时、不可逆转账，欺诈资金仅在停留于可追踪账户时才能追回；2025年央行MED机制仅返还受理争议金额的9%。因此干预必须在结算前完成，需在有限分析师团队与监管冻结窗口下将交易分流为放行、人工审核或阻断。作者称尚无公开仿真器同时建模不可逆结算、受监管追偿机制、下游资金分散与容量受限审核四要素。

Method: 构建PixSim开源仿真器，校准于巴西央行公开数据，每个参数均注明来源、由单一已公布观测值校准或登记为假设。模型冻结后进行全尺度运行验证，并在含付款方评分器、四个参考策略与十个场景的基准上开展实验，采用成对种子比较策略表现。

Result: 全尺度运行将2025年追偿率复现误差控制在0.006以内、其分解误差在0.02以内；2026年2—4月窗口报告为失配，2026年5月追踪制度作为外推。主要发现：结算后追回受资金分散速度制约；按到达分布配置人力可将固定规则的告警过期率从52%降至2%且工时不变；团队减半会消除固定"阈值加阻断"规则相对队列感知规则在损失及损失加误阻断危害上的优势（+0.106受害者价值，二十个成对种子上均为正），而在团队规模三分之二处的反转未能在独立种子上确认；留出AUC 0.82的合成评分器可将损失价值削减约四分之一。

Conclusion: PixSim为不可逆即时支付轨道的结算前干预与人力配置提供了可复现、参数可追溯的仿真基准；结果表明追偿上限受资金分散速度限制，人力调度与队列感知策略在资源受限下尤为关键，评分器精度提升可显著降低损失价值，同时部分阈值效应需独立种子进一步验证。

Abstract: Brazil's Pix settles about 5.9 billion instant, irreversible transfers a month. A fraudulent transfer can be recovered only while the funds remain in a traceable account, and in 2025 the Central Bank's recovery mechanism (MED) returned 9% of accepted contested value. Interdiction therefore has to happen before settlement, by routing each transaction to pass, human review or block, under a finite analyst team and a regulatory hold window. To our knowledge no public simulator jointly models irreversible settlement, a regulated recovery mechanism, downstream fund dispersal and capacity-constrained review. We present PixSim, an open-source simulator of the Pix rail with these elements, calibrated to Banco Central do Brasil open data, with every parameter sourced, calibrated to one published observable, or registered as an assumption. With the model frozen, full-scale runs reproduce the 2025 recovery rate within 0.006 and its decomposition within 0.02; the February-April 2026 window is reported as a misfit and the May 2026 tracing regime as a projection. On a benchmark with a payer-side scorer, four reference policies and ten scenarios, within the simulated mule model: recovery after settlement is constrained by dispersal speed; staffing by the arrival profile cuts a fixed rule's alert expiry from 52% to 2% at constant hours; halving the team removes a fixed threshold-and-block rule's advantage over a queue-aware rule, on loss and on loss plus false-block harm (+0.106 of victim value, positive on all twenty paired seeds), while a reversal at two thirds of the team was not confirmed on independent seeds; and a synthetic scorer of held-out AUC 0.82 cuts lost value by about a quarter. Code and data: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22948895

</details>


### [54] [LUMO (Lightweight Unified Multilingual Orchestrator): A Privacy Preserving Offline Voice Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30692)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan Naeem,Mst. Kamrunnahar Ruma,Nafiza Anjum,Shakila Sultana,Md. Sujan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUMO是一个完全离线的隐私保护语音助手，集成ASR、量化LLM与TTS，在树莓派5上实现6.8% WER、2.0-4.0秒端到端延迟与约9.0 W峰值功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音助手多依赖云端服务，导致延迟、网络依赖与隐私风险；而已有边缘方案（Mycroft、Rhasspy）或缺乏生成式能力，或延迟与功耗偏高，难以满足低资源与隐私敏感场景需求。

Method: 提出LUMO（轻量统一多语言编排器），在8 GB内存的树莓派5上构建全离线流水线，集成本地ASR、本地部署的量化LLM与TTS；采用4-bit GGUF量化压缩语言模型，在受限硬件上降低内存占用并保留实用对话能力。

Result: 短英文低噪条件下WER为6.8%，端到端响应延迟2.0-4.0秒，峰值功耗约9.0 W；并实现对孟加拉语的有效离线识别，支持多语言可及性。

Conclusion: LUMO通过完全离线运行强化数据隐私、降低云端连接需求，适用于农村医疗、教育与灾害响应等隐私敏感的边缘计算场景。

Abstract: Reliable voice interaction is essential in environments with limited internet connectivity and strong privacy. However, most existing voice assistants depend on cloud-based services, which leads to latency issues, dependency on internet access, and privacy vulnerabilities. This research presents LUMO (Lightweight Unified Multilingual Orchestrator), a privacy preserving offline voice assistant designed for edge computing environments. This system integrates local Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR), locally deployed quantized Large Language Model (LLM), and Text-to-Speech (TTS) synthesis into a fully offline pipeline running on a Raspberry Pi 5 with 8 GB RAM.
  To enable efficient operation on resource constrained hardware, the language model is compressed using 4-bit GGUF quantization, which reduces memory usage while preserving practical conversational capability. Existing edge based voice assistants Mycroft provides partial offline functionality without a generative LLM, with an approximate latency of ~5 s and power consumption of ~12 W, while Rhasspy supports full offline operation but lacks generative capabilities, with ~3 s latency and ~11 W power usage. In contrast, LUMO achieves a Word Error Rate (WER) of 6.8% for short English utterances in low noise conditions, an end-to-end response latency of 2.0-4.0 s, and a lower peak power consumption of approximately 9.0 W. The system also achieves effective offline recognition for Bangla speech, supporting multilingual accessibility in low resource settings. By operating entirely offline, LUMO provides strong data privacy, reduced need for cloud connectivity, and suitability for privacy sensitive edge execution such as rural healthcare, education, and disaster response scenarios.

</details>


### [55] [NEMSim: Learning Control-Conditioned Multi-Event Physical Dynamics via Executable Event-Mechanism Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30718)
*Junsong Yu,Junjie Xie,Pengwei Liu,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NEMSim，将事件-属性描述编译为可执行转移动力学结构以模拟受控多事件物理系统，并构建3D KMC基准；平均RMSE较最强基线降低58.9%-81.3%，在10%训练数据下仍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控多事件物理系统的高保真模拟在广阔控制空间与长轨迹上计算代价高昂。此类系统宏观演化源于局部离散事件，其强度与效应依赖过程控制和演化中的局部状态，而系统知识通常以事件-属性描述形式存在。纯数据驱动代理需从有限轨迹覆盖中推断事件效应，难以泛化到未见控制区间；物理引导方法则多基于方程级约束或可微求解器，缺乏离散事件规则先验。

Method: NEMSim（Neural Event-Mechanism Simulator）：将预定义的事件-属性描述编译为可执行转移结构，该结构连接控制依赖的事件强度、先验引导的机制归因与状态依赖响应。为评估具有显式系统知识的受控多事件动力学，构建基于3D KMC的基准，提供高保真轨迹、显式事件规则、标准化划分与评测协议。

Result: 在三种设置下，NEMSim相对各设置中最强基线将平均RMSE降低58.9%-81.3%；在训练数据比例低至10%的数据效率研究中仍保持最优。机制分析表明增益源于可执行规则整合，而非仅来自先验获取或网络架构。

Conclusion: 将离散事件-属性规则编译为可执行转移结构，可有效提升受控多事件物理系统代理模型在控制泛化与数据效率上的表现；基准与消融分析进一步验证了规则整合机制的独立贡献。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of control-conditioned multi-event physical systems is computationally expensive, especially across broad control spaces and long trajectories. In these systems, macroscopic evolution emerges from localized discrete events whose intensities and effects depend on process controls and evolving local states, while the available system knowledge is typically expressed as event-attribute descriptions. Purely data-driven surrogates must infer these event effects from limited trajectory coverage, which can hinder generalization to unseen control regimes. Physics-guided methods instead primarily build on equation-level constraints or differentiable solvers rather than discrete event-rule priors. We therefore propose NEMSim (Neural Event-Mechanism Simulator), which compiles predefined event-attribute descriptions into an executable transition structure linking control-dependent event intensities, prior-guided mechanism attribution, and state-dependent responses. To enable evaluation of control-conditioned multi-event dynamics with explicit system knowledge, we construct a 3D KMC-based benchmark pairing high-fidelity trajectories with explicit event rules, standardized splits, and evaluation protocols. Across three settings, NEMSim reduces Avg. RMSE by 58.9%-81.3% relative to the strongest baseline in each setting. It also remains best in the data-efficiency study with training-data fractions down to 10%. Mechanism analyses further show that these gains arise from executable rule integration rather than prior access or architecture alone.

</details>


### [56] [When 10,000 Windows Are Not 10,000 Tests: Auditing Statistical Confidence in Sliding-Window Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30721)
*Xinze Shi,Litian Zhang,Binrui Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对滑动窗口分类器的实用审计框架，将"已观测记录/已观测受试者的未来记录/未见受试者"三类泛化声明映射到明确的聚合规则与依赖稳健推断，并揭示重叠窗口带来的统计信息虚增与置信区间膨胀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动窗口分类器常在数千个重叠测试窗口上评估，相邻预测共享观测且嵌套于记录与受试者之中。受试者不交叉（subject-disjoint）评估仅能防止一种泄漏，并不能使测试窗口相互独立，因此现有评估会高估有效样本量与统计功效。

Method: 提出一套审计流程：将三类声明（观测记录、观测受试者的未来记录、未见受试者）对应到显式聚合规则与依赖稳健推断方法；采用受控模拟与"会话中心化 Bartlett-HAC"等稳健推断手段；对冻结的 WISDM 与 HARTH 预测结果进行审计；设置独立重算、共同会话检查、类别级结果及独立随机种子校准等可检验环节。

Result: 在 75% 重叠下，受控模拟显示 IID 观测记录推断的 Type-I 错误率为 16.9%，会话中心化 Bartlett-HAC 为 7.2%（显著改善但仍存在残余误校准）；WISDM 与 HARTH 审计显示测试行数增长近四倍仅带来 1.75–1.94 倍的方差等效信息增长；该重叠下固定记录配对 Accuracy 差异区间为 IID 宽度的 1.22–1.66 倍，而零重叠时该膨胀并不普遍；HARTH 上配对 Accuracy 差异区间在三种重叠设置下均包含零，而 Macro-F1 支持 MiniROCKET。

Conclusion: 该审计流程能够区分"额外预测"与"额外独立证据"，避免因滑动窗口重叠而虚增的统计显著性；独立重算与类级检查使审计的范围与局限可被检视。

Abstract: Sliding-window classifiers are often evaluated on thousands of overlapping test windows, even though neighboring predictions share observations and remain nested within recordings and subjects. Subject-disjoint evaluation prevents one form of leakage but does not make those test windows independent. We present a practical audit that maps three claims - performance on observed recordings, future recordings from observed subjects, and unseen subjects - to explicit aggregation rules and established dependence-robust inference. At 75% overlap, controlled simulations give 16.9% Type-I error for IID observed-record inference and 7.2% for session-centered Bartlett-HAC: a substantial improvement with residual miscalibration. Audits of frozen WISDM and HARTH predictions show that nearly fourfold growth in test rows yields only 1.75-1.94-fold variance-equivalent information growth. At that overlap, fixed-record paired Accuracy-difference intervals are 1.22-1.66 times the IID widths; this inflation is not universal at zero overlap. On HARTH, paired Accuracy-difference intervals include zero across three overlap settings, whereas Macro-F1 favors MiniROCKET. Independent recomputation, common-session checks, class-level results, and separately seeded calibration make the audit's scope and limitations inspectable. The resulting workflow distinguishes additional predictions from additional independent evidence.

</details>


### [57] [Mechanism-Aware Ensemble Conditioning for Data-Limited Emulation of Extreme Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30746)
*Isabella S. Thiel,Juan Bello-Rivas,Yannis G. Kevrekidis,Themistoklis P. Sapsis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出机制感知的条件化插件框架，将nudged粗集合转化为局部不稳定几何的非侵入式传感器，用FiLM模块注入统计量，在低维混沌与两层QG流中提升稀有事件模拟精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌系统中的极端事件由瞬态有限时间不稳定性控制，而非频繁观测的体动力学，因此短轨迹难以学习；需要一种能捕捉局部不稳定几何、且数据高效的条件化机制。

Method: 构建机制感知条件化插件：在小噪声区间，nudged粗集合的协方差聚合了支配局部不稳定性的有限时间形变核，提供同步粗轨迹周围局部放大结构的无Jacobian代理；用小型FiLM模块将该集合几何统计注入未改动的骨干网络，粗模拟器保持不变。在两条流水线验证：低维混沌系统的Transformer式残差注意力校正器，以及地形两层准地转（QG）流的概率循环STORN校正器。

Result: 低维基准中，集合协方差方向与OTD模态共激活，FiLM条件化相对无上下文Transformer基线改善了99百分位超阈频率误差；QG中，仅用50时间单位训练的固定集合条件化FiLM-STORN显著改善长时程稀有事件统计（密度尾部误差、超阈频率、空间超阈面积分布），其高阈值超阈诊断均值优于使用20倍高分辨率数据训练的基线STORN。

Conclusion: 局部不稳定几何不仅是事后可解释的，更是数据高效稀有事件模拟的可操作条件化信号。

Abstract: Extreme events in chaotic systems are difficult to learn from short trajectories because they are controlled by transient finite-time instability rather than by frequently observed bulk dynamics. We propose a mechanism-aware conditioning plug-in framework that turns a nudged coarse ensemble into a non-intrusive sensor of local instability geometry. In the small-noise regime, the ensemble covariance aggregates the same finite-time deformation kernels that govern local instability, providing a Jacobian-free proxy for the local amplification structure around a synchronized coarse trajectory. A small FiLM module injects statistics of this ensemble geometry into an otherwise unchanged backbone while leaving the coarse simulator unchanged. We demonstrate this interface in two distinct pipelines: a Transformer-style residual-attention corrector for a controlled low-dimensional chaotic system and a probabilistic recurrent STORN corrector for topographic two-layer quasi-geostrophic (QG) flow. In the low-dimensional benchmark, ensemble covariance directions co-activate with OTD modes and FiLM conditioning improves 99th-percentile exceedance-frequency errors over an identical no-context Transformer baseline. In QG, a fixed ensemble-conditioned FiLM-STORN model trained on only \(50\) time units substantially improves long-horizon rare-event statistics in the data-limited regime, including density-tail errors, exceedance frequencies, and spatial exceedance-area distributions relative to an unconditioned STORN trained on the same data; on averaged high-threshold exceedance diagnostics, it also outperforms the baseline STORN trained with $20$ times more high-resolution data. These results show that local instability geometry is not merely interpretable post hoc, but an actionable conditioning signal for data-efficient rare-event emulation.

</details>


### [58] [Differentiable RNA Secondary Structure Extraction for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30752)
*Tyler Illman,Max Ward,Marcell Szikszai,Ryan K. Krueger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了RNA二级结构预测中"结构提取"步骤与训练方法的一致性对性能的影响，并提出一种可微的对称双随机矩阵(SDSM)归一化算法，使模型可直接输出碱基配对概率矩阵而无需单独的提取步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习RNA二级结构预测方法通常输出权重矩阵W，需通过下游算法转换为二级结构或碱基配对概率矩阵，但这些转换（即结构提取）算法多为启发式且存在问题，且在文献中长期被忽视。作者希望分析训练方法与提取方法之间的一致性如何影响预测性能。

Method: 对比四种提取算法：类Nussinov动态规划、最大权重图匹配、SPOT-RNA和RiNALMo使用的贪心提取算法。在预训练RiNALMo模型及三个本文训练的玩具模型上评估：可微类Nussinov模型、二元交叉熵(BCE)基线，以及引入新型可微SDSM归一化算法的模型（可直接输出碱基配对概率矩阵，无需单独提取步骤）。该SDSM归一化可微，可在训练和评估时内联加入任意深度学习模型。

Result: 每种提取方法的性能强烈依赖于对应模型的训练方式。在玩具模型比较中，SDSM模型整体性能最强：在四种提取算法下均优于BCE基线，且其提取前的输出最接近真实标签。

Conclusion: SDSM归一化是传统结构提取的一种可行替代方案，训练与提取方法的一致性对RNA二级结构预测性能具有重要影响。

Abstract: Many deep learning approaches to RNA secondary structure prediction have recently been proposed. They typically output a weight matrix $W$ where $W_{ij}$ is an arbitrary weight for base $i$ pairing with base $j$. Converting this matrix to a predicted secondary structure or base-pairing probability matrix typically involves ad hoc and problematic downstream algorithms. Despite the importance of this conversion step, which we refer to as structure extraction, it has received relatively little attention in the literature. In this work, we analyze how the congruence between training and extraction methods affects prediction performance. To do this, we compare four extraction algorithms: a Nussinov-like dynamic programming method, maximum-weight graph matching and the greedy extraction algorithms used by SPOT-RNA and RiNALMo. These are evaluated on outputs from the pretrained RiNALMo model and three toy models trained in this paper: a differentiable Nussinov-like model, a binary cross-entropy (BCE) baseline, and a model that incorporates a novel symmetric doubly stochastic matrix (SDSM) normalization algorithm during training which allows it to output base-pairing probability matrices directly, without a separate extraction step. This SDSM normalization algorithm is differentiable and can be added inline to any deep learning model during training and evaluation. We find that the performance of each extraction method depends strongly on how the corresponding model was trained. Considering the toy models themselves, the SDSM model showed the strongest overall performance: it outperformed the BCE baseline under all four extraction algorithms and produced pre-extraction outputs closest to the ground truth. These results suggest that SDSM normalization is a tractable alternative to traditional structure extraction.

</details>


### [59] [Missingness-Aware Conformal Prediction Under Cross-Hospital Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30781)
*Liang You,Dongwen Ou,Hengyu Shi,Siyuan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出缺失感知的保形校准方法，用于跨医院死亡预测，在六个场景中多数降低最差组覆盖差距，但收益不随医院统一，合并评估可能误导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床测量仅对部分患者记录，且各医院记录率不同；缺失性定义的群体中，边际保形覆盖并不能保证组内覆盖。跨医院分布偏移下，死亡预测的组内公平覆盖缺乏保障。

Method: 提出缺失感知保形校准：在独立样本上选择一项测量，按该测量是否被记录对患者分组，在各组内应用Mondrian保形校准，避免复用校准结果。在eICU跨医院及单一MIMIC-IV医院跨护理单元上，用三个预测器评估；并与合并校准及按预测风险校准对比；用医院级分解解释差异，并构造总体分布进行分析。

Result: 相对合并校准，该方法在其选定分组上于全部六个场景降低平均最差组覆盖差距，中位降幅1.9个百分点，五个场景的配对站点自助区间不含零。收益不一致：按预测风险校准在更广缺失分组面板上差距更小；单独评估eICU各医院时，三个预测器收益均缩小，其中一个符号反转。分解表明合并通过组份额与覆盖误差的协方差重加权医院，并允许相反符号误差抵消：重加权解释反转，抵消解释另两个预测器的大部分衰减；构造分布显示无抽样噪声时两种评估也能给出相反排名。

Conclusion: 合并评估的改善不足以证明院内覆盖更好，即使校准分组固定；评估保形校准需区分合并与院内视角，避免因医院重加权和误差抵消产生误导性结论。

Abstract: Clinical measurements are recorded for some patients but not others, at rates that differ across hospitals, and marginal conformal coverage does not ensure coverage within groups defined by missingness. We propose a missingness-aware conformal calibration procedure for mortality prediction under cross-hospital distribution shift. It selects a measurement on an independent sample, groups patients by whether that measurement is recorded, and applies Mondrian calibration within each group, so no calibration outcome is reused. We evaluate the procedure across hospitals in eICU and across care units within one MIMIC-IV hospital, using three predictors. Relative to pooled calibration, it reduces the average worst-group coverage gap on its selected groups in all six settings, with a median reduction of 1.9 percentage points; paired site-bootstrap intervals exclude zero in five. These gains do not extend uniformly. Calibration by predicted risk achieves smaller gaps on a broader panel of missingness groups, and when eICU hospitals are evaluated separately, the gain shrinks for all three predictors and reverses in sign for one. We explain this discrepancy with a hospital-level decomposition. Pooling reweights hospitals through a covariance between group shares and coverage errors, and lets errors of opposite sign cancel: weighting explains the reversal, and cancellation accounts for most of the attenuation for the other two predictors. Constructed population distributions show that pooled and within-hospital evaluations can rank calibration methods oppositely even without sampling noise. Pooled improvement alone therefore cannot establish better coverage within hospitals, even when the calibration groups are fixed.

</details>


### [60] [Interpretable-by-Design Descriptor Portfolios Match a 2048-Dimensional Foundation Embedding on Low-Data Molecular Assays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30789)
*Yiqi Yao,Miquel Duran-Frigola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在低数据分子性质预测中，作者构建由可溯源、语义命名的紧凑描述符块组成的表征组合，在9个ADME/Tox数据集上达到与2048维CheMeleon嵌入相当的AUC（0.762 vs 0.764），但逐数据集差异未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子表征的选择在低数据场景下可能比预测器选择更关键，而表格基础模型放大了这一效应。作者希望验证：由紧凑、语义命名、可审计（每个维度都有模型名与训练来源记录）的描述符块组成的组合，能否在不牺牲特征级可解释性的前提下达到高维嵌入的精度。

Method: 以固定的11维物理化学基础描述符为起点，仅使用有标签的上下文信息，贪心地拼接经过来源筛选（provenance-screened）的描述符块，形成可审计的特征组合；在9个ADME/Tox测定、50个评估单元上评测，并限定在公共覆盖子集（所有表征均覆盖的分子）上计算指标；采用任务级bootstrap置信区间与预先声明的汇总/逐测定门槛进行判定，并报告4个被证伪的候选选择规则，以及冻结后的10种子与3个未见测定的检验。

Result: 组合表征平均测试AUC为0.762，CheMeleon为0.764，Mordred为0.756；与CheMeleon的汇总差距为+0.003 AUC（任务bootstrap 95% CI [-0.020, +0.030]），通过预先声明的汇总等效门槛但未通过逐测定门槛。25个上下文标签时仍满足汇总门槛，10个标签时不满足。同宽度随机块对照未能证明贪心成员选择本身带来精度提升。

Conclusion: 紧凑、可审计的分子表征在汇总层面可与高维黑箱嵌入竞争，但逐测定层面的差异仍未解决；贪心块选择本身的增益缺乏证据，需进一步研究。

Abstract: In low-data structure-activity prediction, the choice of molecular representation can matter more than the choice of predictor, and tabular foundation models sharpen that effect. We ask whether a portfolio of compact, semantically named descriptor blocks can reach the accuracy of a 2048-dimensional CheMeleon embedding while staying auditable at the feature level, meaning that every input dimension carries a model name and a recorded training provenance. Starting from a fixed 11-dimensional physicochemical base, we greedily concatenate provenance-screened blocks using the labelled context alone. Across nine ADME/Tox assays and 50 evaluation cells, scored on common-coverage subsets restricted to the molecules that every representation covers, the portfolio reaches a mean test AUC of 0.762, against 0.764 for CheMeleon and 0.756 for Mordred. The pooled gap to CheMeleon is +0.003 AUC (task-bootstrap 95% CI [-0.020, +0.030]), which satisfies our predeclared pooled parity gate but not the per-assay gate. At 25 context labels the headline rule again satisfies the pooled gate; at 10 labels it does not. We also report four predeclared candidate-selection rules that we falsified. Post-freeze checks over ten seeds and three previously unseen assays support pooled competitiveness for compact, auditable representations; a same-width random-bundle control does not establish that greedy membership itself adds accuracy. Assay-level differences remain unresolved.

</details>


### [61] [Towards Universal Representation-Based Process Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30790)
*Jinmyeong Choi,Taesup Kim,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将窗口级时间序列监控重塑为“基于参考分布的假设检验”，提出结合预训练时间序列编码器、核密度估计与保形校准的非参数框架，在学习到的表示空间内实现有限样本有效的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多时间序列过程学习与监控流程在局部窗口上运行，窗口级决策不可避免。经典统计检验虽可逐窗口应用，但通常评估预定义的参数化假设（如单位根或矩条件），当参考行为需从任务或领域数据中经验性地定义时灵活性受限。

Method: 将窗口级监控视为过程控制问题，重构为基于参考的假设检验：原假设由经验参考分布而非固定参数模型指定。具体通过一个基于表示的非参数框架实现，融合预训练时间序列编码器、核密度估计与保形校准，从而在学习表示空间中获得有限样本有效的推断。

Result: 实验表明，该方法对窗口级分布偏移具有敏感性，同时在稳定的参考机制下保持良好校准的推断。

Conclusion: 平稳性、循环平稳性等经典概念可作为该框架中经验参考集的自然实例；所提方法广泛适用于时间序列过程控制与监控任务。

Abstract: Many temporal process learning and monitoring pipelines operate in local windows, making window-level decisions unavoidable in practice. In such settings, classical statistical tests can be applied to individual windows, but they typically evaluate predefined parametric hypotheses-such as unit-root or moment-based conditions-thereby limiting flexibility when reference behavior is defined empirically from task- or domain-specific data. In this work, we view window-level monitoring as a process control problem and reformulate it as reference-based hypothesis testing, where the null hypothesis is specified by an empirical reference distribution rather than a fixed parametric model. We operationalize this perspective through a representation-based, nonparametric framework that combines pretrained time series encoders, kernel density estimation, and conformal calibration, yielding finite-sample valid inference in learned representation space. Classical notions such as stationarity and cyclostationarity arise as natural instantiations of empirical reference sets within this framework. Through experiments, we demonstrate sensitivity to window-level distributional deviations while maintaining well-calibrated inference under stable reference regimes, highlighting the applicability of the proposed approach to a broad class of time series process control and monitoring tasks.

</details>


### [62] [Counterfactual Online Conformal Prediction Under Adaptive Logging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30811)
*Xinyu Qiao,Yichen Lin,Kaihong Ji,Xue Wang,Tao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在线共形预测在"预测影响行动、行动决定校准数据"场景下的失效问题，提出倾向加权在线共形预测（PW-OCP）及其双重稳健变体（DR-OCP），以修正校准偏差并匹配信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自适应在线共形预测方法在决策反馈回路中可能仅保持边际覆盖率，却系统性地对"极少被选择的行动"的反事实结果产生错误覆盖。作者希望通过形式化反事实覆盖率这一失效模式，解决预测与行动相互影响时校准集存在选择性偏差的问题。

Method: 作者将失效形式化为反事实覆盖率问题，提出倾向加权在线共形预测（PW-OCP），采用逆倾向加权递归对校准进行去偏；并进一步提出双重稳健变体（DR-OCP），将冗余偏差降低至结果模型误差与倾向误差的乘积。在正性（positivity）假设下进行理论分析。

Result: 在正性假设下，所提方法的覆盖率可达信息论下界（至多相差对数因子）。在合成决策任务、公开赌博机数据以及金融再平衡实验上，PW-OCP 与 DR-OCP 均改善了反事实覆盖率并降低下游遗憾，同时未牺牲预测集锐度。

Conclusion: 通过逆倾向加权与双重稳健设计，可以有效纠正决策反馈回路中的在线共形预测偏差，实现接近最优的反事实覆盖率，并在多类实际任务中兼顾覆盖率、遗憾与预测集锐度。

Abstract: Online conformal prediction can fail when predictions shape actions and actions determine which outcomes enter calibration. Standard adaptive methods may retain marginal coverage while systematically miscovering the counterfactual outcomes of rarely selected actions. This paper formalizes the failure through counterfactual coverage and introduces Propensity-Weighted Online Conformal Prediction, an inverse-propensity-weighted recursion that debiases calibration. A doubly robust variant further reduces nuisance bias to the product of outcome-model and propensity errors. Under positivity, the resulting coverage rate matches an information-theoretic lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Experiments on synthetic decision tasks, open bandit data, and financial rebalancing show that PW-OCP and DR-OCP improve counterfactual coverage and downstream regret without sacrificing prediction-set sharpness.

</details>


### [63] [Learning Provable Neural Network Observer for Uncertain Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30819)
*Zhangyi Wang,Jiaxu Liu,Chen Song,Chao Xu,Shengze Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段训练框架，先点引导Lyapunov预训练后LMI微调，实现可证明稳定且可扩展的神经网络观测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键应用中，不确定动力系统的控制依赖观测器估计状态与外部扰动。神经网络观测器虽能提升估计精度，但用LMI约束证明其Lyapunov稳定性会形成大规模SDP，网络较大时难以求解，存在可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段为点引导Lyapunov预训练，在采样状态上快速获得高估计精度与局部稳定性；第二阶段为LMI微调，高效满足严格的全局Lyapunov稳定证书。并在给定正则性与采样假设下，对局部稳定半径及指定紧误差状态域上的概率覆盖给出形式化理论保证。

Result: 在非线性控制基准与X-29飞机消融实验中，LMI认证的神经网络观测器训练速度显著快于直接基于LMI的方法，能跨多种系统稳健泛化，并在多个观测器基线上取得更优跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 通过解耦优化为预训练与LMI微调，缓解了LMI认证神经观测器的可扩展性瓶颈，兼顾精度与全局稳定性证明，代码已开源。

Abstract: In many safety-critical applications, control of uncertain dynamical systems relies on observers that estimate states and external disturbances. Neural network observers can improve estimation accuracy, but certifying their Lyapunov stability via Linear Matrix Inequality (LMI) constraints leads to large-scale semidefinite programs (SDPs) that are difficult to solve for large networks. To overcome this scalability bottleneck, we propose a novel two-stage training framework for provably stable neural network observers. Our approach decouples the optimization into a point-guided Lyapunov pre-training phase, which rapidly achieves high estimation accuracy and local stability over sampled states, followed by an LMI fine-tuning phase that efficiently satisfies a strict global Lyapunov stability certificate. We provide formal theoretical guarantees for local stability radii and probabilistic coverage over a prescribed compact error-state domain under specified regularity and sampling assumptions. Experiments on nonlinear control benchmarks and X-29 aircraft ablations show that our LMI-certified neural network observers train significantly faster than direct LMI-based methods and generalize robustly across diverse systems, achieving improved tracking accuracy over a range of observer baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/Berry-Myon/LearningNeuralNetworkObserver.

</details>


### [64] [Quantizing Looped Transformers: Feedback Exposure and Calibration Blindness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30820)
*Nux Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环Transformer跨递归步复用权重，使低比特量化尤具吸引力。本文识别出标准训练后量化（PTQ）在循环模型上的两种失效模式：反馈暴露（feedback exposure）与校准盲区（calibration blindness），并提出跨递归步累积GPTQ Hessian的方法予以解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer权重在不同递归步之间共享，低比特量化收益显著，但标准PTQ在此类结构上的失效机理尚不清楚。作者希望厘清量化误差如何进入递归过程、以及校准应观测哪些状态这两个关键问题。

Method: 在Huginn-3.5B上进行逐通道INT4与分组INT4实验，定位误差来源；在线性滤波器与Mamba状态空间模型上做受控实验验证反馈暴露的普适性；对比RNN标准（RTN）、一步GPTQ与跨递归步累积Hessian的GPTQ，并在来自7种循环架构的9个checkpoint上评测。

Result: Huginn-3.5B上逐通道INT4主要在非残差的循环入口adapter处失效，而量化残差core损害较小；一步GPTQ因仅用step-0激活构建Hessian，导致后续递归步使用的输入方向权重不足，在9个checkpoint中有5个主任务指标不如RTN；累积Hessian的GPTQ在全部9个checkpoint上优于一步GPTQ与RTN，并在Huginn上恢复bf16级精度。

Conclusion: 循环模型PTQ需区分两个独立问题：量化误差从何处进入递归，以及校准能观测到哪些状态。跨递归步累积Hessian是同时应对两者的有效方案。

Abstract: Looped transformers reuse weights across recurrence steps, making low-bit quantization especially attractive. We identify two distinct failure modes of standard post-training quantization. On Huginn-3.5B, per-channel INT4 fails primarily at the non-residual loop-entry adapter, while quantizing the residual core is much less damaging. We call this feedback exposure: a quantized layer perturbs the recurrent state without an identity path, and the resulting error is fed back at later steps. Controlled experiments on linear filters and Mamba state-space models show that feedback exposure also occurs outside transformers. Grouped INT4 reveals a separate failure, calibration blindness: our one-step GPTQ baseline builds its Hessian from step-0 activations, leaving input directions used later in the recurrence nearly unweighted. Across nine checkpoints from seven looped architectures, one-step GPTQ is worse than round-to-nearest (RTN) on the primary task metric for five checkpoints. Accumulating the GPTQ Hessian across recurrence steps outperforms both one-step GPTQ and RTN on all nine checkpoints and recovers bf16-level accuracy on Huginn. These results separate two questions for PTQ on looped models: where quantization error enters the recurrence, and which states calibration sees.

</details>


### [65] [Adaptive Interaction Graphs for Particle Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30822)
*Aiden Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将粒子模拟中固定的交互图拓扑改为由不确定性驱动的自适应拓扑，实现 WaterDrop 上的严格帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型粒子模拟器（基于GNN）单步精度高，但长时程误差累积。现有方法用k近邻或静态半径规则固定交互图拓扑，忽略局部模型置信度，交互图这一变量尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出自适应图：一个逐粒子的方差头与加速度头在异方差高斯NLL损失下联合训练，使高不确定性粒子获得扩展邻域；利用上一步的不确定性估计，额外推理开销很小。

Result: 方差头学到有意义的不确定性概念——高方差粒子集中在复杂区域（如飞溅区、自由表面）。该信号驱动图拓扑后，AdaptGNS 在 WaterDrop 上取得严格帕累托改进，在 Sand 上有适度增益。

Conclusion: 自适应图在复杂度空间集中时最有用（基于 WaterDrop 上更强的表现提出该假设）；代码见 https://github.com/aidenzhou8/AdaptGNS。

Abstract: Learned particle simulators based on graph neural networks achieve strong one-step accuracy, but errors compound over long horizons. An underexplored variable is the interaction graph: existing methods fix its topology via k-nearest neighbors or a static radius rule, regardless of local model confidence. We propose making this graph adaptive: a per-particle variance head, trained jointly with the acceleration head under a heteroscedastic Gaussian NLL loss, drives a trajectory in which high-uncertainty particles receive an expanded neighborhood. This is done at little extra inference cost by using the previous step's uncertainty estimate. A key discovery is that the variance head learns a meaningful notion of uncertainty: high-variance particles concentrate near complex regions, such as splash zones or free surfaces. When this signal drives graph topology, the resulting AdaptGNS simulator achieves a strict Pareto improvement on WaterDrop and a modest gain on Sand. Given the model's stronger performance on WaterDrop, we hypothesize that adaptive graphs are most useful when complexity is concentrated in space. Our code can be found at https://github.com/aidenzhou8/AdaptGNS.

</details>


### [66] [MOPD-Router: Rethinking Teacher Routing in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30837)
*Tianze Xu,Yanzhao Zheng,Zhentao Zhang,Yuanqiang Yu,Chao Ma,Jihuai Zhu,Lelun Wu,Lyumanshan Ye,Pengfei Liu,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang,Gang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MOPD-Router框架，在token级别对全体教师池进行路由，无需域标签或独立路由模型；其中ExpertAlign指标按教师修正是否体现其专业化能力打分，在四种设置下均最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）通常将每个prompt在整段rollout中硬路由到域匹配的单一教师，依赖prompt级域标签，导致无法利用无标签数据混合，且浪费其他教师的互补监督信号。

Method: MOPD-Router：在每个token处对完整教师池分配监督，无需域标签、无需训练额外路由模型，提供插件式接口以支持不同度量来选择和加权各教师的OPD信号。接口内提出ExpertAlign，依据教师对当前token的修正是否表达其在后训练阶段获得的专业化能力进行打分，并与基于教师置信度（Entropy）和师生差异（Novelty）的两种参考度量对比。

Result: 在无标签与有域标签训练混合、强到弱与同规模蒸馏共四种设置中，ExpertAlign整体表现最强：无标签数据上较Mean聚合提升5.88（+12.3%）分；有域标签数据上在不使用域标签的情况下超过标准MOPD 3.95（+7.8%）分。

Conclusion: token级路由能够利用跨域互补监督，减少对prompt级域分配的排他性依赖。代码见 https://github.com/TURLEing/MOPD-Router。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates specialized capabilities into a single student, but existing practice typically hard-routes each prompt to a domain-matched teacher for the entire rollout. This dependence on prompt-level domain labels restricts using unlabeled training mixtures and leaves complementary signals from other teachers unused. We introduce MOPD-Router, a framework that routes supervision over the full teacher pool at each token, without domain labels or training a separate routing model. Its plug-in interface supports different metrics for selecting and weighting teacher-specific OPD signals. Within this interface, we propose ExpertAlign, which scores each teacher by whether its correction to the student at the current token expresses the specialization that teacher acquired during post-training, and compare it against two reference metrics built on teacher confidence (Entropy) and teacher-student discrepancy (Novelty). Experiments on unlabeled and domain-labeled training mixtures under strong-to-weak and same-size distillation scenarios show that ExpertAlign achieves the strongest overall performance in all four settings. On unlabeled data, it improves the overall score by 5.88 (+12.3%) points over Mean aggregation; on domain-labeled data, it outperforms standard MOPD by 3.95 (+7.8%) points without using available domain labels. These results demonstrate token-level routing can exploit cross-domain complementary supervision, and reduce exclusive reliance on prompt-level domain assignment. Code is available at: https://github.com/TURLEing/MOPD-Router.

</details>


### [67] [Peer-Grounded Counterfactual Path Planning for Chronic Health Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30838)
*Saman Khamesian,Hassan Ghasemzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出POROS框架，通过构建行为进展图将慢性病行为干预分解为同伴可及的增量步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性病管理需要一系列渐进的行为改变步骤，而现有反事实解释方法只给出目标状态、不提供路径、不保证健康单调改善、也不利用同伴证据，导致需一次性跨越巨大行为差距，患者难以采纳。

Method: 基于班杜拉自我效能理论与费斯廷格社会比较理论，构建行为进展图（观察患者状态上的有向无环图），每条边同时要求同伴行为邻近性与健康结局严格改善，边即为队列中个体已证明单周期可实现的行为改变，并通过最小代价路径分解行为差距。

Result: 在两个独立糖尿病纵向队列上评估：对TIR低于70%临床阈值的患者，单步所需平均增益从26.3个百分点降至5.5个百分点（一队列）及从31.1个百分点降至5.7个百分点（另一队列）；两队列中97-98%的多跳路径跨越患者边界，天然嵌入社会比较。

Conclusion: POROS将不可行的行为差距分解为增量、基于同伴的步骤，为慢性病行为干预提供可执行的渐进路径。

Abstract: Effective behavioral intervention in chronic disease management requires not a single prescription but a sequence of incremental steps, each grounded in what real, similar individuals have demonstrably achieved. Counterfactual explanation offers a natural computational route to such guidance, answering what change in behavior would have produced a better outcome. But existing methods return a target state without a route to it, guarantee no monotone health improvement along the way, and draw no evidence from peer behavior -- asking a patient to close a wide gap in one move, which is precisely the recommendation structure least likely to be attempted. We propose POROS (Peer-Grounded Optimal Routes Over States), a domain-agnostic framework rooted in Bandura's self-efficacy theory and Festinger's social comparison theory that constructs a Behavioral Progression Graph -- a directed acyclic graph over observed patient states in which every edge requires both peer-grounded behavioral proximity and strict health outcome improvement. Every edge is therefore a behavioral change that individuals in the cohort have demonstrated is achievable within a single period. Minimum-cost paths through this graph decompose otherwise inactionable behavioral gaps into incremental, peer-grounded steps. We evaluate POROS on two independent longitudinal cohorts of patients with diabetes. For patients below the 70% clinical threshold for time in range (TIR, blood glucose within 70-180 mg/dL), it reduces the mean gain required per step from 26.3 percentage points (pp) to 5.5 pp on one cohort and from 31.1 pp to 5.7 pp on the other, decomposing large behavioral jumps into the incremental steps that self-efficacy requires. Across both cohorts, 97-98% of multi-hop paths cross patient boundaries, embedding social comparison by construction.

</details>


### [68] [Attention-Based Adaptive Policies for Simultaneous Speech-to-Text Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30839)
*Filip Tăşădan,Ema Tomanová,Ondrej Lopuch,Paweł Bilko,Anders Søgaard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RFAP与DCAP两种策略，利用编码器-解码器交叉注意力机制确定语音帧与目标文本的最佳对齐，使离线训练的语音到文本翻译模型无需额外训练即可用于流式场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步语音到文本翻译（Simul-S2TT）需在处理音频帧时生成部分译文，其流式特性带来核心挑战：如何在保证翻译准确性的同时选择最佳翻译时机并最小化延迟。

Method: 利用编码器-解码器架构中的交叉注意力机制，寻找输入语音帧与目标文本标记之间的正确对齐关系，据此提出Recent Frame Attention Policy（RFAP）和Dual-Condition Attention Policy（DCAP）两种策略，使离线训练的语音到文本翻译模型无需额外训练即可应用于流式场景。

Result: 在CVSS-C语料库的三组不同语言翻译对上，RFAP超越其他策略，BLEU提升最高达4.0，同时将翻译延迟减少近1秒；DCAP在极低延迟下仍能保持较高的翻译质量。

Conclusion: 基于交叉注意力的RFAP和DCAP策略能够在不重新训练的前提下有效支持流式语音翻译，RFAP兼顾质量与延迟优化，DCAP则适用于极低延迟场景。

Abstract: Simultaneous speech-to-text translation (Simul-S2TT) consists of generating partial translations while the incoming audio frames are processed by the system. However, the streaming nature of this setup creates the challenge of deciding the best moment to perform an accurate translation while minimizing the delay. To address this challenge, we utilize the cross-attention mechanism of the encoder-decoder architecture to find the right alignment between the input speech frames and the target text tokens. In this paper, we propose the Recent Frame Attention Policy (RFAP) and the Dual-Condition Attention Policy (DCAP) that allow offline trained speech-to-text translation models to be used in streaming scenarios without requiring additional training. Results on three different language translation pairs over the CVSS-C corpus show that the RFAP is able to surpass other policies with gains of up to 4.0 BLEU while reducing the translation delay by almost 1 second. Moreover, the DCAP is able to preserve a high translation quality when the latency is very low.

</details>


### [69] [Aligning One-Step Generative Models with Reward-Weighted Transport Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30840)
*Austin Wang,Ziheng Cheng,Lexing Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RWTD（Reward-Weighted Transport Distillation）后训练方法，仅需生成样本与标量奖励，通过特征空间最优传输与不动点回归实现自适应目标分布，将一步生成器SANA Sprint 1.6B的GenEval分数从0.73提升至0.80。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
