Motivation: 一步生成器虽能单次网络评估实现高质量视觉生成，但其后训练困难：通用隐式生成器既无易处理似然也无去噪轨迹，且许多奖励不可微。

Method: RWTD仅依赖生成样本和标量奖励评估。它不局限于对齐传统奖励倾斜的参考分布，而是构建混合"当前分布倾斜分量"与"参考分布倾斜分量"的自适应目标：当前分量纳入训练中发现的改进，参考分量锚定预训练生成器。通过特征空间最优传输与不动点回归实现该目标。

Result: 理论分析表明RWTD的不动点分布在参考模型的离策略奖励倾斜与当前模型的在策略倾斜之间插值，实现奖励适配与先验知识保留的平衡。实验上，RWTD将一步SANA Sprint 1.6B的GenEval分数从0.73提升至0.80，偏好对齐实验显示强跨奖励泛化，带来均衡改进并保持组合能力。

Conclusion: RWTD为缺乏似然与去噪轨迹、奖励不可微的一步生成器提供了一种有原则的后训练框架，兼顾奖励适配与先验保留，并具备良好的跨奖励泛化性。

Abstract: One-step generators enable high-quality visual generation with a single network evaluation, but their post-training is difficult: general implicit generators provide neither tractable likelihoods nor denoising trajectories, and many rewards are non-differentiable. We introduce Reward-Weighted Transport Distillation (RWTD), a post-training method that requires only generated samples and scalar reward evaluations. Rather than aligning solely to the conventional reward-tilted reference distribution, RWTD constructs an adaptive target that mixes separately tilted current and reference distributions. The current component incorporates improvements discovered during training, while the reference component anchors the target to the pretrained generator. RWTD realizes this target through feature-space optimal transport and fixed-point regression. Theoretical analysis shows that the fixed-point distributions of RWTD interpolate between off-policy reward tilting of the reference and on-policy tilting of the current model, providing a principled approach to balancing reward adaptation with retention of prior knowledge. Empirically, RWTD substantially improves the GenEval score of the one-step SANA Sprint 1.6B backbone from 0.73 to 0.80, while separate preference alignment experiments demonstrate strong cross-reward generalization that yields balanced improvements and preservation of compositional capabilities.

</details>


### [70] [Learning Chance-Constrained MDPs with Bellman Distributional Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30856)
*Chenbei Lu,Hongyu Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对表格型折扣机会约束MDP（CCMDP），提出"Bellman分布证书"，证明其计算难度并不必然带来更高的统计代价，给出模型基上下界与无模型策略梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全强化学习通常采用期望代价约束，但期望安全无法控制稀有高代价轨迹发生的概率。机会约束MDP（CCMDP）可施加更强的概率级安全要求，然而普遍被认为更难：机会约束非凸，且依赖整条轨迹而非Bellman线性的期望。本文旨在探究这种计算难度是否必然意味着更高的统计代价（样本复杂度）。

Method: 1) 针对固定有界后继支撑、可访问认证规划oracle的表格型折扣CCMDP，提出核心概念"Bellman分布证书"：在策略选择之前为违反约束的概率构造Bellman递归，该证书可跨候选策略复用；2) 结合逐行反向KL共享置信集，得到策略一致（policy-uniform）的轨迹KL转移，避免对策略或时间-预算Bellman表的union bound；3) 对随机策略提出无模型、方差缩减的策略梯度算法，给出有限样本期望KKT残差保证，并对每个被接受策略进行独立验证。

Result: 在表格型折扣CCMDP下建立了模型基上界，并给出与之匹配（至对数项）的下界，说明机会约束的计算困难不必然导致更高的统计代价。合成CCMDP实验与IEEE 14节点储能控制基准实验验证了所提算法的安全性与机制行为。

Conclusion: 机会约束MDP虽然计算上更困难（非凸、轨迹依赖），但其统计代价并不比期望约束更高。Bellman分布证书为机会约束提供了可复用、策略一致的统计分析工具，配合方差缩减策略梯度，可在有限样本下保证KKT残差并确保策略安全性。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) commonly enforces expected-cost constraints, but such expectation safety may fail to control the probability of rare high-cost trajectories. Chance-constrained MDPs (CCMDPs) impose a stronger probability-level requirement, but are widely viewed as harder because the chance constraint is nonconvex and depends on the full trajectory rather than a Bellman-linear expectation. In this paper, we reveal that this computational difficulty does not necessarily imply a higher statistical price. For tabular discounted CCMDPs with fixed bounded successor support and access to a certified planning oracle, we establish a model-based upper bound, with a matching lower bound up to logarithmic terms. Technically, our key idea is the \emph{Bellman distributional certificate}, which constructs a Bellman recursion for constraint violation probabilities before policy selection. The certificate can be reused across candidate policies; combined with shared row-wise reverse-KL confidence sets, it gives a policy-uniform trajectory-KL transfer without a union bound over policies or time--budget Bellman tables. For stochastic policies, we give a model-free variance-reduced policy-gradient algorithm with a finite-sample expected KKT-residual guarantee and independent validation of every accepted policy. Numerical experiments on synthetic CCMDPs and an IEEE 14-bus energy storage control benchmark illustrate the safety and mechanism behavior of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [71] [CacheReforge: Bounded Recovery for Stale KV Caches under Evolving Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30884)
*Yuhang Cao,Yanzhou Mu,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对适配器迭代导致的陈旧KV缓存失真问题，提出CacheReforge，将陈旧缓存建模为逐层混合版本对象，区分依赖深度与功能重算边界，实现最小化重算，在保真度与缓存收益间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型依赖KV缓存降低长上下文与交互场景的重复预填充开销；轻量适配器（如LoRA）持续更新后，缓存状态反映旧版本，直接复用会扭曲当前模型输出，而完整重算受影响后缀代价高昂。现有系统仅跟踪token、上下文或稳定适配器标识，无法表示旧版本缓存，也未区分更新传播与行为恢复所需的重算，因此需要一种最小重算且能恢复当前适配器行为的方法。

Method: 提出CacheReforge，将陈旧KV缓存表示为逐层混合版本对象，结合逐层适配器锚点、校准敏感度、累积漂移与可执行重启边界，在直接复用、有界重算、完整受影响后缀恢复间进行选择；区分依赖深度与功能重算视界（recomputation horizon），并利用累积尾部影响刻画有界恢复保持当前模型行为的条件。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B与Qwen2.5-7B上以持续LoRA更新进行评测，涵盖16K的HotpotQA和2WikiMQA负载。相比陈旧复用，CacheReforge将平均KL散度降低92.4%，仅重算5.44%的层，相对全新完整预填充将缓存维护时间减少93.2%。

Conclusion: 版本感知的缓存恢复方法在显著保持模型保真度的同时保留了绝大部分KV缓存收益，表明最小化、边界感知的重算可有效解决适配器迭代带来的缓存失配问题。

Abstract: Large language models rely on KV caching to reduce repeated prefill computation in long context and interactive applications. As lightweight adapters evolve, cached states reflect earlier versions, so stale reuse distorts current model outputs, while complete affected suffix recomputation restores fidelity at substantial cost. We seek minimal recomputation that recovers current adapter behavior. Existing systems track token, context, or stable adapter identity, but neither represent caches from earlier adapter versions nor distinguish update propagation from the recomputation required for behavioral recovery. To address these gaps, we introduce CacheReforge, which represents stale KV caches as layerwise mixed-version objects. It combines per-layer adapter anchors, calibrated sensitivity, accumulated drift, and executable restart boundaries to select direct reuse, bounded recomputation, or complete affected-suffix recovery. We distinguish dependency depth from the functional recomputation horizon and use cumulative tail influence to characterize when bounded recovery preserves current-model behavior. We evaluate CacheReforge on Qwen2.5-1.5B and Qwen2.5-7B with continual LoRA updates, including 16K HotpotQA and 2WikiMQA workloads. CacheReforge reduces mean KL divergence by 92.4% relative to stale reuse, while recomputing only 5.44% of layers and reducing cache-maintenance time by 93.2% relative to fresh full prefill. These results show that version-aware recovery preserves model fidelity and most KV caching gains.

</details>


### [72] [Robust to Which Model Change? A Unified Evaluation of Robust Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30918)
*Marcin Kostrzewa,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出统一的跨族评估协议，将事实实例与已生成反事实固定，用八种模型变化测试六种鲁棒CFE方法与两种基线，揭示不同变化族下性能与失效模式差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒反事实解释承诺"模型改变后仍有效"，但该承诺取决于"改变"的类型——参数扰动、新数据重训练、新架构是不同事件，现有方法各自只面向其设计针对的那种变化。因此已报道的鲁棒性分数回答的是不同问题，彼此不可比。

Method: 提出统一的跨族评估协议：固定事实实例与已生成的反事实，对每种方法施加相同的八种模型变化进行测试；在四个表格数据集上比较六种鲁棒方法与两种标准基线；通过输出刻画每个变化后的分类器，并报告经验鲁棒性及覆盖度、基础有效性、接近度。

Result: 不同变化族下相对性能与失效模式各异。有界参数扰动平均仅改变0.95%的测试预测，而自助重训练为4.9%；针对参数扰动有保证的方法未必迁移到其他变化。RobX在实验中迁移最一致，但更高稳定性可能需要更大的干预幅度。

Conclusion: 鲁棒CFE方法应在统一协议下评估：明确指定模型变化、度量其实际行为幅度，并将生成性能与鲁棒性分离评价。

Abstract: Robust counterfactual explanations promise recourse that still works after the model behind it changes. Whether they keep that promise depends on what the change is. A small perturbation of the parameters, retraining on new data, and a new architecture are different events, and each existing method is evaluated against the one it was built for. Reported robustness scores, therefore, answer different questions and cannot be compared. We propose a unified cross-family evaluation protocol that holds factual instances and generated counterfactuals fixed while testing every method against the same eight types of model change. The benchmark compares six robust methods and two standard baselines on four tabular datasets. It characterizes every changed classifier through its outputs and reports empirical robustness together with coverage, base validity, and proximity. We find that relative performance and failure modes vary across change families. Bounded parameter perturbations change 0.95\% of test predictions on average, compared with 4.9\% for bootstrap retraining. Methods with guarantees for these perturbations do not necessarily transfer to other changes. RobX transfers most consistently in our experiments, although greater stability can require larger interventions. We argue that robust CFE methods should be evaluated through a common protocol that specifies the model changes, measures their realized behavioral magnitude, and keeps generation performance separate from robustness.

</details>


### [73] [EPOC: Endpoint-Preserving Online Correction With Compressed Residual State for Multi-Horizon Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30929)
*Takumi Fujimoto,Hiroaki Nishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EPOC方法，通过压缩残差状态（含低阶DCT系数与残差块端点值）对多步预测进行在线校正，在96个匹配实验条件下，MSE和MAE平均降低15.40%和9.35%，且辅助状态仅约6,352 B，显著小于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已完成的多步预测可为固定预测器提供残差反馈，但保留完整残差块会大幅增加辅助状态开销。因此需要一种既能有效利用残差信息进行在线校正、又能压缩状态存储的方法。

Method: 提出Endpoint-Preserving Online Correction（EPOC）：仅存储低阶DCT系数与前一残差块的最终值（端点）。在每个通道内，端点作为共享输入传递给分量级在线岭回归；回归同时使用当前预测的DCT系数拟合校正项，并将拟合的DCT校正与基础预测混合。

Result: 在DLinear与PatchTST、三个随机种子、两种训练变体、八个多变量序列、24步预测的96个匹配条件下，EPOC相对未校正基础模型平均降低MSE 15.40%、MAE 9.35%，辅助数组中位存储仅6,352 B；在多数条件下配对MSE低于δ-Adapter、COSA、FAC与OMPB，且状态更小。ELF平均MSE降幅最大（19.29%），但中位状态达474,048 B（约为EPOC的75倍）。等大小汇总对照显示端点在配对MSE上优1.65%–2.20%；系数重建端点与观测端点精度相近；DCT分量由4增至8可再增1.00个百分点MSE降幅，代价为5,728 B；联合训练基线下EPOC相对全局混合TEFL式适配器降低MSE 16.69%–20.15%。

Conclusion: EPOC以极小的压缩残差状态实现了显著且稳定的多步预测在线校正效果，在精度-状态权衡上优于多种现有适配/校正方法；端点作为共享输入发挥关键作用，增加DCT分量数可进一步提升精度。代码与数值记录已公开。

Abstract: Completed multi-horizon forecasts provide residual feedback for a fixed forecaster, but retaining full residual blocks increases auxiliary state. We propose Endpoint-Preserving Online Correction (EPOC) with a compressed residual state. It stores low-order discrete cosine transform (DCT) coefficients and the final value of the preceding residual block. Within each channel, the endpoint is shared across component-wise online ridge regressions that also use current-forecast coefficients. The fitted DCT correction is blended with the base forecast. We evaluate eight multivariate series with DLinear and PatchTST, three seeds, and two training variants, yielding 96 matched fixed-base conditions at a 24-step horizon. EPOC achieves mean condition-wise reductions in mean squared error (MSE) and mean absolute error (MAE) of 15.40% and 9.35% from the uncorrected base, respectively, with a median of 6,352 B in retained auxiliary arrays. It has lower paired MSE than the $δ$-Adapter, COSA, FAC, and OMPB in a majority of conditions and uses less state than each. Full ELF achieves the largest mean MSE reduction, 19.29%, but its median retained state is 474,048 B ($\times$75 relative to EPOC). Equal-size summary controls favor the endpoint by 1.65--2.20% in paired MSE; a coefficient-reconstructed endpoint yields similar accuracy to the observed endpoint, highlighting its role as a shared input. Increasing the retained DCT component count from 4 to 8 adds 1.00 percentage point of MSE reduction for 5,728 B. On jointly trained bases, EPOC lowers MSE by 16.69--20.15% relative to globally blended TEFL-style adapters applied to the same base. The code and numerical records are available at https://github.com/keiotakmin/endpoint-preserving-residual-correction.

</details>


### [74] [PORL: Pretrained Offline Reinforcement Learning for the Job Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30948)
*Mateo Toro Diz,Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PORL方法，将仿真环境下的在线预训练与生产数据上的离线微调结合，用于作业车间调度问题，在分布偏移场景下优于纯离线RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作业车间调度是工业优化的基础组合问题。在线RL依赖仿真环境且存在"仿真到现实"差距，离线RL虽不需交互但性能受数据质量与覆盖度影响严重。需结合两者优势，适用于难以在线探索的工业调度场景。

Method: PORL：先通过在线交互学习通用调度策略，再离线适配到目标分布；引入基于KL散度的策略约束，限制微调时相对预训练策略的偏离。在带分布偏移的JSSP实例上评估，数据集由启发式、噪声专家和随机行为策略生成。

Result: PORL的最优性差距（optimality gap）持续低于独立离线RL及所比较的通用调度基线；且数据集质量越低，其相对离线RL的优势越大，对离线数据质量与覆盖度的敏感度更低。

Conclusion: 对预训练策略进行离线适配，是直接在线探索不可行的工业调度环境中一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: The Job Shop Scheduling Problem (JSSP) is a fundamental combinatorial optimization problem in industrial optimization. This work introduces Pretrained Offline Reinforcement Learning (PORL), a hybrid approach that combines simulation-based online pretraining with offline fine-tuning on production-specific data.
  Reinforcement learning through online interaction enables exploration of general scheduling strategies, but typically relies on simulation environments and may suffer from a simulation-to-reality gap. In contrast, offline RL avoids direct interaction with the environment by learning from historical data, but its performance is strongly influenced by dataset quality and coverage. PORL combines the strengths of both paradigms by first learning a general scheduling policy through online interaction and subsequently adapting it offline to a target distribution. A KL-divergence-based policy constraint is introduced to limit deviations from the pretrained policy during fine-tuning.
  The approach is evaluated on JSSP instances with distribution shift and datasets generated from heuristic, noisy-expert, and random behavioral policies. The results show that PORL consistently achieves lower optimality gaps than standalone offline RL and the considered general scheduling baselines. Furthermore, its advantage over standalone offline RL increases as dataset quality decreases, indicating reduced sensitivity to the quality and coverage of the available offline data. The results suggest that offline adaptation of pretrained policies is a promising approach for industrial scheduling environments where direct online exploration is impractical.

</details>


### [75] [Low-Bit Recurrent States in Hybrid Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30950)
*Hongren Chen,Jiayang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需校准、旋转或训练的混合语言模型状态量化方法：由可观性Gramian推导失真权重并结合归一化状态范围进行混合精度比特分配，同时对衰减率做对数量化。每token状态量化下，4-bit平均负载将超额NLL较七个基线中最优者降低3.3--27.9倍；6-bit时NLL与FP32状态基线差距小于0.005 nats。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合语言模型保持固定大小的递归状态，但现有量化器通常使用8比特或更高精度，存在量化误差；且量化误差会随通道衰减率持续累积，因此需要更高效的比特分配方案。

Method: 从可观性Gramian推导失真权重，与归一化状态范围结合用于混合精度比特分配，无需校准数据、旋转或训练；同时对衰减率进行对数量化；采用每token状态量化。

Result: 4-bit平均负载下，超额负对数似然相对七个基线中最优者降低3.3--27.9倍（三个混合模型，元数据成本各异）；6-bit时NLL与FP32状态基线差异小于0.005 nats。消融实验分离了可变比特宽度、衰减加权与范围归一化的贡献；写回频率降低时收益减少且依赖模型与预算。

Conclusion: 基于可观性Gramian的失真加权与归一化范围可用于混合语言模型递归状态的混合精度量化，在低比特（4-bit）下显著降低超额NLL，6-bit时接近FP32基线；但收益受写回频率、模型与比特预算影响。

Abstract: Hybrid language models maintain fixed-size recurrent states, but existing quantizers typically use eight bits or more. Quantization errors persist according to channel decay rates. We derive distortion weights from the observability Gramian and combine them with normalized state ranges for mixed-precision bit allocation, without calibration data, rotation, or training. We also quantize decay rates logarithmically. With per-token state quantization, a four-bit mean payload reduces excess negative log-likelihood by factors of 3.3--27.9 relative to the best of seven baselines across three hybrid models; metadata costs vary. At six bits, negative log-likelihood differs from the FP32-state baseline by less than 0.005 nats. Ablations separate gains from variable bit widths, decay weighting, and range normalization. With less frequent write-backs, gains diminish and depend on the model and budget.

</details>


### [76] [Towards Understanding Momentum Acceleration in River-Valley Loss Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30957)
*Miao Lu,Zeyu Bian,Kaiyue Wen,Beining Wu,Siyu Chen,Tianhao Wang,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在"河谷"损失景观框架下研究动量在优化中的作用，理论证明动量通过稳定大学习率间接加速优化，而非直接加速沿河跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练大语言模型的经验成功促使研究者深入探究损失景观与优化动态。已有研究指出训练损失景观常呈现"河谷"结构：低损失流形（河）两侧被陡峭的正交高损失方向（山）所包围，长期优化进度主要由沿河的进展决定。在此景观下，大学习率梯度下降虽因垂直震荡而损失高，却能更快沿河前进，随后学习率的急剧衰减抑制震荡、揭示真实进展，这解释了warmup-stable-decay（WSD）调度器相较余弦调度的成功。本文在此基础上进一步研究动量在该景观中的作用。

Method: 建立理论分析框架，刻画动量如何通过稳定大学习率来加速优化：即动量使原本vanilla GD无法容忍的大学习率不至于显著偏离"河"，从而间接获得更快沿河速度。

Result: 理论证明了动量通过允许更大学习率来加速优化，该大学习率反过来提升沿河速度，带来更快的长期本质进展；并发现对于极平坦且缓慢旋转的河谷景观，动量本身并不直接贡献于沿河跟踪速度的加速，主要加速来源于可容许的更大学习率。

Conclusion: 动量在河谷景观中的加速作用主要是间接的——它通过稳定大学习率实现加速，而非直接加快沿河跟踪；对于极平坦缓慢旋转的河谷，加速几乎完全来自更大学习率本身。

Abstract: The empirical success of pretraining large language models has inspired a deeper investigation into the underlying loss landscapes and the optimization dynamics. Recent empirical and theoretical study suggest that the training loss landscape often exhibits a "river-valley" structure, which features a low-loss manifold (river) flanked by sharp orthogonal directions with higher loss (mountains). In the long term, the optimization progress is determined primarily by the progress along the river. Within such a landscape, gradient descent with large learning rates can move faster along the river despite high apparent loss due to vertical oscillations, while a subsequent sharp decay in the learning rate suppresses these oscillations, revealing genuine optimization progress. This explains the recent success of warmup-stable-decay (WSD) learning rate scheduler which, unlike cosine scheduling, keeps stable high learning rate and decays before producing intermediate checkpoints. Building on this foundation, in this work we take a step further and study the role of momentum within such a loss landscape. We establish theoretical analysis that characterizes how momentum accelerates optimization by stabilizing large learning rates that can not be tolerated by vanilla GD without deviating significantly from the river. The enabled large learning rate in-turn gives greater speed along the river and makes faster essential progress in the long run. Another intriguing observation from theory is that for a river-valley landscape with very flat and slow-spinning river, the momentum itself does not contribute directly to acceleration in terms of the speed of tracking the river, while the main acceleration comes from the admissible larger learning rate.

</details>


### [77] [Gradient Surgery for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30966)
*Thomas Borsani,Giuseppe Di Fatta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析PINN训练中的梯度冲突，发现三者训练阶段（角度冲突与幅度冲突交替、同一时刻仅一种存在），据此提出物理感知梯度手术方法PAM-GS，自适应缓解任务干扰，在四个PDE基准上实现竞争性精度与稳定任务平衡性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN通过优化数据拟合与物理约束的复合目标进行训练，构成高度不平衡的多任务优化问题；现有优化策略受冲突任务梯度影响，导致收敛慢、训练不稳定，尤其对刚性及高频偏微分方程问题尤为严重。

Method: 分析标准优化器训练PINN过程中的梯度冲突，并考察多任务深度学习（MTDL）优化方法；基于四个基准问题观察到PINN优化呈现三个不同阶段，角度型与幅度型梯度冲突交替出现且同一时刻仅有一种；据此提出PAM-GS——一种物理感知梯度手术方法，依据所观察到的冲突类型在训练中自适应缓解任务干扰。

Result: 在四个代表性PDE基准上实验表明，PAM-GS在多数问题上优于现有方法，兼具竞争性的求解精度与持续较强的任务平衡性能。

Conclusion: 梯度冲突在PINN训练中呈现阶段性、交替性特征，识别冲突类型并自适应施加梯度手术可有效缓解任务干扰，提升PINN在多任务目标下的优化稳定性与整体性能。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are trained by optimising a composite objective that combines data fitting with physics-based constraints, typically resulting in a highly imbalanced multi-task optimisation problem. Under these conditions, existing optimisation strategies are affected by conflicting task gradients, leading to slow convergence and unstable training, particularly for stiff and high-frequency partial differential equations. We analyse gradient conflicts throughout training of PINNs with standard optimiser and investigate Multi-Task Deep Learning (MTDL) optimisation methods. In our analysis across four benchmark problems we observed that PINN optimisation exhibits three distinct phases in which angle- and magnitude-based gradient conflicts alternate, with only one present at a time. Building on these observations, we propose PAM-GS, a physics-aware gradient surgery method that adaptively mitigates task interference during training according to the observed conflict types. Experiments on four representative PDE benchmarks demonstrate that PAM-GS combines competitive solution accuracy with consistently strong task-balanced performance, outperforming existing methods on most problems.

</details>


### [78] [LipSSM: Structurally Lipschitz-Bounded Cascaded State-Space Model via Metric Transfer between Consecutive SSM Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30973)
*Natsuki Yoshino,Ren Uchida,Kazuki Matsumoto,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LipSSM，将LipKernel的跨层信息传递思想推广到级联状态空间模型（SSM），以构建具有更紧Lipschitz上界、可建模长期依赖的Lipschitz连续深度神经网络，并给出理论证明与实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lipschitz连续性是可验证鲁棒DNN设计的核心原则，调节Lipschitz常数具有重要理论意义。传统逐层约束方法会得到过于保守的整体Lipschitz估计，导致网络表达能力受限、在给定鲁棒性水平下经验性能下降。近期提出的LipKernel通过跨层信息传递获得更紧的整体Lipschitz上界，但尚未扩展到能建模长期依赖的结构。

Method: 将LipKernel的跨层信息传递概念推广到级联状态空间模型（SSM），构建名为LipSSM的Lipschitz连续DNN架构，从而在保持整体Lipschitz连续性的同时获得更紧的上界并具备建模长期依赖的能力。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数据；作者声明对LipSSM进行了理论论证与实证评估。

Conclusion: LipSSM将LipKernel的紧Lipschitz界思想拓展至级联SSM，为构建兼具可验证鲁棒性与长期依赖建模能力的Lipschitz连续网络提供了新方案，并获得理论与实验支持。

Abstract: Lipschitz continuity is a fundamental principle in the design of certifiably robust deep neural networks (DNNs), wherein adjusting the Lipschitz constant, which quantifies network robustness, is of central theoretical importance. A standard approach to enforcing Lipschitz continuity requires each layer of a DNN to be Lipschitz continuous, thereby guaranteeing overall Lipschitz continuity. However, this layer-wise approach typically imposes overly conservative restrictions by producing a loose estimate of the overall Lipschitz constant, which limits the expressive capacity of the DNN and degrades empirical performance at a prescribed level of robustness. To overcome this loose estimation, the recently proposed LipKernel transfers information across layers to yield a much tighter overall Lipschitz bound than conventional layer-wise construction. In this paper, we extend this concept to cascaded state-space models (SSMs) to construct Lipschitz-continuous DNNs capable of modeling longer-term dependencies. The proposed architecture, named LipSSM, is theoretically justified and empirically evaluated.

</details>


### [79] [Does Uniform Discrete Diffusion Need Time?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30977)
*Chunsan Hong,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Satoshi Hayakawa,Yuhta Takida,Jong Chul Ye,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑均匀离散扩散模型（UDM）中显式时间条件的必要性：群体最优预测器虽依赖时间，但在有限数据（语言任务）设定下，经验最优预测器在大部分扩散轨迹上几乎对时间不敏感，且无时间条件的预测器表现可与甚至优于时间条件模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UDM通常默认引入显式时间条件，但作者发现这一做法在实践中可能并不必要，因此希望厘清时间条件在理论与实际中是否真正发挥作用。

Method: 从理论分析入手：先证明群体最优UDM预测器一般依赖时间（时间控制模型对观测上下文的信任程度）；再分析有限数据设定，指出当加噪后的训练序列仍明显更接近其原始干净序列而非其他训练序列时，经验最优预测器在扩散轨迹的大部分区间对时间几乎不敏感，仅在高噪声端该保证减弱；并辅以语言UDM的实验验证其时间敏感性。

Result: 训练得到的语言UDM在轨迹大部分区间时间敏感性有限；无时间条件的预测器在多个数据集和训练目标下与时间条件模型相当，且常常更优。

Conclusion: 尽管群体最优依赖于时间，但在实际语言任务中显式时间条件往往不必要，这挑战了UDM中普遍采用显式时间条件的做法。

Abstract: Uniform discrete diffusion models (UDMs) commonly use explicit time conditioning, but we find that it can often be unnecessary in practice. In this paper, we first show that the population-optimal UDM predictor generally depends on time: time controls how much the model should trust the observed context. We then show that this dependence can become negligible in finite-data settings relevant to language. When a corrupted training sequence remains much closer to its original clean sequence than to competing training sequences, the empirical-optimal predictor is nearly insensitive to time over most of the diffusion trajectory, where the guarantee weakens toward the high-noise endpoint. Empirically, trained language UDMs exhibit limited time sensitivity over most of the trajectory, while time-agnostic predictors remain competitive with, and often outperform, time-conditioned models across datasets and training objectives. These results challenge the use of explicit time conditioning in UDMs: although the population optimum depends on time, explicitly conditioning on it may often be unnecessary in practice.

</details>


### [80] [Learning Hierarchical Causal Representations of the Effects of Forcings on Temperature in Climate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30995)
*Shan Zhao,Ilija Trajkovic,Julia Kaltenborn,Yaniv Gurwicz,Peer Nowack,David Rolnick,Julien Boussard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层因果表征学习框架，用于从全球气候模式的海表温度场中学习可解释的因果表征，能同时建模内部气候变率驱动的大气动力相互作用与温室气体、气溶胶浓度变化导致的外强迫响应，在新情景下准确预测长期全球与区域温度演变并给出物理上合理的扰动响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习气候模拟器虽快速低成本，但其黑箱性质限制了输出的可用性与可信度，尤其难以作为因果归因工具；作者希望提升气候模式模拟的可解释性与因果推断能力。

Method: 提出一种分层因果表征学习框架，应用于最先进全球气候模式的海表温度场。相对于以往工作，该框架显式地同时建模两类过程：由内部气候变率引起的大气动力相互作用，以及由大气温室气体和气溶胶浓度变化引起的强迫响应。并在未来气候变化情景上训练。

Result: 在未见情景评估中，方法能准确预测长期全球平均及区域温度演变，并对温室气体与气溶胶浓度扰动表现出物理上真实的响应。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了因果表征学习框架在推进气候模式模拟方面的潜力。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) emulators provide a fast and cost-effective method to simulate climate change scenarios after being trained on Earth System Models projections. However, the black-box nature of those data-driven approaches limit the usability and trustworthiness of their outputs and in particular their use as causal attribution tools. Here, we develop a hierarchical causal representation learning framework applied to sea surface temperature fields from a state-of-the-art global climate model. As a key advance over previous work, our framework explicitly models both atmospheric dynamical interactions arising from internal climate variability and forced responses due to changes in atmospheric greenhouse gas and aerosol concentrations. When trained on future climate change scenarios, our method accurately predicts the long-term global mean and regional temperature evolution and shows physically realistic responses to perturbations in greenhouse gas and aerosol concentrations when evaluated on unseen scenarios. Our results underline the potential of causal representation learning frameworks for advancing climate model emulation.

</details>


### [81] [The Linear Representation Hypothesis for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30996)
*Minseok Jeong,Hyewon Choi,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将线性表示假设（LRH）推广到视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，提出基于签名（signature）的理论框架统一表示与策略：在表示侧证明可从表征线性探测物理量（QoI）的未来演化，在策略侧提出签名广义线性模型以支持线性操控，并在平面控制仿射导航实验中构造显式oracle表示验证预测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性表示假设已成为测量与干预大语言模型内部语义信息的标准工具，近期研究将其扩展到VLA模型。但具身交互的动态性带来新挑战：与LLM中常见的静态语义属性（如性别、语言）不同，VLA中关注的物理量（QoI）与系统动力学共同演化——表示影响策略选择的动作，动作改变物理状态，进而改变下一时刻的表示。这种表示-动作-状态的闭环耦合使经典LRH无法直接适用，因此需要建立统一表示与策略的新理论表述。

Method: 作者提出基于签名的LRH理论表述，统一表示与策略两个侧面。表示侧：证明存在一类表示，使得在候选动作轨迹下QoI的未来演化可通过线性探测（linear probing）恢复。策略侧：引入面向随机动作块（action chunks）的签名广义线性模型（signature generalized linear model），该结构使期望未来QoI沿自然参数空间中的线性路径单调变化，从而实现线性操控（linear steering）。随后在平面控制仿射导航实验中构造显式的oracle表示，对预测的线性探测与操控机制进行验证。

Result: 在平面控制仿射导航实验中构造了显式oracle表示，实验结果验证了理论预测的两项机制：一是可从表示中通过线性探测恢复QoI的未来演化；二是沿自然参数空间线性路径可实现期望未来QoI的单调变化与线性操控。

Conclusion: 本文为VLA模型中的线性表示假设提供了基于签名的理论形式化，将表示与策略纳入统一框架，并给出线性探测与线性操控的可证机制，通过平面导航实验中的oracle表示加以验证，为具身场景下的表示测量与干预奠定了理论与实验基础。

Abstract: The linear representation hypothesis (LRH) has become a standard lens for measuring and intervening on semantic information through the internal representations of large language models (LLMs). A growing body of work has begun extending this perspective to vision-language-action (VLA) models, but the dynamical nature of embodied interaction introduces an additional challenge. Unlike semantic attributes commonly studied in LLMs, such as gender or language, a physical quantity of interest (QoI) in a VLA evolves jointly with the system dynamics: the representation influences the actions selected by the policy, which alter the physical state and, in turn, the next representation.
  In this paper, we develop a theoretical, signature-based formulation of the LRH for VLA that unifies representations and policies. On the representation side, we establish the existence of representations from which the future evolution of a QoI under a candidate action trajectory can be recovered via linear probing. On the policy side, we introduce a signature generalized linear model for stochastic action chunks. This structure yields a monotonic change in the expected future QoI along linear paths in natural parameter space, enabling linear steering. We construct an explicit oracle representation in a planar control-affine navigation experiment and verify the predicted linear probing and steering mechanisms.

</details>


### [82] [Robust Successor Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31016)
*Erik Nikulski,Yamen Habib,Vicenç Gomez,Anders Jonsson,Rubén Moreno-Bote,Javier Segovia-Aguas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"鲁棒后继特征"（robust successor features），在奖励函数与转移核均发生变化的情形下实现强化学习的泛化，统一了后继特征迁移学习与鲁棒强化学习两大范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于后继表征（successor representation）及其函数近似扩展的迁移强化学习，通常仅能泛化到奖励函数不同、但动态（转移核）相同的任务。与此同时，运筹学领域独立发展出鲁棒强化学习，针对转移核未知的情形最大化期望奖励。两类范式长期割裂，缺乏统一框架，本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 本文引入鲁棒后继特征（robust successor features），在任务为线性马尔可夫决策过程（linear MDP）的假设下，使其能够同时跨奖励函数与转移核进行泛化。作者推导了广义策略改进（GPI）的误差界，显式刻画了性能随转移核失配程度而下降的规律，并证明当动态共享时可退化为已有的后继特征保证。

Result: 推导出的GPI界定量化了转移核不匹配对性能的影响，并在动态共享的退化工况下恢复经典后继特征的理论保证。随后在若干基于网格（grid-based）的基准环境中验证了鲁棒后继特征的泛化能力，并与仅关注奖励或仅关注转移核的既有方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 鲁棒后继特征为跨奖励与动态的强化学习泛化提供了统一的理论框架，扩展了后继特征迁移的适用范围，并在实证上优于仅处理单一变化来源的先前方案。

Abstract: Generalization in Reinforcement Learning (RL) refers to the ability to execute close-to-optimal policies in unseen tasks after the agent has been trained on a different set of tasks. Building on the seminal work of the successor representation and further adaptations with function approximation, Transfer in RL has traditionally focused on generalizing to tasks that only differ in the reward function. A decade after the introduction of the successor representation, Robust RL emerged simultaneously from several articles in the field of operations research. In Robust RL, the transition kernel is unknown, and the goal is to maximize the expected reward under this uncertainty. Our work unifies these two paradigms through robust successor features, which generalize across both the reward function and the transition kernel, under the assumption that tasks are linear Markov Decision Processes. We derive a bound on Generalized Policy Improvement (GPI) that explicitly quantifies how performance degrades with the mismatch between transition kernels, recovering existing successor-feature guarantees when dynamics are shared. Finally, the generalization capabilities of robust successor features are validated on several grid-based benchmarks and compared to previous alternatives that focus solely on either the reward or the transition kernel.

</details>


### [83] [Metacognitive Selective Ensemble for Mobile Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31031)
*Sungmin Lee,Kichang Lee,Joonhee Lee,JaeYeon Park,Songkuk Kim,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaSE主动集成框架，利用模型可靠性的短期持续性降低移动感知中深度集成的推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动感知中深度集成可提升鲁棒性，但连续传感器流上反复执行多模型开销大；仅选少数成员虽降本，但自适应选择通常需要额外执行模型才能获得关于非活跃候选的可靠证据。

Method: MetaSE在窗口间维护一个小规模活跃集，用执行后证据剔除不可靠成员，仅在需要替换时才触发轻量级路由；该有状态设计无需重复全池评估即可访问更大模型池的多样性。

Result: 在四个HAR数据集和四种模型架构上，MetaSE持续优于固定三模型集成，精度与更高开销的自适应推理及全集成推理相当；在Raspberry Pi 4B上，比十模型全集成推理快2.7倍且内存占用减少69%。

Conclusion: 通过利用逐模型可靠性的短期持续性，MetaSE以低开销实现接近全集成/昂贵自适应方法的精度，适合资源受限的移动感知场景。

Abstract: Deep ensembles improve robustness in mobile sensing, but repeatedly executing many models over continuous sensor streams is costly. Selecting only a few members reduces this cost, yet adaptive selection often requires additional model execution to obtain reliable evidence about inactive candidates. We present MetaSE, an active ensemble framework that exploits short-term persistence in per-model reliability. MetaSE maintains a small active set across windows, uses post-execution evidence to reject unreliable members, and invokes lightweight routing only when replacement is needed. This stateful design accesses the diversity of a larger pool without repeated full-pool evaluation. Across four HAR datasets and four model architectures, MetaSE consistently improves over a fixed three-model ensemble and achieves accuracy comparable to substantially more expensive adaptive and full-ensemble inference. On a Raspberry Pi 4B, MetaSE is 2.7x faster and uses 69% less memory than full ten-model inference.

</details>


### [84] [Robust Graph Clustering Network for Multiple Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31033)
*Keyuan Qiu,Renda Han,Zhen Tang,Qiang He,Xingwei Wang,Wenxin Zhang,Guangzhen Yao,Junxin Chen,Qingjian Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RGCN，一种面向节点属性与图结构同时缺失的鲁棒图聚类网络，通过视图解耦双分支补全、多超球混合先验与边界感知对比增强，在多类缺失模式下超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图聚类中节点属性与结构链接常同时部分缺失，现有方法多采用"先补全再聚类"的单视图缺失处理范式，易引发跨视图误差传播，并在属性与结构同时缺失时造成聚类边界模糊。因此需要一种能同时应对两类缺失、抑制误差传播并保持清晰簇边界的方法。

Method: RGCN包含三项核心设计：（1）视图解耦双分支补全，减少属性与结构两视图间干扰并实现互相增强；（2）多超球混合先验，在方向性隐流形上提升簇内紧致性与簇间可分性；（3）边界感知对比增强目标，缓解补全偏差导致的簇边界模糊。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，RGCN在多种缺失模式下一致优于当前最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: RGCN为属性与结构同时缺失的图聚类提供了有效的统一框架，验证了视图解耦补全与几何先验、边界对比约束相结合的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: Clustering on graphs where both node attributes and structural links are partially missing remains a challenging task. Existing methods typically rely on imputation-then-clustering on single-view missingness incomplete graphs, which are vulnerable to cross-view error propagation and cluster-boundary blurring under simultaneous attribute and structure missingness. To address these limitations, we propose a Robust Graph Clustering Network for Multiple Missing Data (RGCN), which is designed to handle simultaneous node attribute and graph structure incompleteness. RGCN introduces three key innovations: First, we design a view-decoupled dual-branch imputation to mitigate interference and enable mutual enhancement in recovering missing data. Second, we employ a multi-hyperspherical mixture prior to enhance intra-cluster compactness and inter-cluster separability on a directional latent manifold. Third, a boundary-aware contrastive enhancement objective mitigates the blurring of clusters caused by imputation bias. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that RGCN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines under various missing patterns.

</details>


### [85] [Aurora-X: Built for Extreme Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31038)
*Xingjian Wu,Chenjuan Guo,Xiangfei Qiu,Zhigang Hu,Hanyin Cheng,Peng Chen,Yang Shu,Jilin Hu,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Aurora-X 是十亿参数规模的时间序列基础模型（TSFM），通过渐进式课程训练与统一架构实现跨域预测，在 GIFT-Eval、TIME、FEV-Bench、TFB、DAG-Bench 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TSFM 虽支持跨域预测，但作为通用预测器仍受两方面的制约：训练潜力的挖掘不足，以及架构通用性有限。

Method: 采用渐进式课程：① 通道独立预训练学习时序模式；② 中期训练引入跨变量依赖、多变的上下文与预测长度、未来协变量；③ 变分辨率后训练，使推理时每个 token 可调时间跨度。架构上支持跨变量建模、协变量条件化与未来 patch 并行解码；提出模式引导的 MoE，通过稀疏激活扩展容量，并用浅层 patch 相似度约束深层路由以引导专家在异质时序上的专门化；另提出隐式分位数网络头，预测任意分位数以刻画预测分布。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval、TIME、FEV-Bench、TFB 与 DAG-Bench 的综合实验中，预测性能优于预训练 TSFM 与任务特定监督模型，达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: 渐进式课程与统一架构相结合，配合变分辨率机制带来的测试时扩展能力，可有效释放 TSFM 的训练与推理潜力，实现通用且高性能的时间序列预测。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) enable cross-domain forecasting, but their development as general-purpose forecasters remains constrained by underexplored training potential and limited architectural versatility. To address these challenges, we introduce Aurora-X, a billion-scale TSFM with a progressive curriculum and a unified architecture. We first use channel-independent pretraining to learn temporal patterns, then introduce cross-variable dependencies, varied context and horizon lengths, and future covariates if available during midtraining. Variable-resolution post-training further enables an adjustable temporal span per token at inference. With fixed model weights, this supports longer histories under a fixed token budget or fewer tokens for the same history, enabling test-time scaling. With a versatile architecture, Aurora-X supports cross-variable modeling, covariate conditioning, and parallel decoding of future patches for probabilistic forecasting. These are supported by a novel pattern-guided mixture-of-experts that expands model capacity through sparse activation and uses shallow patch similarities to constrain deep-layer routing, guiding expert specialization across heterogeneous time series. Furthermore, we propose an implicit quantile network head that predicts arbitrary quantiles to characterize predictive distributions, enhancing probabilistic forecasting flexibility. Comprehensive experiments on GIFT-Eval, TIME, FEV-Bench, TFB, and DAG-Bench demonstrate state-of-the-art forecasting performance against pretrained TSFMs and task-specific supervised models.

</details>


### [86] [Distributed Learning as a Service: The Developer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31061)
*Tianyue Chu,Filippo Vannella,Dimitra Tsigkari,Paula Delgado-Santos,Fernando López,Pablo Gomez Guerrero,Sotirios Spantideas,David Solans Noguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个名为 DLaaS 的分布式学习服务系统，开发者通过单一管理面板以声明式选项启用差分隐私、拆分学习、分层聚合与知识蒸馏，无需改动客户端代码。作者在工业级智能家居唤醒词任务（Ok Aura 数据集）上演示了完整服务生命周期，并开放源码与视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型联邦学习循环无法解决开发者面临的多项问题：模型更新仍可能泄露隐私数据、设备受资源限制无法参与训练、单一聚合器难以扩展、模型权重传输带宽开销巨大。

Method: 提出 DLaaS（Distributed Learning as a Service），从开发者视角出发，通过单一管理面板发起分布式/联邦学习任务，并以声明式方式激活四种机制：差分隐私（DP）、拆分学习（SL）、分层聚合（HA）和知识蒸馏（KD），且不修改客户端代码。系统将任务分发给一组 Android 客户端与 Docker 化的辅助聚合器，各机制在不同配置下实时运行；客户端本地训练模型并返回更新。

Result: 在工业级智能家居唤醒词（WuW）任务上使用 "Ok Aura" 数据集完成端到端演示：训练后模型部署到消费端 Android 应用，可在实时麦克风流上执行设备端唤醒词检测；会议参会者可现场说出触发词并实时监控各类别置信度与推理延迟。作者还发布了源代码与各配置的短视频演示。

Conclusion: DLaaS 验证了将 DP、SL、HA、KD 作为声明式选项集成到分布式学习服务中的可行性，实现了无需改动客户端代码的全生命周期部署与实时推理演示。

Abstract: Application developers of distributed learning services face challenges that a typical federated learning loop does not address. Specifically, the model updates can still leak private data, devices might not be able to participate in the training due to limited resources, a single aggregator might not be able to scale, and the transmissions of model weights induce a considerable bandwidth cost. This paper demonstrates DLaaS (Distributed Learning as a Service) from the developer's vantage point. Using a single admin dashboard, the developer initiates a distributed/federated learning job and is able to activate Differential Privacy (DP), Split Learning (SL), Hierarchical Aggregation (HA), and Knowledge Distillation (KD) as declarative options, with no change to the clients' code. We demonstrate the complete service lifecycle on an industrial smart-home Wake-up Word (WuW) task, using the "Ok Aura" dataset. Once the developer initiates a distributed learning job by toggling DP, SL, HA, and KD in the admin dashboard, the system dispatches the job to a set of Android clients and Dockerized helper aggregators. In the demonstration, these mechanisms run live across configurations. Then, the clients train the model locally and return their updates. The trained model is served to a consumer-side Android application that performs on-device WuW detection on a live microphone stream. In particular, the conference attendees will be invited to speak the trigger phrase and monitor in real time the per-class confidence and inference latency. Finally, we release the source code and short video walkthroughs of these configurations.

</details>


### [87] [SAGE: A sampling-aware global evaluation benchmark for species distribution modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31082)
*Emilia Arens,Nina van Tiel,Robin Zbinden,Damien Robert,Lukas Drees,Chiara Vanalli,Benjamin Kellenberger,Niklaus E. Zimmermann,Loïc Pellissier,Devis Tuia,Jan Dirk Wegner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE基准，利用GBIF训练数据与sPlotOpen验证数据，对5771种植物进行多物种SDM评估，发现随机森林与DeepSDM整体表现最佳，但DeepSDM仅对低频记录物种占优，且该优势依赖偏差校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物种分布模型（SDM）准确性受数据与模型影响，需明确哪些物种的模型可信。DeepSDM在数千物种、上亿条记录尺度上联合建模，平均性能掩盖了物种级差异，尤其对保护关注度高的稀有物种；且记录严重偏差使出现计数误导。因此需要可靠、信息量充分的评估框架。

Method: 提出采样感知全局评估（SAGE）基准：以GBIF记录作训练，sPlotOpen植被样方提供存在-缺失验证，覆盖5771种植物。按两个属性对物种分组——采样努力（分布区采样密度）与相对多度（记录频率）。对比单物种SDM与多物种DeepSDM，并纳入空间稀疏化、重加权等偏差校正实践。

Result: 随机森林与DeepSDM整体表现最佳，但无一方全面占优：DeepSDM对低频记录物种优于单物种SDM，对采样充分的物种则无稳定优势。该优势仅在偏差校正实践（空间稀疏化、重加权）迁移到深度学习设定时才出现。

Conclusion: SAGE可识别特定方法适用的物种与数据条件，有助于开发更透明、生态上更可信的SDM。数据和代码已公开。

Abstract: Knowing where species occur is fundamental for biodiversity research and conservation. Species distribution models (SDMs) link species observations to environmental conditions to estimate their spatial distribution. However, accuracy varies with the underlying data and models, making it essential to know for which species models can be trusted. Deep-learning-based SDMs ("DeepSDMs") now jointly model thousands of species, drawing on hundreds of millions of community-science records. At this scale, averaging performance hides substantial species-level variability, particularly for rare species, often of greatest conservation concern. Records are also strongly biased, making occurrence counts misleading. Accounting for these factors is essential for a reliable and informative evaluation of multi-species SDMs. Here, we introduce a Sampling-Aware Global Evaluation (SAGE) benchmark, combining GBIF records for training with sPlotOpen vegetation plots for presence-absence evaluation across 5771 plant species. We propose an evaluation framework that groups species based on two properties, sampling effort and relative prevalence, which describe how densely a species' range is sampled and how frequently the species is recorded. Evaluating single-species SDMs and multi-species DeepSDMs, we find that Random Forests and DeepSDMs perform best overall, but neither dominates: DeepSDMs outperform single-species SDMs for infrequently recorded species while offering no consistent advantage for well-sampled ones. Crucially, this advantage emerges only when established bias-correction practices, such as spatial thinning and reweighting, are carried over to the deep-learning setting. SAGE helps identify the species and data conditions for which a given approach is beneficial, thereby supporting the development of more transparent and ecologically credible SDMs. Data and code: https://earens.github.io/sage/

</details>


### [88] [Block Sparse Attention with Log-Linear Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31093)
*Bohao Tang,Zhen Qin,Yuqi Pan,Zheng Li,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PISA块稀疏注意力机制，通过金字塔Top-K选择策略将块选择复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N log N)，并开发Triton内核，在常识推理上性能相当、检索任务上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型受限于自注意力的二次复杂度。块稀疏注意力是高效替代方案，但传统块选择需对所有query-block对打分，序列长度上仍是二次复杂度，成为瓶颈。

Method: PISA采用金字塔Top-K选择：构建由粗到细的键层级（通过pooling得到O(log N)层），从最粗层开始，每层对有限候选集用LogSumExp打分并选出下一更细层的候选，直至最细层。整体复杂度O(N log N)。同时开发面向训练与推理的硬件感知Triton内核，融合层级路由与LogSumExp打分，不物化query-key打分矩阵。

Result: 在语言建模任务上评测：与基线相比，在常识推理等基准上性能相当，在检索任务上取得更好结果。

Conclusion: PISA通过金字塔式逐层筛选候选，实现了O(N log N)的块稀疏注意力，兼顾效率与效果，在检索类任务上优于基线。

Abstract: Scaling language models to long contexts is limited by the quadratic cost of self-attention. Block sparse attention offers an efficient alternative, but selecting the retained blocks remains a bottleneck. Conventional block selection requires scoring all query-block pairs and therefore remains quadratic in sequence length. To address this issue, we propose PISA, a block-sparse attention mechanism that employs a pyramid Top-$K$ selection strategy. The main idea is to gradually narrow down the candidates across different levels, making it more efficient to find the most relevant keys. Specifically, we construct a coarse-to-fine hierarchy of keys and perform selection from the coarsest level. At each level, LogSumExp scoring is applied to a bounded candidate set to select candidates for the next finer level, continuing until the finest level is reached. Through pooling, we construct $O(\log N)$ levels of keys, yielding an overall complexity of $O(N\log N)$, where $N$ denotes the sequence length. We develop hardware-aware Triton kernels for both training and inference, fusing hierarchical routing and LogSumExp scoring without materializing the query-key score matrix. We further evaluate our method on language modeling tasks. Compared with the baseline, our method achieves comparable performance on benchmarks such as commonsense reasoning while delivering better results on retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [89] [The Residual Stream's Effective Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31098)
*Barak Gahtan,Ido Galil,Alex M. Bronstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"有效深度"(Deff)：将Transformer逐层残差流视为离散时间过程，用表征相似度随层距的衰减曲线聚合成单一标量诊断指标。在16个decoder-only语言模型上，Deff揭示了残差更新相关性的校准特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏一个能刻画残差流累积结构、且可跨模型比较的简洁诊断量；同时"深度是否被有效利用"这一问题的判断容易受初始状态持续性、更新幅度不均、BOS与主成分等表象因素干扰，需要区分结构性必然与经验性现象。

Method: 定义有效深度Deff：把逐层残差流建模为离散时间过程，度量层间表征相似度随层距的衰减，并将该衰减剖面聚合为单一数值。构造全正交更新下的闭式参考上界F_L=2L/(L+1)<2；通过匹配参考实验排除初始状态持续与更新幅度失衡；采用位置0对称化、token归一化、top-PC移除等控制条件；考察中间checkpoint演化；并实施受控残差携带干预验证机制。

Result: 16个模型中15个默认测量值低于闭式参考F_L（Qwen3.5为32–44%，OLMo-2为40–41%，Pythia为23–28%）。匹配参考显示该差距并非由持续初始状态或更新规模失衡导致，而是残差更新相关性的校准特征，而非深度未被使用的证据。位置0、token归一化与top-PC控制表明该现象不能归结为BOS或主成分伪影：唯一高于参考的默认离群点归一化后归入同一regime，全部16个模型在token归一化或移除top-1-PC后均低于参考。中间checkpoint显示OLMo-2早期即确立该regime并在5T token内稳定，Pythia-1.4B则呈不同下降轨迹。

Conclusion: Deff刻画的是残差更新相关性的全局累积状态特征，受控残差携带干预支持其机制解释；应将其理解为全局累积状态诊断量，而非能力评分或剪枝方法。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{effective depth} ($\Deff$), a scalar diagnostic that treats the layer-wise residual stream of a transformer as a discrete-time process, measures how representation similarity decays with layer distance, and aggregates that profile into one number. Across sixteen decoder-only language models, $\Deff$ separates a structural consequence of residual accumulation from an empirical one: even maximally diverse orthogonal updates have the closed-form reference $F_L = 2L/(L+1)<2$, yet fifteen of sixteen default measurements lie below $F_L$ (Qwen3.5: 32--44\%, OLMo-2: 40--41\%, Pythia: 23--28\%). Matched references show that the gap is not caused by the persistent initial state or update-size imbalance, but is largely a calibrated signature of correlated residual updates rather than evidence that depth is unused. Symmetric position-0, token-normalisation, and top-PC controls show the regime is not reducible to BOS or top-PC artefacts: the lone above-reference default outlier joins the same regime, and all sixteen models are sub-reference after token-normalisation or top-1-PC removal. Intermediate checkpoints show that the regime is established early in OLMo-2 and stable through 5T tokens, while Pythia-1.4B follows a distinct decreasing trajectory. A controlled residual-carry intervention supports the mechanism, and $\Deff$ is best read as a \emph{global} accumulated-state diagnostic, not as a capability score or pruning method.

</details>


### [90] [Bayesian Optimization with Fisher Information Geometry: Gradient Bounds and Trust-Region Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31107)
*Saksham Kiroriwal,Julius Pfrommer,Jürgen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将贝叶斯优化（BO）置于信息几何视角下，通过代理后验映射回拉Fisher信息度量，得到输入空间上的局部敏感度张量，并据此提出基于信赖域与pullback-Fisher权重的FITR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释高维BO中可重参数化采集函数的梯度消失现象，并为RAASP、维度缩放长度尺度等启发式方法提供统一的理论解释，同时突破仅依赖显式长度尺度的GP核限制。

Method: 通过代理后验映射将Fisher信息度量回拉到输入空间，构造局部敏感度张量，导出采集函数梯度的上界；基于该分析提出FITR，用局部pullback-Fisher权重替代基于长度尺度的缩放，并采用信赖域策略进行优化。

Result: 在SE核GP基准上，FITR取得有竞争力的性能；方法可自然推广到非各向同性代理模型，但该情形下收益更依赖具体任务。

Conclusion: 信息几何视角为高维BO的梯度消失与相关启发式提供了统一解释，FITR作为一种不依赖显式长度尺度的信赖域BO方法，在标准基准上表现竞争性，并具备向非各向同性代理扩展的能力。

Abstract: We study Bayesian optimization (BO) through the lens of information geometry. Pulling back the Fisher information metric through the surrogate posterior map yields a local sensitivity tensor on the input space, which leads to an upper bound on the gradient of reparameterizable acquisition functions. This view explains vanishing-gradient behavior in high-dimensional BO and provides a common interpretation of heuristics such as RAASP and dimension-scaled lengthscales. Building on this analysis, we propose FITR, a trust-region-based BO method that replaces lengthscale-based scaling by local pullback-Fisher weights. FITR is not restricted to GP kernels with explicit lengthscales. On GP benchmarks with an SE kernel, experiments show competitive performance using FITR. The proposed method also easily generalizes to non-isotropic surrogates, although the gains are more task-dependent in that setting.

</details>


### [91] [From Shortcut Learning to Discrete Neural Insertion Sort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31114)
*Konstantinos Mylonas,Thrasyvoulos Spyropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以插入排序为案例，揭示CLRS30神经算法推理基线存在捷径学习与中间监督不足问题，并提出离散神经插入排序模型，在仅用长度16训练的情况下实现长度64和128的100%排序准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算法推理希望网络习得真实算法并可跨输入规模泛化，但正确输出与中间监督并不能保证模型遵循预期执行过程。作者希望检验模型是否真正执行算法，找出基线缺陷并据此设计更具归纳偏置的模型。

Method: 以插入排序为研究对象分析CLRS30基线NAR：发现hint目标优化较弱、hint准确率偏低，且参考执行尚未终止时中间表示已可解码为有序序列，表明存在通向最终输出的捷径。据此提出Discrete Neural Insertion Sort：将序列表示为链，分离标量交换与控制状态转移，并在每步处理器后把节点表示投影回离散状态；另做消融验证。

Result: 仅在长度16序列上训练时，模型在长度64与128上达到100%有序序列准确率。消融显示，仅有离散化和图结构并不充分：若缺乏对全局内循环状态的额外监督，模型在训练长度上也会失败。

Conclusion: 离散执行可支持强长度泛化，但要学习忠实于算法的执行过程，仍需针对具体问题引入恰当的归纳偏置，尤其是对全局循环状态的有效监督。

Abstract: Neural algorithmic reasoning aims to train neural networks to follow known algorithms and generalize beyond the input sizes seen during training. However, correct final outputs and intermediate supervision do not necessarily show that a model follows the intended execution. We study this problem using insertion sort. Our analysis of the CLRS30 baseline NAR shows that the hint objective is weakly optimized and that hint accuracy remains low. Moreover, many intermediate representations can already be decoded into sorted sequences before the reference insertion-sort execution terminates, suggesting that the model learns a shortcut to the final output. Motivated by these findings, we introduce Discrete Neural Insertion Sort. Our model represents the sequence as a chain, separates scalar exchanges from control-state transitions, and projects node representations back to discrete states after every processor step. When trained only on sequences of length 16, the model achieves $100\%$ sorted-sequence accuracy on sequences of length 64 and 128. However, an ablation shows that discretization and graph structure alone are insufficient: without additional supervision of the global inner-loop state, the model fails even at the training length. Our results show that discrete execution can support strong length generalization, while also highlighting the problem-specific inductive bias required to learn a faithful algorithmic execution.

</details>


### [92] [Frame the adversary: a structure-aware attack methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31128)
*Vicky Kouni,Stelios Perrakis,Francis Bach,Pascal Frossard,Yann Chevaleyre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于优化框架的频域对抗攻击方法，通过引入与高度结构化非正交变换相关的扰动约束集，证明攻击等价于该集上的加权ℓ2投影，从而给出清晰的几何刻画，并在标准数据集及鲁棒模型上验证其高效性和跨架构迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频域对抗攻击能利用跨神经架构共享的频谱敏感性，揭示比空间扰动更深的脆弱性，对安全关键应用的价值评估尤为重要。然而现有方法通常并非作为显式捕捉变换域结构的优化问题的解导出，缺乏原理性。

Method: 提出一个专用优化框架来构造有原则的频域对抗攻击。核心是引入与高度结构化非正交变换（以灵活、非预定义频率处理著称）相联系的扰动约束集，并证明攻击是该集合上的加权ℓ2投影，从而得到通用且可控的攻击生成机制与清晰几何刻画。

Result: 在标准化数据集上，针对预训练模型和对抗鲁棒模型进行评估，结果表明该攻击作为结构化扰动集上优化问题的解，效果显著，甚至能跨不同且未见过的架构保持有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为设计与分析基于变换的攻击的理论基线，针对模型的基本脆弱性，而非鲁棒性文献中常见的特定架构伪影。

Abstract: Frequency-based adversarial attacks have recently grown popular by exploiting spectral sensitivities shared across neural architectures. Unlike spatial perturbations, frequency-based attacks expose deeper vulnerabilities, making them especially valuable for robust evaluation of safety-critical and security-sensitive applications. Yet, existing approaches are typically not derived as solutions to an optimization problem that explicitly captures transform-domain structure. In this paper, we propose a methodology for crafting principled frequency-based adversarial attacks, via a dedicated optimization framework. A cornerstone of our method hinges on the introduction of a perturbation constraint set, tied to highly structured non-orthogonal transforms, well-known for their flexible, non-predefined frequency handling. We prove that the attacks emerge as weighted $\ell_2$-projections onto this set, yielding a general and controlled attack generation mechanism. By this, we provide a clear geometric attack characterization, ensuring alignment between the optimization objective and the perturbation constraint. We assess our framework on standardized datasets, for pretrained and adversarially robust models. Results highlight that our attacks, being solutions to an optimization problem, over a structured perturbation set, are highly effective, even across different, unseen architectures. Our methodology could serve as a theoretical baseline for designing and analyzing transformed-based attacks, targeting fundamental model vulnerabilities, instead of mere architecture-specific artifacts typically studied in the robustness literature.

</details>


### [93] [CRNDiff: Count-Native Diffusion Framework via Chemical Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31149)
*Yuxuan Qiu,Praful Gagrani,Tetsuya J Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRNDiff框架，将计数原生马氏跳过程（基于化学主方程/生灭过程）与扩散模型结合，实现非负整数计数数据的结构化生成，并支持推理时对稀有亚群的条件采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scRNA测序等科学测量本质是非负整数计数，而连续状态扩散模型用连续坐标近似这种离散结构，存在建模不匹配问题；同时，在冻结生成器上无需重训练地对稀有亚群进行条件采样也很困难，稀有目标会导致重要性权重集中。

Method: 构建于随机化学主方程（CRN）这一类计数原生马氏跳过程之上。采用独立的生灭（birth-death）实例，得到前向加噪的闭式转移核；该核支持通过前向滤波-后向采样（FFBS）进行反向采样，并支持数据驱动地选择终止加噪时间，无需验证集扫描。进一步提出倾斜Feynman-Kac（FK）引导：在FK粒子校正前对后验边缘进行倾斜，缓解稀有目标下的重要性权重集中，从而从冻结生成器中采样目标亚群而不重新训练。

Result: 在人类心脏细胞图谱scRNA-seq数据上测试细胞类型特异分布的生成能力。在三个评估目标群体上，CRNDiff取得最高条件保真度，且目标越稀有平均纯度优势越大；生成细胞保留标记水平差异表达结构；用生成细胞替换目标类别的真实训练细胞后，下游分类性能接近真实数据参考水平。

Conclusion: 计数原生扩散建模与推理时条件采样相结合，可有效生成稀有亚群并保持生物学结构，为离散计数数据提供优于连续扩散的生成方案。

Abstract: Scientific measurements such as single-cell RNA (scRNA) sequencing often take the form of nonnegative integer counts, whereas continuous-state diffusion models approximate this discrete structure using continuous coordinates. Building on stochastic chemical reaction networks (CRNs), a class of count-native Markov jump processes, we introduce CRNDiff, a structured framework that combines count-space diffusion with inference-time conditioning on rare subpopulations. An independent birth--death instantiation yields a closed-form transition kernel for forward noising. This kernel enables reverse sampling via forward-filtering backward-sampling (FFBS) and supports data-driven selection of the terminal noising time, eliminating the need for a validation sweep. This tractability also lets us introduce tilted Feynman--Kac (FK) steering, a method for sampling target subpopulations from a frozen generator without retraining. By tilting posterior marginals before FK particle correction, steering mitigates importance-weight concentration when the target population is rare. Using scRNA-seq data from the human heart cell atlas, we test the ability of CRNDiff to generate cell-type-specific distributions. Across the three evaluated target populations, CRNDiff achieves the highest conditional fidelity among the evaluated generative models, with larger mean purity margins for rarer target populations. Generated cells preserve marker-level differential-expression structure. Replacing real training cells for the target classes with generated cells yields downstream classification performance approaching that of the real-data reference.

</details>


### [94] [Teacher-Anchored Selection of Post-Training Quantized Models under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31155)
*Alejandro Rodriguez Dominguez,Muhammad Shahzad,Xia Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在域偏移且目标标签缺失/稀缺条件下，如何从压缩模型家族中选择部署候选，提出以教师相对锚点为基准的选择方法，并在134个候选家族上验证其在极小标签预算下降低平均遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后压缩会产出一系列部署候选，域偏移下最激进的压缩未必最优，需要在候选与教师固定、目标标签缺失或稀缺时进行选择。现有无标签估计器在过自信崩溃情境下排序失效，因此需要更稳健的选择准则。

Method: 研究无标签情形下最小教师失真与现有估计器的行为差异；给出该家族二次类比下的精确二次恒等式；证明在对称损坏下，若系数和与候选无关，则线性于标签指示的准则中标签相关部分被同一因子缩放；并以教师相对锚点进行选择。

Result: 最小教师失真近似常数规则，总是选中八位、逐通道、不裁剪配置，且不最小化经验目标交叉熵；过自信崩溃下置信度估计器排序近乎反向，输出分布估计器与教师相对锚点一致并在某架构上更优；134个候选家族中，锚定法在每个设置的最小标签预算下降低平均遗憾，但优势在超过25个标签后消失。

Conclusion: 教师相对锚定适合标签极少时的压缩候选选择，但该刻画未给出选择遗憾界，且优势随标签增加而衰减。

Abstract: Compressing a trained model yields a family of deployment candidates, and under domain shift the most compressed one need not be the one to deploy. We study selection over such a family, with candidates and teacher fixed and target labels absent or scarce. Two findings organize the label-free case. Minimum teacher distortion behaves almost as a constant rule, selecting the same eight-bit, per-channel, unclipped configuration in every run, which does not minimize empirical target cross-entropy. Established estimators divide sharply: in the overconfident-collapse regime of the CNN families, confidence-based estimators order the family close to backwards, and the diagnostics that identify it need the labels the setting denies, while output-distribution estimators match the teacher-relative anchor and on one architecture beat it. Distortion is nonetheless stable, so a supervised term can move selection away from it. Combining the two, we give exact quadratic identities for a canonical quadratic analogue of the family. We also show that under symmetric corruption the label-dependent part of a criterion linear in the label indicator is multiplied by one common factor whenever its coefficient sums are candidate-invariant, a class holding teacher contrasts and accuracy but not cross-entropy. These characterize the score's components without bounding selection regret. Across one hundred and thirty-four candidate families, one per independently trained convolutional or Vision Transformer teacher, anchoring reduces mean regret at the smallest label budget in every setting, an advantage that fades beyond twenty-five labels.

</details>


### [95] [Bayesian Tensor Autoencoder with Physics-informed Predictive Prior for Multi-dimensional Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31157)
*Jianan Liu,Chunguang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PPPTAE框架，将预测先验通过贝叶斯融合引入张量自编码器的重构分支，并嵌入张量低秩分解的物理规律，用于多维时间序列异常检测，避免重塑破坏内在相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维时间序列本质为张量，但现有异常检测方法多面向单/多变量序列，处理时需重塑，破坏内在相关性并导致性能下降；同时重构型与预测型自编码器各用不同信息（当前观测 vs 历史信息），尚未充分融合利用。

Method: 以张量自编码器为骨干以避免破坏多维序列内在相关性；设计预测先验并将其融入重构型自编码器，提出贝叶斯融合方法（分析表明可增强模型对正常数据的建模能力）；在预测先验的神经网络中引入物理规律，即张量低秩分解规则，缓解AE的过泛化问题。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明所提方法有效。

Conclusion: 通过贝叶斯融合将预测先验引入重构型张量自编码器，并结合张量低秩分解的物理约束，可在保持张量相关性结构的同时融合历史与当前信息，提升多维时间序列异常检测性能。

Abstract: Multi-dimensional time series, inherently tensorial, are common in practice. Despite great progress in time series anomaly detection, most existing methods are confined to uni-/multi-variate time series. When handling multi-dimensional time series using these methods, reshaping operations are required, which inevitably break the intrinsic correlations and thus lead to performance degradation. In uni-/multi-variate time series anomaly detection, AutoEncoders (AEs) are widely adopted and generally categorized into reconstruction-based and prediction-based AEs. The reconstruction-based AE utilizes the current observation for reconstruction, while the prediction-based AE utilizes the historical information to predict the current observation. Thus, the two AEs utilize different information. To bridge the gap between reconstruction-based and prediction-based AEs, so as to fully leverage the available information and thus further enhance performance, we propose a predictive prior and incorporate it into the reconstruction-based AE. It may not be very difficult to conceive this idea, but designing the predictive prior so that it can work for tensor anomaly detection is non-trivial. Specifically, to avoid breaking the intrinsic correlations within the multi-dimensional time series, we use the tensor AE as the backbone. To incorporate the predictive prior into the reconstruction-based AE, we propose a Bayesian fusion approach and our analysis reveals that this approach can enhance the modeling capability of the model for normal data. To mitigate the over-generalization problem of AE, we incorporate physical laws, i.e. tensor low-rank decomposition rules, into the neural networks in the predictive prior, leading to the Physics-informed Predictive Prior Tensor AE (PPPTAE) framework. Experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [96] [I Act Therefore I Am: When Is JEPA's Action-Conditioning Enough to Learn Causal Mechanisms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31161)
*Yuhang Liu,Zhuo Huang,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究JEPA类世界模型何时能恢复潜在因果状态，提出信息论目标与可识别性条件，并实例化为A-JEPA，实验验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA可能学到动作条件下预测未来的有用表征，成为世界模型基础，但准确预测不必然意味着恢复产生观测动态的潜在因果状态，需明确其可识别性条件。

Method: 引入潜在变量模型：高维观测由潜在因果状态生成，其动态由动作条件转移机制决定；构建结合条件似然最大化（学转移动态）与熵最大化（保留潜在状态信息）的通用信息论目标；推导可识别性条件，使表征可恢复到逐分量可逆变换与置换；以动作调制高斯加性噪声模型实例化，得到A-JEPA。

Result: 合成环境实验验证可识别性条件下理论结论及对适度违反的鲁棒性；视觉基准显示状态恢复改善，并可迁移到未见转移机制。

Conclusion: JEPA在满足如充分动作诱导变化等条件下可恢复潜在因果状态，A-JEPA为有原则的世界模型表征学习提供方案。

Abstract: Recent empirical and theoretical advances suggest that joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) may learn meaningful representations for action-conditioned prediction of future outcomes, thus becoming one of the foundational structures for world models. However, accurate prediction does not, in general, necessarily imply recovery of underlying causal states that give rise to the observed dynamics. This work investigates when and how JEPAs can recover the underlying causal states from observations. We first introduce a latent variable model, in which high-dimensional observations are generated from latent causal states whose dynamics are governed by action-conditioned transition mechanisms. Based on this formulation, we develop a general information-theoretic objective that combines conditional likelihood maximization for learning transition dynamics with entropy maximization for preserving latent state information. We then establish identifiability conditions under which representations learned by this general objective recover the underlying latent causal states up to component-wise invertible transformations and permutation. One key condition for such identifiability is sufficient action-induced variation in the transition mechanisms. Guided by this finding, we instantiate the general objective with an action-modulated Gaussian additive-noise model, yielding action-modulated JEPA (A-JEPA). Experiments on synthetic environments verify the theoretical findings under the identifiability conditions and robustness to moderate violations, while visual benchmarks demonstrate improved state recovery and transfer to unseen transition mechanisms.

</details>


### [97] [WorldTS: World Modeling for Multimodal Covariate-aware Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31162)
*Yuhan Zhu,Xiangfei Qiu,Hanyin Cheng,Wangmeng Shen,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang,Jilin Hu,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldTS，一种基于世界建模的时间序列预测框架，将多模态协变量直接融入潜在状态的形成与演化，通过两阶段训练实现更优预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列预测直接学习观测空间中从历史到未来的映射，但观测序列仅提供系统动态的部分视角，未来观测由潜在动态及外部因素共同决定。现有潜在空间预测方法虽有所改进，但如何将外部（常为多模态的）信息融入预测，使其直接影响潜在状态的形成与演化，仍研究不足。

Method: WorldTS采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段，以多模态协变量为条件，学习与预测相关的潜在状态动态，得到编码的未来状态；第二阶段，冻结已学得的动态，训练观测解码器，将预测的未来状态映射回未来观测。

Result: 在21个真实世界数据集上进行的大量实验，验证了WorldTS的有效性并提供了对其机制的洞察。

Conclusion: 将多模态协变量直接整合进基于世界建模的潜在空间预测框架，能有效提升时间序列预测性能。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is typically framed as learning a direct mapping from historical to future observations in the observation space. However, sequences of observations generally provide only a partial view of the dynamics of the underlying system, with future observations being shaped by latent dynamics. Recent latent-space forecasting methods thus achieve improved performance by predicting future observations from latent-space representations of historical observations rather than directly forecasting future observations in the observation space. Next, while future observations are also shaped by external factors, how to incorporate external, often multimodal, information into forecasting, so that it can shape latent-state formation and evolution directly, remains underexplored. We propose WorldTS, a world-modeling based forecasting framework that integrates multimodal covariates directly into the forecasting to further improve forecasting performance. Specifically, WorldTS employs a two-stage training strategy. First, it learns forecasting-relevant latent state dynamics conditioned on multimodal covariates, yielding encoded future states. Next, the learned state dynamics are frozen, and an observation decoder is trained to map the predicted future states back to future observations. Extensive experiments on 21 real-world datasets offer insight into WorldTS and its effectiveness.

</details>


### [98] [Audio emotion recognition for atypical hearing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31168)
*Ulysse Roussel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 博士研究探索非典型听觉情境下的音频情绪识别（AER），聚焦自闭症患者的听觉过敏现象；核心思路是利用声学特征理解情感，并以少量标注数据泛化情感反应；初步做法是基于神经典型听者的效价-唤醒数据集，用LoRA微调CLAP大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自闭症人群的听觉过敏难以评估且高度个体化，现有情感标注数据稀缺，需要一种能从少量标注样本中泛化情感反应的AER方法。

Method: 以声学特征情感建模为基础，采用低秩适配（LoRA）对大型基础模型CLAP进行微调，训练数据为神经典型听者的效价（valence）与唤醒（arousal）数据集。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验结果，仅说明第一步已完成基于LoRA的CLAP微调方案设计。

Conclusion: 初步验证了利用基础模型加参数高效微调来开展非典型听觉AER研究的可行性，后续将面向自闭症听觉过敏的个体化情感反应评估。

Abstract: My doctoral work aims to explore Audio Emotion Recognition (AER) in the context of atypical listening. This research focuses on auditory hypersensitivity in people with autism, a phenomenon that is often difficult to evaluate and unique to each individual. Our core idea is to leverage our understanding of affect from acoustic traits, relying on the possibility of generalizing affective responses from a small amount of annotated data. As a first step, we fine-tune a large foundation model, Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) using low-rank adaptation (LoRA), trained on a valence and arousal dataset of neurotypical listeners.

</details>


### [99] [ALF: An Active Learning Framework for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31197)
*Shikha Surana,Alex Hawkins-Hooker,Olivia Gallup,Christoph Brunken,Jules Tilly,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ALF，一个模块化的主动学习框架，通过五个可组合组件覆盖完整的数据采集闭环，并统一支持离线基准测试与在线部署两种使用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现中的机器学习受限于数据，在预算约束下生成高质量数据是推动该领域发展的关键。主动学习（AL）在需要昂贵实验、测量或仿真的标注场景中很有价值，但现有工具大多只覆盖数据采集流程的一部分，且通常要么专注离线基准测试，要么专注在线部署，难以兼顾二者。

Method: ALF将完整的数据采集闭环拆解为五个模块化组件，并提供统一的API以支持两种运行模式：离线模式基于已有数据集，用于受控且可复现的实验；在线模式基于oracle，用于在真实部署中获取新候选样本。框架为开源实现。

Result: ALF实现了离线与在线两种设置下的完整数据采集闭环，具备模块化设计和统一API，并作为开源项目发布（https://github.com/instadeepai/alf）。

Conclusion: ALF为主动学习提供了一个兼顾可复现实验与真实部署的模块化开源框架，弥补了现有工具仅覆盖部分采集流程、且离线与在线割裂的不足。

Abstract: Machine learning for scientific discovery is almost systematically data bound. Producing relevant high quality data, under budget constraints, is amongst the most promising ways to advance the field. Active learning (AL) offers promise wherever labelling requires expensive experiment, measurement, or simulation. Most existing tools cover only part of the data acquisition loop, and typically focus on either offline benchmarking or online deployment, but not both. We present ALF, a modular AL Framework that runs the full data acquisition loop via five modular components. One clear API for both settings: offline, against an existing dataset for controlled and reproducible experimentation; and online, against an oracle for acquiring new candidates in real-world deployments. ALF is open-source and available at https://github.com/instadeepai/alf.

</details>


### [100] [Self-Supervised Representation Learning: From Spectral Foundation Models to Auroral Emission Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31206)
*Matthieu Le Lain,Gaël Cessateur,Sébastien Lefèvre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用掩码自编码器在22.3万条无标签极光光谱上预训练1D Vision Transformer，学到的表征可恢复物理诊断所需的谱线强度比，微调后在分类基准上超越既有监督模型，且优于两种跨领域预训练基础模型的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASIS等极光光谱仪产出数十万条光谱，但专家仅能标注数百条，大量无标签数据未被利用；同时需要验证通用/天文预训练基础模型能否直接替代领域内预训练。

Method: 对223,000条无标签极光光谱，用掩码自编码器（masked autoencoder）预训练1D Vision Transformer；随后进行线性探针与微调评估，并与专家设计的13个特征、既有监督极光分类器比较；再迁移两个天文光谱基础模型（红外的SpectraFM、光学的SpecFormer）和一个时间序列模型作为对照，并用归因分析考察模型利用的谱带。

Result: 无标签表征恢复的发射线强度比与物理量关系达R^2=0.91（未训练对照0.77）；单线性探针分类性能与13个专家特征相当。微调后在原基准上macro-AP 88.5，超过此前监督分类器的77.8，mAP达0.870；仅用10%标签即比同架构从零训练高+0.159。归因显示模型同时利用了N2+两条谱带。跨域迁移方面：红外预训练的SpectraFM低于未训练对照，光学预训练的SpecFormer接近但未达到域内预训练水平。

Conclusion: 领域内自监督预训练能有效利用海量无标签极光光谱，在高标签效率下匹配或超越专家特征与既有监督模型；现有跨领域光谱基础模型无法简单替代，其迁移效果取决于预训练光谱窗口与目标域的匹配程度。

Abstract: Auroral spectrographs such as the Auroral Spectrograph In Skibotn (ASIS) record hundreds of thousands of emission spectra, but only a few hundred can be labelled by an expert. To exploit the rest, we pretrain a 1D Vision Transformer with a masked autoencoder on 223,000 unlabelled spectra. Without labels, its representation recovers the emission-line intensity ratios that physicists use to diagnose the precipitating particles (R^2 0.91 vs. 0.77 for an untrained control) and, under one linear probe, classifies as well as 13 features designed by experts. Fine-tuned, the model outperforms the previous supervised auroral classifier on its own benchmark (macro-AP 88.5 vs. 77.8), reaches 0.870 mAP, and exceeds the same architecture trained from scratch by +0.159 with 10% of the labels; attribution shows that it uses both N2+ bands. Could an existing pretrained model replace it? Two astronomical spectral foundation models and a time-series model transfer according to their spectral window: SpectraFM, trained in the infrared, falls below the untrained control, whereas SpecFormer, trained in the optical, approaches in-domain pretraining without reaching it.

</details>


### [101] [Budgeted Quotient-Residual Guidance for Frozen Pocket-Conditioned Molecular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31222)
*Xinyu Wang,Jinbo Bi,Minghu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QRG是一种无需重训练的推理时校正方法，通过将商空间目标提升为度量水平环境方向并以采样器步长设定信任预算，激活冻结分子扩散生成器上的商目标信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口袋条件分子扩散虽能更新环境原子坐标，但许多先导优化目标定义在商特征（距离、接触、锚定子结构）上，这些目标在原始局部商梯度下往往处于"休眠"状态，而重训练或修改骨干代价高昂。

Method: 提出预算化商残差引导（QRG）：将商余向量提升为度量水平环境方向，由冻结采样器自身步长范数设定信任预算控制尺度（商几何选方向、采样器运动限幅度）；推导水平提升、闭式采样器预算更新、冻结反向步附近的KL/动能解释、等变性条件，以及针对预算上限的截面与残差控制的产品预算分配。

Result: 受控商任务验证采样器相对投递能激活原始局部商梯度无法激活的信号。在冻结TargetDiff骨干、官方seed-0 CBGBench配体生成/编辑扫描中：Local-QRG将有效性在片段生长上从0.815提升至0.864、骨架跃迁0.664至0.707、连接子设计0.681至0.712；PredNext-QRG改善片段/骨架且在连接子上近中性。新颖性保持1.000，多样性在匹配多种子切片中得以保留。

Conclusion: QRG为冻结分子采样器提供了一条轻量级的商感知推理路径，具备显式运行时核算，可在不重训练采样器、不修改骨干的前提下实现任务相关的性能提升。

Abstract: Pocket-conditioned molecular diffusion updates ambient atom coordinates, but many lead-optimization objectives are expressed on quotient features such as distances, contacts, and anchored substructures. We introduce budgeted quotient-residual guidance (QRG), an inference-time correction that makes these quotient objectives active without retraining the molecular generator. QRG lifts quotient covectors to metric-horizontal ambient directions and delivers them through a trust budget set by the frozen sampler's own step norm: quotient geometry chooses the direction, while sampler motion bounds the scale. We derive the horizontal lift, closed-form sampler-budget update, KL/kinetic interpretation around a frozen reverse step, equivariance conditions, and a product-budget split for budget-capped section and residual controls. Controlled quotient tasks confirm that sampler-relative delivery activates signals that raw local quotient gradients leave dormant. On frozen TargetDiff backbones, official seed-0 CBGBench ligand-generation/editing sweeps show practical quality-runtime gains: Local-QRG improves validity from 0.815 to 0.864 on fragment growing, 0.664 to 0.707 on scaffold hopping, and 0.681 to 0.712 on linker design, while PredNext-QRG improves fragment/scaffold and remains near-neutral on linker. Novelty remains 1.000 and diversity is preserved in the matched multi-seed molecular slice, giving task-dependent improvements without sampler retraining or backbone modification. Overall, QRG provides a lightweight route to quotient-aware inference for frozen molecular samplers with explicit runtime accounting.

</details>


### [102] [Deterministic Regime Switching and Feasibility Inversion in Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31250)
*Mahesh Reddy Pagadala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在DTR参考模拟器simrd上，发现内存预算的微小变化（0.10%）即可导致截然不同的执行状态：LSTM轨迹上开销相差达7.3倍，ResNet-32轨迹上出现确定性的可行性反转（0.102–0.106出现OOM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTR作为内存受限DNN训练的在线驱逐策略，其行为通常被假设对预算变化平滑鲁棒；作者希望检验该假设，揭示预算微调下的确定性不稳定现象，并区分不同病理机制。

Method: 基于公开执行轨迹，使用DTR作者提供的参考模拟器（simrd）进行细粒度预算扫描与消融实验：统计驱逐次数、不同存储集合、Jaccard重叠度；对ResNet-32做精细预算扫描定位OOM区间；用DTR作者自有变体定位LSTM不稳定的成因（联合size-staleness评分项）。

Result: LSTM：预算相差仅0.10%即切换快/慢执行区，开销差达7.3倍；慢区由同一批存储被反复驱逐驱动（每存储驱逐次数1.33→8.27，而不同被驱逐存储集合几乎不变，5233 vs 5236，Jaccard 0.999）。ResNet-32：ratio 0.101可行，0.102–0.106不可行（OOM），0.107起恢复可行；OOM直接原因是所有可驱逐张量驱逐后，全pinned的递归重计算前沿仍超预算。

Conclusion: 这些是至少两种不同的预算敏感病理，而非单一机制；LSTM不稳定与联合size-staleness评分项相关。所有结论仅限参考模拟器，生产运行时的复现为未来工作。

Abstract: We report fine-grained, deterministic instability in Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization (DTR), an online eviction policy for memory-constrained DNN training, measured on the reference DTR simulator (simrd) using public execution traces. On an LSTM trace, memory budgets differing by 0.10% of unconstrained peak memory select fast and slow execution regimes whose overheads differ by as much as 7.3x; the slow regime is driven by broadly repeated re-eviction of the same storages (evictions per storage rise from 1.33 to 8.27 while the set of distinct evicted storages is essentially unchanged: 5,233 vs 5,236, with the two sets overlapping at Jaccard 0.999). On a ResNet-32 trace, a fine budget sweep reveals a deterministic feasibility inversion: the run is feasible at ratio 0.101, infeasible (OOM) across 0.102-0.106, and feasible again from 0.107. We trace the immediate cause of the OOM to a fully pinned recursive rematerialization frontier that exceeds the budget after every evictable tensor has been evicted. Ablations using the DTR authors' own variants implicate the joint size-staleness scoring term in the observed LSTM instability. We argue these are at least two distinct budget-sensitive pathologies rather than one mechanism, and we separate what is demonstrated from what remains hypothesised. All results concern the reference simulator; reproduction in a production runtime is future work. Code, instrumentation, and raw results accompany this preprint.

</details>


### [103] [Softmax Reparameterization for Output-Head Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31291)
*Asim Kadav,Christian Flores,Chirag Arora,Varun Kotte,Hongbo Zheng,Lan Yan,Priya Shanmugasundaram,Tracy Holloway King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 softmax 重参数化：在量化前选择与原始等价的功能性输出头，通过一维系数搜索降低 W4/W2 量化的 KL 偏差，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大词表使小语言模型的输出头（output head）成为推理成本的重要来源，而低比特量化（W4/W2）会显著扭曲其 softmax 预测分布，需要在保持功能等价的前提下改善量化保真度。

Method: 后训练方法：从每一输出行减去词表行均值的标量倍，并以验证集 KL 为目标分别针对 RTN、激活加权 MSE（AW-MSE）和全 Hessian GPTQ 选择系数；该一维搜索涵盖原始头与固定均值中心化两种情形，保持全精度 softmax 分布不变且不改动解码器；对 soft-capping 等非线性 logit 路径用秩一校正处理。

Result: 在七个头上，W4 增益集中于基线量化严重失真处：Phi-4-mini 上 AW-MSE KL 从 0.936 降至 0.256；增益在更强 GPTQ 校准下依然存在，并与精确逐通道缩放和仿射量化互补。四个头、三种 W4 量化器下，WikiText 选定的冻结系数可迁移到 C4 与 OpenWebMath，在 18 个系数不等于 1 的对比中全部优于均值中心化，其余 6 个持平。W2 压力测试下收益覆盖近全部模型—量化器矩阵。匹配残差分析表明保真度提升可伴随更大的 logit 重建误差，但降低残差的 Fisher 加权代价。对 shift-compatible 头，该方法不增加推理操作并保持 packed W4 执行；解码器保持 BF16 时，量化 Phi 输出头使 batch-one 生成延迟较 BF16 头基线降低 10.8%。

Conclusion: softmax 重参数化是一种免训练、免额外推理开销的即插即用后处理，可在多种量化器与低比特设置下稳定改善输出头的量化保真度与压缩收益。

Abstract: Large vocabularies make output heads a substantial inference cost in small language models. We propose softmax reparameterization, a post-training method that selects a functionally equivalent output head before quantization. The method subtracts a scalar multiple of the vocabulary-row mean from every output row and selects the coefficient by validation KL separately for RTN, activation-weighted MSE, and full-Hessian GPTQ. This one-dimensional search includes the original head and fixed mean-centering, preserves the full-precision softmax distribution, and leaves the trained decoder unchanged; a rank-one correction handles nonlinear logit paths such as soft-capping. Across seven heads, W4 gains concentrate where baseline quantization substantially distorts predictions: on Phi-4-mini, AW-MSE KL falls from 0.936 to 0.256. The gains survive stronger GPTQ calibration and remain complementary to exact per-channel scaling and affine quantization. Across four heads and three W4 quantizers, frozen WikiText-selected coefficients also transfer to C4 and OpenWebMath, outperforming mean-centering in all 18 comparisons where the frozen coefficient differs from $1$ and matching it in the remaining six. At W2, used as a compression stress test, benefits broaden across nearly the full model--quantizer matrix. Matched residual analysis shows that improved fidelity can accompany greater logit reconstruction error while reducing the residual's Fisher-weighted cost. For shift-compatible heads, reparameterization adds no inference operation and preserves packed W4 execution: with the decoder held in BF16, quantizing the Phi output head reduces batch-one generation latency by 10.8% relative to the BF16-head baseline.

</details>


### [104] [Benchmarking Attention for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31306)
*Maximilian Schambach,Clemens Biehl,Sam Thelin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了面向表格上下文学习的二维注意力基准测试，系统比较 Torch SDPA、FlashAttention-2/3/4、vLLM 与 SageAttention 在三种 GPU 上的前向/反向吞吐，发现行/列注意力最优后端不同且依赖硬件、序列长度与头维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN、Mitra、ConTextTab 等表格上下文学习器依赖对二维潜在嵌入序列的交替行/列注意力，其注意力模式与语言模型的一维情形显著不同：行注意力序列更长、列注意力序列很短；表格数据的跨步内存布局使连续张量生成代价高；且当前模型隐维度较小。然而高效注意力研究多集中于一维序列，二维表格场景尚未被系统性探索。

Method: 作者建立了可复现的基准测试框架，针对表格注意力的独特特性，在多种后端（Torch SDPA 的高效与 cuDNN 实现、FlashAttention-2/3/4，以及推理专用后端 vLLM 和 SageAttention）上，于三种 GPU 世代（A100、H100、B200）测量真实表格形状下的前向与反向吞吐。

Result: 最优后端在列注意力与行注意力之间不同，并随硬件和模型细节变化：针对各 GPU 世代定制的 FlashAttention 实现总体表现最佳；但在列注意力、较长序列情形下，cuDNN 有时更优，交叉点取决于头维度。推理专用后端中，SageAttention 在行注意力及超过 16k 行的大序列上表现良好。

Conclusion: 该可复现基准为表格原生注意力的后续改进奠定了基础，基准与评估代码已开源：https://github.com/SAP-samples/tabular-attention-benchmark。

Abstract: Tabular in-context learners such as TabPFN, Mitra, or ConTextTab rely on alternating row and column attention over 2D sequences of latent embeddings.
