These attention patterns differ markedly from the one-dimensional case in language models: row attention involves longer sequences while column attention operates on much shorter ones, and the strided memory layout of tabular data makes producing contiguous tensors costly. Moreover, the hidden dimensions used in current models are small compared to recent language models. Yet efficient attention has been studied mostly for one-dimensional sequences, leaving the two-dimensional tabular setting unexplored. To this end, we create a reproducible benchmarking setup and study the unique characteristics of tabular attention across several backends -- Torch SDPA (efficient and cuDNN), FlashAttention-2/3/4, and the inference-only backends vLLM and SageAttention -- measuring forward and backward throughput across realistic tabular shapes on three GPU generations (A100, H100, B200). We find that the optimal backend choice differs between column and row attention and varies across hardware as well as model specifics: While the FlashAttention implementations tailored for each GPU generation perform overall best, they are at times outperformed by CuDNN in the case of column attention at longer sequences with cross-over points depending on the head dimension. Among inference-only backends, SageAttention performs well for row attention and large sequences beyond 16\,k rows. Our reproducible benchmark lays the foundation for future improvements to table-native attention. The self-contained benchmarking and evaluation code is openly available at: https://github.com/SAP-samples/tabular-attention-benchmark

</details>


### [105] [LUCID: Learning Under Confounding for Inference and Discovery in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31315)
*Mohammad Fesanghary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUCID 是一种"调节自适应"的去混杂层，先用 Marčenko--Pastur 谱路由器判定混杂机制，再施加匹配的去混杂策略，可包裹现有因果发现引擎，在同步（lag-0）结构恢复上显著提升有向、含滞后分辨的图 $F_1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列中普遍存在未观测的共同原因（混杂因子），会诱发虚假关联，使因果发现方法误将其当作直接边，从而破坏图结构的正确性。现有方法通常假定单一混杂设定，难以适应混杂强度、稀疏性等机制的差异。

Method: LUCID 是一个调节自适应（regime-adaptive）的去混杂层：首先用 Marčenko--Pastur 谱路由器从数据中估计混杂机制；当谱显示普遍存在因子混杂时，对因子主导的变异进行衰减，并从残差创新中恢复同步（lag-0）结构；边选择通过数据驱动的无边缘零模型（edge-free null）进行校准。该方法不绑定于特定发现算法，可直接包裹已有因果发现引擎。

Result: 在覆盖混杂强度与稀疏性、载荷密度、滞后结构、波动动态、边异质性、持续性、间歇性与尾部行为等变化的合成 OOD 基准上，LUCID 取得最佳 family-weighted 有向、含滞后分辨的图 $F_1=0.60$，比最强基线绝对提升 $0.19$（相对约 $46\%$）。相对更宽松的滞后折叠评分其优势更明显，并在间歇性与重尾混杂下保持稳健；在三种现有发现方法上均取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: LUCID 通过谱路由器驱动的机制识别与匹配式去混杂，为受未观测共同原因干扰的时间序列因果发现提供了算法无关、稳健且可迁移的增强方案，代码、基准生成器与全部实验均在 https://github.com/bloomberg/causal-ts 开源。

Abstract: Unobserved common causes are pervasive in real-world time series and can induce spurious associations that causal discovery methods mistake for direct edges. We propose LUCID (Learning Under Confounding for Inference and Discovery, a regime-adaptive deconfounding layer that first estimates the confounding regime from data using a Marčenko--Pastur spectral router, then applies a deconfounding strategy matched to that regime. When the spectrum indicates pervasive factor confounding, LUCID attenuates factor-dominated variation and recovers contemporaneous (lag-$0$) structure from the resulting innovations, with edge selection calibrated against a data-driven edge-free null. Rather than being tied to a particular discovery algorithm, it can wrap existing discovery engines; we demonstrate consistent improvements across three such methods. On a diverse synthetic out-of-distribution benchmark spanning changes in confounder strength and sparsity, loading density, lag structure, volatility dynamics, edge heterogeneity, persistence, intermittency, and tail behavior, LUCID achieves the best family-weighted directed, lag-resolved graph $F_1$ ($0.60$), improving over the strongest baseline by $0.19$ absolute ($\approx\!46\%$ relative). Its advantage widens relative to looser lag-collapsed scoring, and remains robust under intermittent and heavy-tailed confounding. Code reproducing the method, the benchmark generators, and every reported experiment is available at https://github.com/bloomberg/causal-ts.

</details>


### [106] [More Sensors Only One Field: Rethinking Continual Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31325)
*Lewei Xie,Haoyu Zhang,Jiajun Zhou,Yulong Chen,Guanxing Chen,Yu-An Huang,Hau-San Wong,Yifan Zhang,Zhi-An Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STFO（时空场算子），将预测知识参数化为共享的场演化算子，通过观测与查询接口应对传感器扩展，实现持续时空预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续时空预测需应对动态演化与传感器网络扩张，但传统基于图的持续学习方法将预测表征绑定在当前传感器布局上，传感器扩展会改变已学空间关系的表征。作者的关键洞察：传感器扩展改变的是关于过程的可用证据，而非需要学习的动力学本身。

Method: STFO将预测知识参数化为共享的场演化算子，用观测与查询接口处理变化的传感器布局。基于归一化坐标的聚合将不规则传感器历史提升到固定潜网格，无需传感器特定参数即可跨观测集复用已学空间映射；针对过程漂移，用谱描述子概括跨空间尺度的变化，并调节傅里叶传播与注意力以适应当前空间状态；基于坐标的解码在传感器位置查询演化后的场，并将空间修正与局部历史预测结合。

Result: 在PEMS-Stream、CA-Stream和AIR-Stream上取得最优平均预测性能。STFO-Large相比DOL在PEMS-Stream上平均MAE降低8.4%，在CA-Stream上降低4.7%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: STFO通过解耦传感器布局与动力学学习，有效支持传感器扩展下的持续时空预测，验证了场算子参数化与坐标接口设计的有效性。

Abstract: Continual spatio-temporal forecasting supports traffic management and environmental monitoring under evolving dynamics and expanding sensor networks. However, conventional graph-based continual learning methods tie forecasting representations to the current sensor layout, so sensor expansion can alter the representation of learned spatial relationships. Our key insight is that sensor expansion changes the evidence available about a process without necessarily changing the dynamics to be learned. We propose STFO (Spatio-Temporal Field Operator), which parameterizes forecasting knowledge as a shared field-evolution operator and handles changing sensor layouts through observation and query interfaces. Normalized coordinate-based aggregation lifts irregular sensor histories onto a fixed latent grid, enabling reuse of learned spatial maps across observation sets without sensor-specific parameters. To accommodate process drift, a spectral descriptor summarizes variation across spatial scales and conditions Fourier propagation and attention to adapt operator responses to the current spatial regime. Coordinate-based decoding queries the evolved field at sensor locations and combines spatial corrections with local-history predictions. Experiments on PEMS-Stream, CA-Stream, and AIR-Stream demonstrate state-of-the-art average forecasting performance. STFO-Large reduces average MAE over DOL by 8.4% on PEMS-Stream and 4.7% on CA-Stream. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xielewei/Spatio-Temporal-Field-Operator.

</details>


### [107] [Bridging Body and Brain: Gene-Driven Morphology--Control Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31329)
*Fu Feng,Ruixiao Shi,Yucheng Xie,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出Morphogene隐变量蓝图与GeCode协同设计框架，将形态与控制的联合优化转化为紧凑隐空间中的探索，在2D/3D任务上实现更快收敛与更高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形态-控制协同设计方法多采用独立网络分别建模形态与控制，仅通过共享任务目标间接耦合，缺乏显式的高层协调，导致探索效率与身心兼容性不足。受生物基因协同调控发育的启发，作者希望用一个紧凑的隐式蓝图显式桥接"身体"与"大脑"。

Method: 提出Morphogene：一个紧凑的隐变量蓝图，通过AdaConcat在肢体层面同时条件化形态生成与控制生成，其变化可诱导两个组件产生协同改变。基于该表示提出GeCode，将协同设计建模为Morphogene空间中的探索：每个Morphogene锚定一个局部设计区域，在区域内探索邻近的"身体-大脑"设计；同时以性能引导更新将锚点移向更优区域，从而结合局部精化与全局探索，并保持身心兼容性。

Result: 在多种2D与3D协同设计任务上，GeCode持续优于现有最先进方法，收敛速度显著更快，最终性能更高。

Conclusion: 以紧凑隐式基因蓝图显式耦合形态与控制生成，并将协同设计转化为该隐空间的锚点式探索，是一种兼顾局部精化、全局探索与身心兼容的有效协同设计范式。

Abstract: Morphology--control co-design jointly optimizes an agent's body structure and control policy as an integrated embodied system. However, existing methods typically model morphology design and control with separate networks coupled only indirectly through a shared task objective, limiting explicit high-level coordination. Inspired by natural genes that coordinate biological development, we introduce \textbf{Morphogene}, a compact latent blueprint that bridges an agent's body and brain. Through AdaConcat, Morphogene jointly conditions morphology and control generation at the limb level, allowing its variations to induce coordinated changes in both components. Building on this representation, we propose \textbf{GeCode}, which formulates co-design as exploration in the compact Morphogene space. Each Morphogene anchors a local design region in which nearby body--brain designs are explored, while performance-guided updates move these anchors toward promising regions for more efficient exploration of the broader design space. This process combines local refinement with global exploration while preserving body--brain compatibility. Extensive experiments across diverse 2D and 3D co-design tasks demonstrate that GeCode consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, achieving substantially faster convergence and higher final performance.

</details>


### [108] [Progressive Memory Transformer: Memory-Aware Attention for Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31351)
*Tord Sture Stangeland,Andreas Köhler,Steffen Mæland,Adín Ramíres Rivera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出渐进记忆Transformer（PMT），通过可写、窗口对齐的记忆模块显式暴露局部、中程与全局三个尺度的表征，并分别施加自监督目标（token连续性、窗口motif、序列一致性），在多类时序基准上取得强少标签分类与竞争性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列在多尺度上同时承载结构信息（细粒度变化、中程motif、全局属性），下游任务也作用于不同尺度。然而现有自监督方法多依赖实例级对比损失和有限的时间邻域监督，仅在全球层面优化表征，未显式利用这种结构层次。

Method: 构建一个显式跨三个尺度独立施加约束的学习框架：局部目标保证token连续性，中程目标建模窗口级motif，全局目标实现序列级一致性。为支撑该框架，需backbone在每个尺度上都暴露表征，因此提出渐进记忆Transformer（PMT）：在标准Transformer上增加可写、与窗口对齐的记忆，从而在token级与序列级表征之外额外提供中程尺度表征。

Result: 在七个UCR/UEA/UCI分类基准、一个cue-retention探针及预测基准上，PMT学到的表征在全局、中程与局部三个尺度均可被良好探测：1–5%标签下分类性能强，多预测视野下性能具竞争力，并给出记忆状态捕获中程motif的定量与定性证据。

Conclusion: 显式建模时序结构层次并让backbone在多尺度暴露表征，是提升时序自监督表征质量的有效途径；PMT验证了窗口对齐记忆作为中程尺度载体的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Time-series carry structure simultaneously at multiple scales (fine-grained variation, mid-range motifs, and global properties) and downstream tasks operate at correspondingly different scales. Most existing self-supervised learning approaches supervise representations globally via instance-level contrastive losses and limited temporal neighborhood supervision, but do not explicitly exploit the structural hierarchy. We propose a learning framework that explicitly enforces a structural hierarchy across three scales independently: a local objective for token continuity, a mid-range objective for window-level motifs, and a global objective for sequence-level agreement. Realizing this framework requires the backbone to expose a representation at each scale; we introduce \textbf{Progressive Memory Transformer} (PMT), which augments a transformer with writable, window-aligned memory that exposes the mid-range scale alongside the token and sequence-level representations conventional transformers already provide. Across seven UCR/UEA/UCI classification benchmarks, a cue-retention probe, and forecasting benchmarks, PMT learns representations that probe well at the global, mid-range, and local scales---strong low-label classification (1--5\% labels), competitive forecasting performance across multiple horizons, and quantitative and qualitative evidence that memory states capture mid-range motifs.

</details>


### [109] [Brenier Meets Adversarial Training: Optimal Transport Geometry for Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31363)
*Alireza Abdollahpoorrostam,Ehsan Sharifian,Buse Şen,Marco Cuturi,Daniel Kuhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带Wasserstein惩罚的DRO公式，将对手问题重构为传输映射优化，证明最优映射满足循环单调性，并据此提出多起点粒子上升与输入凸神经网络两种方法，在多个任务上优于标准对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRO虽为分布偏移下的学习提供了原理性框架，但对非凸损失函数而言，最坏情况风险的评估极为困难，限制了其实际应用。标准对抗训练基于逐样本局部优化，会违背循环单调性并浪费传输成本，因此需要新的求解方法。

Method: 作者采用带Wasserstein惩罚的惩罚型DRO公式，允许对手选择任意分布但偏离经验分布需付出惩罚。将对手问题重构为在传输映射上的优化，并证明最优映射具有循环单调性。据此提出两种补救方法：一是多起点粒子上升，通过交替进行并行梯度上升与重分配来强制样本间的循环单调性；二是将对抗映射参数化为输入凸神经网络的梯度，从构造上保证循环单调性。

Result: 在鲁棒回归、图像分类和鲁棒控制实验中，所提方法一致优于标准对抗训练和最先进基线，在分布偏移下取得了更好的鲁棒性和泛化性能。

Conclusion: 循环单调性是带Wasserstein惩罚DRO中对手最优映射的关键结构性质，显式利用该性质可有效改进对抗训练；两种所提方法在理论与实验上均验证了其有效性，为DRO的实用化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) provides a principled framework for learning under distribution shift, but its practical use is hindered by the difficulty of evaluating worst-case risks for nonconvex loss functions. We study a penalized DRO formulation in which the adversary may choose any distribution but incurs a Wasserstein penalty for deviating from the empirical distribution. We show that the adversary's problem can be reformulated as an optimization problem over transport maps that push empirical samples to adversarial ones, and we prove that optimal maps are cyclically monotone. We also show that standard adversarial training---based on per-sample local optimization---violates cyclical monotonicity and wastes transport costs unless the adversary is severely restricted. We propose two remedies. First, we introduce multi-start particle ascent, which alternates parallel gradient ascent with reassignment to enforce cyclical monotonicity across samples. Second, we parameterize adversarial maps as gradients of input-convex neural networks, which guarantees cyclical monotonicity by construction. Experiments on robust regression, image classification, and robust control show that our methods consistently outperform standard adversarial training and state-of-the-art baselines, achieving improved robustness and better generalization under distribution shift.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Understanding LLM-Based Log Anomaly Detection: An Empirical Study of Performance, Efficiency, and Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31371)
*Bin Li,Dongdong Wang,Siyang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对LLM日志异常检测进行系统实证分析，考察适应策略、模型架构、参数规模、量化配置及噪声鲁棒性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在日志异常检测中表现promising，但其适应策略、架构与部署配置如何影响检测效果尚缺乏系统理解，且现有评估多聚焦精度，忽视效率与鲁棒性。

Method: 在三个公开日志数据集上开展系统实证分析，对比不同适应策略、模型架构、参数规模与量化设置，并在不同扰动水平下考察结构噪声、语义噪声与标签噪声下的检测鲁棒性。

Result: 不同适应策略间性能差异显著；模型规模扩大带来的检测增益随数据集而异；检测精度相近的模型计算成本可能差异明显；低比特量化在所评估配置下基本保持检测性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM日志异常检测在性能、效率与鲁棒性方面的经验规律，强调应超越以精度为唯一导向的评估范式，关注实际部署中的多维权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated promising performance in log anomaly detection, yet how their adaptation strategies, architectures, and deployment configurations affect detection effectiveness remains insufficiently understood. To investigate these factors, we conduct a systematic empirical analysis across three public log datasets, examining different adaptation strategies, model architectures, parameter scales, and quantization settings. Our results reveal substantial performance differences across adaptation strategies, while model scaling yields varying detection gains across datasets. We further observe that models with comparable detection accuracy can exhibit markedly different computational costs, and that low-bit quantization largely preserves detection performance in the evaluated configurations. Finally, we examine detection robustness under structural, semantic, and label noise at different perturbation levels. These findings provide empirical insights into the performance, efficiency, and robustness of LLM-based log anomaly detection, highlighting practical considerations beyond conventional accuracy-oriented evaluation.

</details>


### [111] [Differential Attention Unlocks Complementary EEG and Speech Fusion for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31399)
*Philip H. Lee,Shreeram Suresh Chandra,John H. L. Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EmoSpeechBrain多模态情感识别框架，核心观点是"先抑噪、再融合"：EEG编码器用差分注意力抵消共模噪声，门控适配器对齐并加权两模态，在两个数据集上显著优于SOTA EEG编码器及单模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别常将EEG与语音视为情感的内部神经信号与外部声音表达两个视图，但直接朴素融合反而弱于较强的单模态，原因在于EEG伪迹带来的噪声污染了共享表示，因此需要先抑制噪声才能有效融合。

Method: 提出EmoSpeechBrain框架：EEG编码器采用差分注意力（differential attention），通过两张注意力图相减来抵消共模噪声、分离判别性神经活动；并设计基于注意力的门控适配器（attention-based gating adapter），在共享空间中对齐两个模态并为其预测贡献加权。

Result: 在PME4与EAV两个数据集上，EmoSpeechBrain相比其他SOTA EEG编码器将MER准确率最高提升12.9%，并分别超过单模态语音与EEG基线最高13.1%和23.1%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，一旦EEG噪声被有效抑制，融合能够带来朴素组合无法获得的性能增益，验证了"噪声抑制是有效融合前提"这一核心洞见。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition (MER) increasingly pairs EEG with speech, treating internal neural signals and external vocal expression as informative views of affect. In practice, naive fusion underperforms the stronger single modality, because EEG artifacts inject noise that corrupts the shared representation. We introduce EmoSpeechBrain, a multimodal framework built on the insight that noise suppression is a precondition for effective fusion. Its EEG encoder uses differential attention, taking the difference between two attention maps to cancel shared noise and isolate discriminative neural activity. An attention-based gating adapter aligns both modalities in a shared space and weights each one's contribution to the prediction. On two datasets - PME4 and EAV, EmoSpeechBrain improves MER accuracy by up to 12.9% over other state-of-the-art (SOTA) EEG encoders, and surpasses unimodal speech and EEG baselines by up to 13.1% and 23.1%. These results show that once EEG noise is suppressed, fusion delivers gains that naive combination cannot.

</details>


### [112] [Decodable In-Context State and Model Output Across Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31401)
*Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli,Samrath Singh Chadha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文追踪探测准确率、模型输出与引导响应在预训练与后训练检查点上的变化，揭示探测可解码性与引导修复能力随训练动态演化，并指出仅凭错误上的可解码性无法证明输出信息被丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已证明探测可解码上下文绑定上的模型错误，且探测引导可修复部分错误，但尚不清楚这些能力如何随训练检查点演化，以及可解码性能否作为输出信息被丢弃的证据。

Method: 沿公开的预训练与后训练检查点追踪探测准确率、模型输出和引导响应；对比保存的分数以区分探测正确但概率低/高的错误；用oracle-target引导与保留聚合分离目标质量与干预敏感性；用最终状态或候选logits训练解码器进行留出比较，并给出信息论反例。

Result: Pythia预训练中探测准确率上升；探测引导从可忽略的全试验收益变为两个模型规模上更大的收益；保留分数可区分探测正确错误中正确候选概率低与高于均匀的情况；oracle-target引导已能修复许多早期错误，但聚合无法分离目标质量与干预敏感性；晚期检查点模型错误上未检测到最终状态的解码优势。

Conclusion: 探测可解码性与引导修复能力随训练动态增强，但仅凭错误上的可解码性不能确立输出信息被丢弃；与下游遗漏的联系仍待解决。

Abstract: Prior work established that a probe can decode an in-context binding on model errors and that probe-guided steering can repair some of them. We follow probe accuracy, model output, and steering response across public pretraining and post-training checkpoints. Probe accuracy rises during Pythia pretraining, while probe-guided steering moves from negligible all-trial benefit to a larger benefit at two model sizes. Saved scores distinguish probe-correct errors with low and above-uniform model probability for the correct candidate. Oracle-target steering already repairs many early errors, but saved aggregates cannot separate target quality from intervention sensitivity. A held-out comparison of decoders trained on the final state or candidate logits finds no detected final-state advantage on late-checkpoint model errors. An information-theoretic counterexample explains why decodability on errors alone cannot establish discarded output information. The connection to downstream omissions remains open.

</details>


### [113] [Evaluating the accuracy of KV cache reuse techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31415)
*Samuel Cestola,Tianxiang Xia,Pengfei Zheng,Weiyan Zheng,Bo Wang,Yi Zhao,Diego Didona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前位置无关KV缓存复用技术的评估指标无法真实刻画复用带来的精度损失，且现有数据集缺乏充分评估所需的复用动态性；为此提出无歧义的精度损失评估方法，并发布Boxoffice工具以程序化生成具有挑战性复用模式的评估数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置无关KV缓存复用旨在通过跨提示复用块级KV缓存来降低检索增强生成的延迟，但当前评估方法存在缺陷：测量指标无法忠实捕捉复用导致的精度损失，常人为夸大所报告的有效性；同时现有数据集不具备充分评估此类技术所需的复用动态性。因此需要更可靠的评估方法与数据集。

Method: 提出一种能够无歧义地度量KV缓存复用所致精度损失的评估方法；并引入Boxoffice工具，程序化生成评估数据集，以构造并覆盖具有挑战性的KV缓存复用模式。

Result: 揭示了现有评估指标会人为夸大KV缓存复用技术的有效性，且现有数据集不足以评估该技术；所提评估方法可无歧义地测量精度损失，Boxoffice可生成具备挑战性复用模式的评估数据集。

Conclusion: KV缓存复用技术的评估需采用能忠实反映精度损失的方法与具备充分复用动态性的数据集；本文的评估方法与Boxoffice工具为更可靠、可复现的评估提供了基础，有助于避免有效性被高估。

Abstract: Position-independent KV cache reuse aims to reduce latency in retrieval-augmented generation by reusing chunk-level KV caches across prompts. We show that current evaluations of KV cache reuse techniques rely on measurements that fail to faithfully capture the loss of accuracy attributable to reuse, often artificially inflating the reported effectiveness. We also show that existing datasets do not exhibit the reuse dynamics needed to thoroughly evaluate such techniques. To address these issues, we propose an evaluation methodology that measures this accuracy loss without ambiguity and we introduce Boxoffice, a tool that programmatically generates evaluation datasets that exercise challenging KV cache reuse patterns.

</details>


### [114] [Different Corruptions, Different Signals: Uncertainty and Loss in Federated Data Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31454)
*Bradley Scott,Zeqi Luo,Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了联邦学习（FL）中两种数据损坏检测信号：输入条件不确定性（学习和偶然方差、MC dropout 方差与熵）与预测-标签损失，发现二者分别对不同损坏模式更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FL 中数据损坏可能发生在输入或标签上，但不确定性估计与预测-标签损失是否对这两类损坏具有同等检测能力尚不清楚，因此需要系统性比较。

Method: 在 ResNet-20、CIFAR-10 与 SVHN、Dirichlet non-IID 划分下，将学习到的偶然方差、MC dropout 方差与熵等不确定性度量，与基于给定标签的预测-标签损失进行对比，测试对象为加性图像噪声与持续随机标签翻转。

Result: 对持续随机标签翻转，预测-标签损失的客户端内逐样本 AUC 为 CIFAR-10 0.85、SVHN 0.95，而所有不确定性估计接近随机水平（0.49–0.50）；对图像噪声，期望熵不确定性高于随机（CIFAR-10 0.67、SVHN 0.66），损失表现相近（两者均为 0.64）。

Conclusion: 不同损坏模式对应不同最强检测信号：持续标签翻转宜用预测-标签损失，图像噪声宜用期望熵不确定性，且其优势随联邦范围内损坏比例上升而更明显；鲁棒 FL 数据质量评估应按损坏类型匹配信号，而非仅依赖不确定性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) data corruption can affect either inputs or labels, but it remains unclear whether input-conditional uncertainty and prediction-label loss expose these corruption modes equally. This paper compares two corruption-detection signals in FL: input-conditional uncertainty and prediction-label loss. The uncertainty signal is characterised using a learned aleatoric variance estimate together with Monte Carlo (MC) dropout variance and entropy measures, while the loss is computed against the supplied label. We test these signals against additive image noise and persistent random label flips. On ResNet-20 with CIFAR-10 and SVHN under Dirichlet partitions with data that are not independent and identically distributed (non-IID), the two corruption types behave differently. For persistent random label flips, the within-client per-sample area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) is 0.85 on CIFAR-10 and 0.95 on SVHN for prediction-label loss, while every uncertainty estimator stays at chance (0.49--0.50). This pattern is consistent with the model remaining confident in the underlying image despite the supplied label being wrong. For image noise, expected-entropy uncertainty rises above chance (0.67 on CIFAR-10 and 0.66 on SVHN), while loss responds comparably (0.64 on both). Each signal is therefore the stronger detector for a different corruption: the prediction-label loss for persistent label flips, and expected-entropy uncertainty for image noise, with its advantage becoming apparent as federation-wide corruption prevalence increases. Robust FL data-quality assessment should match the signal to the corruption rather than rely on uncertainty alone across corruption types.

</details>


### [115] [Uncertainty-Aware Federated Learning for Infant Movement Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31463)
*Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于骨骼运动数据的联邦学习框架，用于婴儿运动分析与全身运动评估（GMA），并引入不确定性感知的联邦平均策略（UA-FedAvg）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴儿运动分析可为神经发育障碍的早期识别提供生物标志物，深度学习已在GMA等任务上达到专家水平，但现有方法多依赖集中式训练，需要将多机构数据集中存储，在隐私、治理与数据共享方面难以落地。

Method: 以烦躁运动（fidgety movement）分类为临床用例，基于视频骨骼运动表示构建三客户端联邦学习框架；采用蒙特卡洛Dropout在推理阶段估计预测不确定性，并提出UA-FedAvg，将MC-Dropout得到的预测熵引入联邦聚合，按客户端预测不确定性调整其贡献；同时给出结合验证损失的变体。实验采用跨被试评估协议。

Result: 联邦学习相比各客户端独立训练的本地模型显著提升分类性能，并接近集中式训练水平；在评估的多种数据划分配置下，UA-FedAvg及其引入验证损失的变体总体优于传统FedAvg。

Conclusion: 该工作表明联邦学习可在不集中数据的前提下实现接近集中式训练的婴儿运动分析性能，且基于预测不确定性的聚合加权能进一步提升联邦训练效果，为临床场景下的隐私保护型GMA提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Infant movement analysis provides valuable biomarkers for the early identification of neurodevelopmental disorders. Recent advances in deep learning have enabled automated analysis of infant movements from video-derived skeletal representations, achieving performance comparable to expert assessment for tasks such as General Movement Assessment (GMA). However, most existing approaches rely on centralized training, requiring data from multiple institutions to be collected and stored at a single site. Such assumptions are often impractical in clinical settings due to privacy, governance, and data-sharing constraints. To address these challenges, we present, to the best of our knowledge, the first federated learning framework for automated infant movement analysis and General Movement Assessment using skeletal motion data. As a clinically relevant use case, the proposed framework is evaluated on fidgety movement classification. To quantify model confidence, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout is employed to estimate predictive uncertainty during inference. Building upon this, we propose an Uncertainty-Aware Federated Averaging (UA-FedAvg) strategy that incorporates predictive entropy derived from MC-Dropout into the federated aggregation process, enabling client contributions to be adjusted according to their predictive uncertainty. Experiments were conducted using a cross-subject evaluation protocol under a three-client federated learning setting. Results demonstrate that federated learning substantially improves classification performance compared with independently trained local models while achieving performance approaching that of centralized training. Furthermore, UA-FedAvg and its variant incorporating validation loss generally outperform conventional FedAvg across the evaluated data-split configurations.

</details>


### [116] [Scaffold: Support Graph Theory Based Sparsification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31466)
*Siddhartha Shankar Das,Sai Karthik Navuluru,S M Ferdous,Ryan A. Rossi,Baris Coskunuzer,Lakshman Tamil,Edoardo Serra,Alex Pothen,Robert Rallo,Mahantesh M Halappanavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Scaffold 是一个基于支撑图理论预条件子的拓扑型无监督图稀疏化框架，通过联合控制扩张（dilation）与拥塞（congestion）来保留短通信路径并避免结构瓶颈，在19个同质/异质基准上取得最优聚合排名，仅用10%–50%的边即可恢复或接近全图GNN性能，同时显著降低内存与训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN依赖沿图边进行消息传递，其计算与内存开销强烈依赖于图密度。图稀疏化可降低成本，但盲目删边会破坏重要通信结构、导致预测性能下降。因此需要一个能够有原则地控制结构失真的稀疏化方法。

Method: 提出Scaffold，一种源自支撑图理论预条件子的拓扑型、无监督图稀疏化框架。它显式控制两个互补的结构量：扩张（dilation，衡量被删边所诱导的绕行路径长度）与拥塞（congestion，衡量这些绕行路径在保留支撑上的集中程度）。通过联合控制两者，在保留短通信路径的同时避免结构瓶颈。据作者所知，这是首个可扩展的、采用支撑路径扩张-拥塞联合准则的GNN稀疏化框架。

Result: 在涵盖小图到大图的19个同质与异质基准上，Scaffold在评估的稀疏化及相关方法中取得最佳聚合排名。每个稀疏支撑仅使用原图10%–50%的边，即可恢复或接近全图GNN性能，内存占用不到全图训练的一半，并减少端到端训练时间（含稀疏化开销）。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Scaffold通过扩张-拥塞联合准则实现了可控且可扩展的图稀疏化，在保持GNN预测性能的同时显著降低内存与训练成本，是面向GNN的拓扑型无监督稀疏化有效方案。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) rely on message passing over graph edges, making their computational and memory costs strongly dependent on graph density. Graph sparsification offers a natural way to reduce these costs, but removing edges indiscriminately can distort important communication structure and degrade predictive performance. We introduce Scaffold, a topology-based, unsupervised graph sparsification framework derived from support graph theory preconditioners. Scaffold explicitly controls two complementary structural quantities: dilation, which measures the length of rerouting paths induced by removed edges, and congestion, which measures how strongly these rerouted paths concentrate on the retained support. By jointly controlling dilation and congestion, Scaffold preserves short communication paths while avoiding structural bottlenecks. To our knowledge, Scaffold is the first scalable GNN sparsification framework to use a joint supporting-path dilation-congestion criterion. Across 19 homophilic and heterophilic benchmarks spanning small to large graphs, Scaffold achieves the best aggregate rank among the evaluated sparsification and related methods. Using only 10%-50% of the original edges per sparse support, Scaffold recovers or closely approaches full-graph GNN performance while using less than half the memory of full-graph training and reducing end-to-end training time, including sparsification overhead. We provide an open-source software package at https://github.com/siddhartha047/Scaffold.

</details>


### [117] [HySTAR: Anchored Hypergraphs for Stable Credit Assignment in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31531)
*Xinglong Luo,Yuding Zhang,Yuheng Kuang,Shuxuan Yuan,Zhenni Zeng,Weiqiang Zhu,Zhenhai Ji,Zhengning Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HySTAR 是一个基于 MAPPO 的框架，通过将自适应表征学习与时间一致的高阶值分解基座分离，解决部分可观测共享奖励下协作多智能体强化学习中的结构目标漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测与共享奖励的协作多智能体强化学习中，需要将团队结果归因到个体智能体及高阶联盟。MAPPO 风格的 critic 将联合行为压缩为单一全局价值；而动态重构分组拓扑的 critic 会随交互或活跃智能体的变化改变从智能体和联盟到价值分量的映射，本文称这种不一致为结构目标漂移（structural target drift）。

Method: HySTAR 将自适应表征学习与时间一致的高阶值分解基座分离：以重叠稀疏超图作为均匀覆盖的分解支架进行锚定，使用时空编码器表示物理与任务相关交互，并结合时间相关性与结构相关性构建智能体特定的优势函数。

Result: 在 SMAC、GRF、Traffic Junction 和 MPE 上，HySTAR 相较 MAPPO 风格、值分解和动态分组基线取得一致提升。在最难的 SMAC 设置上，相对 MAPPO 提升 16.7%，相对 HYGMA 提升 15.6%；在全部六个 GRF 场景上排名第一；相较 MAGIC 将 Traffic Junction 收敛轮数最多减少 40.2%；在 MPE 上取得最高回合奖励。受控的拓扑、智能体死亡、邻域及参数分析支持锚定分解支架同时自适应传播表征的收益。

Conclusion: 固定且均匀覆盖的分解支架可缓解结构目标漂移，与自适应表征相结合能在多个协作多智能体基准上稳定提升性能。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning under partial observability and shared rewards requires assigning team outcomes to individual agents and high-order coalitions. A MAPPO-style critic compresses joint behavior into one global value, while critics that dynamically reconstruct the grouping topology change the mapping from agents and coalitions to value components as interactions or active agents evolve. We refer to this inconsistency as structural target drift. We introduce HySTAR, a MAPPO-based framework that separates adaptive representation learning from a temporally consistent high-order value-decomposition basis. HySTAR anchors an overlapping sparse hypergraph as a uniformly covered decomposition scaffold, uses a spatiotemporal encoder to represent physical and task-dependent interactions, and combines temporal and structural relevance to construct agent-specific advantages. Experiments on SMAC, GRF, Traffic Junction, and MPE demonstrate consistent improvements over MAPPO-style, value-factorization, and dynamic-grouping baselines. On the hardest SMAC settings, HySTAR achieves relative gains of 16.7\% over MAPPO and 15.6\% over HYGMA, ranks first on all six GRF scenarios, reduces Traffic Junction convergence epochs by up to 40.2\% relative to MAGIC, and obtains the highest MPE episode rewards. Controlled topology, agent-death, neighborhood, and parameter analyses support the benefit of anchoring the decomposition scaffold while adapting the propagated representations.

</details>


### [118] [NEXT: Physics-Informed Neuro-Spectral Exponential Time Differencing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31539)
*Márcio Marques,Leonardo Mendonça,Leonardo M. Moreira,Christian Júnior de Oliveira,Vitor Balestro,Tiago Novello,Daniel Yukimura,Pavel Petrov,Lucas Nissenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEXT（神经谱指数时间差分架构），将NeuSA的谱表示与高阶指数积分器结合，通过对线性刚性部分做矩阵指数精确积分、对非线性余项用神经网络建模，在刚性PDE上实现稳定且高精度的求解，并可应用于反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN存在谱偏差与因果性缺失问题；NeuSA虽缓解了这两点，但在许多物理问题中出现的刚性微分方程上数值积分不稳定。

Method: NEXT将PDE解的谱表示与高阶指数积分器相结合：对PDE诱导向量场中的线性刚性部分通过矩阵指数精确积分，对可能非线性的剩余部分用神经网络建模。

Result: 在一组刚性PDE基准实验中，NEXT稳定且精确，而NeuSA数值发散；同时NEXT可应用于反问题，能从稀疏数据中学习未知参数或边界条件。

Conclusion: NEXT在保持谱架构优势的同时克服了刚性稳定性问题，适用于正向与反向PDE问题；代码已开源。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build neural representations of time-dependent PDE solutions, naturally incorporating physics knowledge and observational data, which makes them well suited to both forward and inverse PDE problems. PINNs, however, are known to suffer from spectral bias and lack of causality. Neuro-Spectral Architectures (NeuSA), a recently proposed alternative to PINNs, mitigate both issues, but their numerical integration becomes unstable for stiff differential equations arising in many relevant physical problems. This study proposes Neuro-Spectral Exponential Time Differencing Architectures (NEXT), which combines the spectral representation of the PDE solution in NeuSA with high-order exponential integrators. Within this approach, the linear stiff part of the vector field induced by the PDE is integrated exactly through matrix exponentials, while the possibly nonlinear remainder is modeled by a neural network. The effectiveness of NEXT is verified through benchmark experiments on a set of stiff PDEs, in which NEXT is stable and accurate while NeuSA diverges numerically. It is also shown that NEXT can be applied to inverse problems, where the model has to learn unknown parameters or boundary conditions from sparse data. All code used in this work is publicly available at: https://github.com/marcioh2m/next.git .

</details>


### [119] [BeatGraph: Self-Supervised Heartbeat Graphs for Infant ECG Representations from the Home Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31546)
*Mohammad Nur Hossain Khan,M. S. Krafczyk,Beverly G. Bolster,Nancy McElwain,Mark A. Hasegawa-Johnson,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BeatGraph，以心搏为表示单元，将30秒ECG窗口建模为心搏图，经掩码心搏嵌入预训练与微调，在多项婴儿ECG任务上显著超越最强基线，并跨年龄段迁移；同时发布首个家庭场景婴儿ECG公开语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG基础模型将信号切分为定长patch进行token化，忽略心脏结构，patch可能切断心搏，且每patch心搏数随心率变化。婴儿心率更高、ECG与成人临床12导联数据差异大，因此婴儿ECG模型应直接对心搏进行推理，而非从任意patch中还原心搏。

Method: BeatGraph以心搏为表示单元，将每段30秒窗口建模为心搏图。共享心搏编码器结合波形与心搏间期嵌入每个心搏；带位置编码的Transformer对心搏按时间排序；残差图注意力层建立所有心搏两两关系；注意力池化得到窗口嵌入。先在新采集的婴儿无标签语料上通过预测被掩码的心搏嵌入进行预训练，再针对各任务微调。

Result: 单一骨干网络支持睡眠-清醒检测、婴儿状态分类、活动来源识别（婴儿或照护者发起的运动）与情感识别，各任务macro-F1较最强基线提升0.076至0.158。跨年龄迁移方面，在ZZU-pECG儿科基准（0至14岁）取得0.892 AUROC，与最佳已发表自监督ECG模型相差0.001；在成人PTB-XL基准线性评估下，尽管仅用婴儿数据预训练，仍与该模型持平。

Conclusion: 以心搏为单元的图表示可有效建模婴儿ECG并支持多任务与跨年龄迁移；据作者所知，发布首个在家庭、教室与实验室场景采集、含状态与情感标签的公开婴儿ECG语料，包含143名3至11月龄婴儿共3,408小时单通道ECG，以及无标签预训练数据、基准任务和受试者级划分。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) foundation models typically tokenize the signal into fixed-length patches that ignore cardiac structure, so a patch may split a heartbeat and the number of beats in each patch shifts with heart rate. This matters most for infants, whose heart rates are higher and whose ECG differs from the adult, clinic-recorded 12-lead data these models are built on. A model for infant ECG should therefore reason about heartbeats directly rather than recover them from arbitrary patches. We propose BeatGraph, which makes the heartbeat its unit of representation, modeling each 30-second window as a graph of beats. A shared beat encoder embeds each heartbeat from its waveform and inter-beat intervals, a Transformer with positional encoding orders the beats in time, and residual graph attention layers relate every beat to every other before attention pooling yields a window embedding. We pretrain BeatGraph on our new corpus of unlabeled infant recordings by predicting masked-beat embeddings, then fine-tune it for each task. One backbone supports sleep-wake detection, infant-state classification, activity-source identification (infant- or caregiver-initiated movement), and affect recognition, improving macro-F1 over the strongest baseline on each task by 0.076 to 0.158. It also transfers across age groups, reaching 0.892 AUROC on the ZZU-pECG pediatric benchmark (ages 0 to 14), within 0.001 of the best published self-supervised ECG model, and matching that model under linear evaluation on the adult PTB-XL benchmark despite infant-only pretraining. Finally, to our knowledge, we release the first public infant ECG corpus collected in homes, classrooms, and laboratory settings with state and affect labels. It contains 3,408 hours of single-channel ECG from 143 infants aged 3 to 11 months, with unlabeled pretraining data, benchmark tasks, and subject-level splits.

</details>


### [120] [Online Learning via Learned Latent Bayesian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31559)
*Guy Gerson,Tomer Raviv,Nir Shlezinger,Tirza Routtenberg,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AURA框架：通过元学习离线学习低维隐状态空间模型来刻画最优参数在分布漂移下的演化，在线时用扩展卡尔曼滤波在该隐空间中跟踪，再经提升映射重构全参数，实现高效单步自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习需在严格计算约束下从流式数据快速适应非平稳环境。将在线学习建模为贝叶斯状态跟踪是原则性做法，但深度模型参数空间维度过高，直接应用贝叶斯滤波计算不可行，现有方法只能依赖限制性近似或人工设计的低维子空间。作者指出核心瓶颈是缺乏合适的低维动力学表示。

Method: AURA（Adaptive Update through Representation Adaptation）是一个元学习框架：离线学习一个低维隐状态空间模型，用以支配分布漂移下最优模型参数的演化；在线自适应在该学习到的隐空间中通过扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）完成，随后通过学习到的提升映射（lifting map）重建完整模型参数。该设计支持高效的单步在线更新并保留模型表达能力。

Result: 在时变信道下神经无线接收机的在线自适应任务，以及非平稳图像分类任务上评估。与现有在线学习和贝叶斯滤波基线相比，AURA在适应速度、准确率和计算效率上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对自适应友好的隐几何结构（adaptation-aware latent geometry）有助于在高维模型中实现有效的贝叶斯在线学习，验证了以学习低维动力学表示为突破口解决贝叶斯滤波在线学习瓶颈的合理性。

Abstract: Online learning in non-stationary environments requires models to adapt rapidly from streaming data under strict computational constraints. A principled approach casts online learning as Bayesian state tracking, where model parameters are updated sequentially via Bayesian filtering. However, applying Bayesian filters directly to modern deep models is computationally prohibitive due to the high dimensionality of parameter space, forcing existing methods to rely on restrictive approximations or manually designed low-dimensional subspaces. In this work, we identify the absence of a suitable low-dimensional dynamical representation as the core bottleneck in Bayesian filtering-based online learning. Accordingly, we propose Adaptive Update through Representation Adaptation (AURA), a meta-learning framework that learns offline a low-dimensional latent state-space model governing the evolution of optimal model parameters under distribution shift. Online adaptation is then performed via extended Kalman filtering in this learned latent space followed by reconstruction of the full model parameters through a learned lifting map, enabling efficient single-step online adaptation while preserving model expressiveness. Evaluated on online adaptation of neural wireless receivers under time-varying channels and on non-stationary image classification, AURA shows substantial improvements in adaptation speed, accuracy, and computational efficiency over existing online learning and Bayesian filtering baselines, demonstrating that an adaptation-aware latent geometry is beneficial for effective Bayesian online learning in high-dimensional models.

</details>


### [121] [Generalization behavior of OPTQ and the role of regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31560)
*Erin George,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在泛化设定下研究OPTQ及其随机变体stochastic OPTQ的量化误差，推导测试点期望平方误差的界，并基于正则项λ的作用提出新的λ选取建议，实验证明优于既有推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型神经网络可通过将权重量化到更少比特来压缩。OPTQ在给定校准集上逐步量化权重以最小化平方量化误差，但其在校准集之外的泛化性能缺乏理论保证，正则项λ的合理选取也缺少依据。作者希望从泛化角度刻画OPTQ的误差，并改进λ的选择。

Method: 在泛化框架下分析OPTQ与stochastic OPTQ：假设测试点来自固定分布，推导算法所累积期望平方误差的上界。分别证明两个结果：一者将泛化误差与由同分布独立样本构成的校准集上的误差联系起来；另一者针对所有足够"良好"的分布，给出stochastic OPTQ泛化误差的界，且不依赖具体校准集。分析中重点考察正则项λ的作用，并据此提出λ的新选取方案。

Result: 得到两个泛化误差界：其一把泛化误差与校准集误差关联，其二对充分良好的分布给出stochastic OPTQ泛化误差的统一界。这些界均显示正则项λ起关键作用，由此导出的λ选取建议在实验中相较文献中先前的推荐表现更优。

Conclusion: OPTQ及其随机变体在泛化设定下的期望平方误差可由依赖λ的界刻画。基于理论洞察提出的λ选择方式在实验中表现良好，优于已有推荐，为OPTQ的实际调参提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Large neural networks can be compressed by rounding or "quantizing" their weights to numbers that admit representations with fewer bits. One algorithm for quantization, OPTQ, progressively quantizes the weights of a neural network so that the squared quantization error on a specified calibration dataset is as small as possible. We study the performance of OPTQ and a variant algorithm, stochastic OPTQ, in a generalization setting and derive bounds for the expected squared error accrued by the algorithm when a test point is drawn from a fixed distribution. We prove two results. One result relates the generalization error to the error on a calibration dataset comprising independent samples from the same distribution as the test distribution. The other result bounds the generalization error of stochastic OPTQ for all sufficiently nice distributions, regardless of the calibration dataset. In both of these results, the regularization term $λ$ plays an important role. We use insights from these results to make a new recommendation for the choice of $λ$ and see that this choice of $λ$ preforms favorably in experiments when compared to prior recommendations in the literature.

</details>


### [122] [Weight Pair Encoding: Inducing a Smaller Grammar in Neural Network Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31564)
*Irene Tallini,Daniele Solombrino,Alberto Cazzaniga,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Weight Pair Encoding (WeightPE)，通过将损失性Re-Pair压缩器嵌入直通估计器，把神经网络权重微调为可被更小文法描述的字符串，首次将文法大小作为显式训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量化（如int8 QAT）使用固定尺寸码本，压缩率受限且缺乏层级复用特性。作者希望利用文法压缩的变长模式与层级复用能力，显式优化权重可压缩性，从而获得比固定码本更小的模型表示。

Method: WeightPE将网络int8权重展平为一个字符串，在直通估计器（straight-through estimator）内嵌入损失性Re-Pair压缩器；在全局L2预算约束下，把近似匹配的Re-Pair模式改写为完全相等。网络使用重写后的权值前向计算，并通过直通估计器反向训练。该方法可与LZ78、SEQUITUR等不同文法压缩器配合。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上微调的ViT-B/16与ViT-L/16的MLP权重上，WeightPE生成的Re-Pair文法大小分别为等价int8 QAT运行的0.43x和0.38x，代价是1.9和1.1个准确率点。该趋势在未针对性微调的LZ78、SEQUITUR压缩器上同样成立。

Conclusion: 这是首次将文法大小作为网络权重的显式训练目标，表明可通过训练使权重更易被文法压缩，在可控精度损失下获得显著小于固定码本量化的表示，并具备跨压缩器泛化性。

Abstract: We show that neural network weights can be explicilty fintuned to admit a smaller grammar. Weight Pair Encoding (WeightPE) does so by placing a lossy Re-Pair compressor inside a straight-through estimator. The int8 weights of the network are flattened into one string, and near-matching Re-Pair patterns are made exactly equal within a global L2 budget. The network computes with the rewritten weights and trains through them with a straight-through estimator. Unlike a flat codebook of fixed-size entries, a grammar offers variable-length patterns and reuses them hierarchically inside larger ones. On the MLP weights of ViT-B/16 and ViT-L/16 finetuned on CIFAR-10, WeightPE produces a Re-Pair grammar 0.43x and 0.38x the size of the one produced by an equivalent int8 QAT run, at a cost of 1.9 and 1.1 accuracy points. The trend extends to different grammar compressors (LZ78, SEQUITUR), over which the networks has not be finetuned against. To our knowledge, this is the first time grammar size has been used as an explicit training objective for network weights.

</details>


### [123] [Trust Guided Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31586)
*Chainesh Gautam,Raghuram Bharadwaj Diddigi,Chandramouli Kamanchi,Pankaj Dayama,Sumanta Mukherjee,Kameshwaran Sampath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TGDT通过模型自身的下一状态预测误差检测Decision Transformer的上下文分布漂移，并先用共形预测筛选可信上下文、再用冻结critic做价值引导，从而缓解长回合性能退化并提升回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Decision Transformer在长回合中性能退化，其根源是条件上下文逐渐偏离训练分布（上下文漂移）。论文指出该漂移可通过模型自身的下一状态预测误差显现：该误差在回合推进中上升并持续偏高，可作为上下文不可靠的直接信号。而已有的价值引导式上下文选择可能让critic从模型已判定为不可靠的上下文中选动作，因此需要先判定可信性再做价值选择。

Method: 提出Trust Guided Decision Transformer（TGDT），在施加价值引导之前先做上下文选择。每一步对若干近期上下文后缀计算滚动下一状态预测误差，并利用split conformal prediction在留出的离线数据上做校准，仅保留误差处于校准阈值内的后缀；随后用冻结critic在这些可信后缀生成的动作中选取价值最高的动作。该流程反转了“仅价值弹性选择”的顺序。

Result: 在D4RL的导航与运动任务上，状态预测、critic引导、硬上下文重置各自只能解决部分问题。TGDT减少了持续高误差回合的出现，并在回报上优于vanilla Decision Transformer、基于重置的上下文控制以及仅价值上下文选择。

Conclusion: 上下文不可靠性可由模型自预测误差量化，并通过共形校准转化为可信性判据；先筛可信上下文、再做价值引导的顺序是缓解长回合性能退化的有效方案。

Abstract: Decision Transformer performance degrades on long rollouts because the conditioning context drifts out of the training distribution. We show that this drift is visible through the model's own next state prediction error, which rises during rollout and stays elevated, giving a direct signal of when context has become unreliable. We introduce Trust Guided Decision Transformer (TGDT), which selects context before applying value guidance. At each step, TGDT evaluates several recent context suffixes using rolling next state prediction error, calibrated against held out offline data via split conformal prediction. It keeps only suffixes whose error stays within the calibrated threshold, then uses a frozen critic to choose the highest value action among the trusted suffixes. This reverses the order used by value only elastic selection, where the critic may choose an action generated from a context the model itself has flagged as unreliable. Experiments on D4RL navigation and locomotion tasks show that state prediction, critic guidance, and hard context reset each solve only part of the problem. TGDT reduces persistent high error runs and improves return over vanilla Decision Transformer, reset based context control, and value only context selection.

</details>


### [124] [New LoRA Skills Should Read but Never Write](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31600)
*Zeyan Li,Panqi Yang,Qirong Guo,Shengda Zhuo,SIyuan Qiu,Hu Xu,Chun Li,Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 READ 方法，通过将每个 LoRA 适配器改写为平衡规范形式并采用单向耦合，实现多个独立训练适配器的无干扰组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个独立训练的 LoRA 适配器难以组合：权重空间合并会相互干扰，重新训练代价高，而路由多个适配器又放弃了单一组合模型的目标。作者将困难归因于组合方法隐含的两个选择——LoRA 更新的等价分解方式，以及新旧技能耦合的方向。

Method: READ 固定上述两个选择：每个适配器被重写为保持更新精确的平衡规范形式；耦合只单向增长，新技能可读取旧技能的输入子空间但不能写入其输出子空间。每次追加时唯一可训练对象是新技能在耦合矩阵中的行，组合后的更新折叠进基础权重，无推理开销、路由或任务特定规则。

Result: 在四个基准套件和两个模型家族上逐次添加技能评估：READ 在所有套件平均值上超过基于相同适配器的最强已发表基线，在 SuperGLUE 上提升超过 20 分，在领域套件上提升超过 7 分，且几乎所有完整添加序列的最终表现都高于所有直接基线。

Conclusion: 因子坐标与耦合方向是单独适配器从不暴露的要素，却决定了组合技能能否存活；显式固定这两者可显著提升多技能组合效果。

Abstract: Low-rank adapters (LoRA) make it cheap to fine-tune a large language model once per task, but combining several independently trained adapters into one model remains difficult: merging the updates in weight space causes interference, retraining on all task data is expensive, and routing between separate adapters gives up the goal of a single combined model. We trace the difficulty to two choices that every composition method makes implicitly. A LoRA update admits infinitely many equivalent factorizations; the choice among them is invisible while an adapter serves alone, but it determines what a learned interaction between adapters can see. A coupling between an old skill and a new one can likewise point in either direction, and the direction decides whether the old skills keep computing what they computed before. We introduce READ (Read-only Expansion of Adapter Deltas), which fixes both choices: each adapter is rewritten into a balanced canonical form that preserves its update exactly, and the coupling grows in one direction only, so a new skill can read the input subspaces of old skills but cannot write into their output subspaces. The only trainable object at each append is the new skill's row of the coupling matrix, and the composed update folds into the base weights with no inference cost, routing, or task-specific rules. We evaluate READ across four benchmark suites and two model families, adding skills one at a time. Across several families, READ improves every suite average over the strongest published baselines built from the same adapters---by more than twenty points on SuperGLUE and more than seven points on the domain suite---and nearly all complete addition sequences end above every direct baseline. Factor coordinates and coupling direction, which a lone adapter never exposes, are what decide whether composed skills survive.

</details>


### [125] [User Model Extraction via Belief Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31603)
*Ali Holmov,Yiran Huang,Kirill Bykov,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Belief Self-Distillation（BSD）统一读写框架，从自然对话中蒸馏用户的隐式信念表征，既能解码又能写回模型，实现对用户信念的因果操控，并发现拒绝行为依赖模型推断的用户意图及跨模型共享的用户表征几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型隐含地推断用户属性并据此调整行为，但这些信念难以被检视和因果操控。传统探针仅能识别激活中存在的信息，无法验证其因果作用；本文旨在弥合线性探针与因果探针之间的鸿沟，构造既可解码又可写回的状态。

Method: 提出 Belief Self-Distillation（BSD）：以冻结的 LLM 自身作为教师，无需外部标注，从自然对话中蒸馏出紧凑的用户表征；该表征既可通过线性解码读出，也可写回模型以进行干预。相较于常规探针，BSD 隔离出因果角色可直接检验的状态，并与匹配的隐状态引导（hidden-state steering）进行对比。

Result: 在多个模型家族上，BSD 能忠实恢复用户信念，干预效果显著强于匹配的隐状态引导；发现拒绝行为不仅取决于请求本身，还取决于模型推断的用户意图——在请求固定时改变该信念即可改变拒绝；并发现独立训练的 LLM 在用户表征上收敛出共享几何结构。

Conclusion: 隐式用户模型是可读且可因果写入的内部状态，对 AI 安全具有直接意义：模型的安全决策会依据其所认为的交互对象而条件化，因此信念层面的读写能力为安全对齐与行为控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) implicitly infer attributes of their users and adapt their behavior accordingly, yet these beliefs remain difficult to inspect and causally manipulate. We introduce Belief Self-Distillation (BSD), a unified read-write framework that bridges linear and causal probing by learning a compact user representation that can be both decoded and written back into the model. The frozen LLM acts as its own teacher, distilling beliefs from natural conversations without external annotations. Unlike conventional probing, BSD isolates not only information present in activations, but a state whose causal role can be directly tested. Across multiple model families, BSD faithfully recovers user beliefs and enables substantially stronger interventions than matched hidden-state steering. Crucially, we find that refusal depends not only on the request, but on the model's inferred user intent: changing this belief alters refusal while holding the request fixed. We further uncover a striking cross-model regularity: independently trained LLMs converge on a shared geometry for representing their users. Together, these results reveal implicit user models as readable and causally writable internal states with direct implications for AI safety, shaping how models condition safety decisions on whom they believe they are interacting with.

</details>


<div id='bioRxiv.Neuroscience'></div>

# bioRxiv.Neuroscience [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Bassoon is required for adult ocular dominance plasticity and experience-induced changes in synapsin phosphorylation in visual cortex](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.21.753144v1)
*Schoene, C.,Montenegro-Venegas, C.,Guhathakurta, D.,Greifzu, F.,Barthel, B.,Boehner, J.,Pothula, S.,Pina-Fernandes, E.,Beth, A.,Annamneedi, A.,Heine, M. D.,Gundelfinger, E. D.,Loewel, S.,Fejtova, A.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究揭示成年小鼠眼优势（OD）可塑性依赖突触前支架蛋白bassoon（Bsn）介导的突触前可塑性，而幼年OD可塑性不依赖Bsn，机制涉及经验诱导的synapsin磷酸化变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉经验依赖的神经环路精细调校对最优感知至关重要，但已有知识主要集中于OD可塑性的突触后机制，对其突触前分子贡献知之甚少。本研究旨在探究突触前分子bassoon在OD可塑性中的作用。

Method: 采用组成型Bsn缺失小鼠（BsnΔEx4/5）及皮层谷氨酸能神经元条件性缺失小鼠（BsnEmx1），结合成年与幼年小鼠单眼剥夺后V1反应的电生理检测、空间视觉评估、培养神经元中的电生理与活细胞成像（诱发的突触囊泡融合），以及突触囊泡相关synapsin磷酸化分析。

Result: BsnΔEx4/5小鼠成年OD可塑性受损而幼年OD可塑性正常；BsnEmx1小鼠成年OD可塑性同样受损，表明皮层谷氨酸能突触的突触前可塑性在该过程中被选择性需要。BsnΔEx4/5小鼠空间视觉严重受损，而BsnEmx1小鼠空间视觉保留。培养神经元中Bsn缺失不影响由AMPA受体转运增加介导的失活诱导突触后AMPA反应缩放，但突触前缩放被完全消除。单眼剥夺诱导对侧V1突触囊泡相关synapsin磷酸化增强，而BsnEmx1小鼠中该效应缺失。

Conclusion: 成年OD可塑性需要Bsn依赖的突触前可塑性，涉及经验诱导的synapsin磷酸化变化；幼年OD可塑性则不依赖Bsn。

Abstract: Sensory stimulation enables experience-dependent fine tuning of brain circuits for optimal perception. In juvenile mice, brief closure of the contralateral eye reduces primary visual cortex (V1) activation through the deprived eye. In contrast, in adult animals, eye closure induces potentiated V1-responses to non-deprived eye visual stimulation. While considerable knowledge exists about postsynaptic mechanisms underlying this ocular dominance (OD) plasticity, little is known about contributing presynaptic molecules. Here we report that constitutive deletion of the presynaptic scaffolding protein bassoon (Bsn{Delta}Ex4/5) disrupted OD-plasticity in V1 of adult mice leaving juvenile OD-plasticity unaffected. Notably, in mice with a conditional deletion of Bsn only from cortical glutamatergic neurons (BsnEmx1), adult OD-plasticity was also impaired indicating that normal presynaptic plasticity of glutamatergic cortical synapses is selectively required in this process. While spatial vision was severely compromised in Bsn{Delta}Ex4/5 mice, it was preserved in BsnEmx1 mice, consistent with normal Bsn expression in subcortical circuit in these mice. Parallel experiments in cultured neurons revealed that deletion of Bsn left the inactivity-induced postsynaptic scaling of AMPA-responses mediated by increased AMPA receptor trafficking unchanged. However, presynaptic scaling was completely abolished as assessed by both electrophysiology and live-cell imaging of evoked synaptic vesicle fusion in cultured primary cortical neurons. Finally, monocular deprivation induced enhanced phosphorylation of synaptic vesicle-associated synapsin in contralateral V1 that was absent in BsnEmx1 mice. Overall, our data indicate that adult OD-plasticity requires Bsn-dependent presynaptic plasticity, involving experience-induced changes in synapsin phosphorylation. In contrast, juvenile OD-plasticity does not appear to be dependent on Bsn.

</details>


### [127] [A bipolar disorder-associated ultra-rare AKAP11 protein-truncating variant attenuates stimulus-dependent PKA activation and induces anxiety- and depression-related behaviors in mice](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.19.752887v2)
*Luo, A. X.,Deng, L.,Li, Y.,Zhang, X.,Li, J.,Zhu, X.,Mao, X.,Su, Y.,Wu, B.,Ross, C. A.,Li, P. P.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究利用CRISPR-Cas9构建了携带人类双相障碍超罕见AKAP11蛋白截断变异（PTV）的小鼠模型，发现该变异导致Akap11单倍剂量不足，雄性杂合小鼠表现焦虑抑郁样行为，新皮层出现PKA信号异常、突触相关分子改变及树突发育受损，为精神疾病超罕见PTV致病机制提供精准模型与治疗测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神疾病相关的罕见遗传变异被认为效应量更大且具直接生物学作用，有助于理解遗传结构与疾病表型的因果关系。全外显子测序荟萃分析发现双相障碍和精神分裂症患者中AKAP11超罕见蛋白截断变异（PTV）负荷增加。AKAP11编码A激酶锚定蛋白，介导PKA与其底物的结合。然而，超罕见AKAP11变异如何导致精神疾病发病机制尚不清楚，因此需要建立临床相关且遗传精确的模型加以研究。

Method: 采用CRISPR-Cas9基因组编辑技术，将一名双相障碍患者中发现的超罕见PTV引入小鼠Akap11位点。对突变小鼠进行分子层面表征（mRNA/蛋白水平、mRNA异构体分析），并开展行为学测试；对新皮层进行转录组与蛋白质组分析；检测PKA调节亚基与催化亚基表达、PKA底物磷酸化水平、皮质兴奋性神经元的刺激依赖性PKA活性，以及皮质神经元新生树突发育情况。

Result: 该超罕见PTV降低了Akap11 mRNA和蛋白水平，未产生稳定的新型mRNA异构体，导致Akap11单倍剂量不足。雄性杂合小鼠表现出符合焦虑和抑郁相关表型的行为异常。新皮层转录组和蛋白质组揭示涉及突触组织、突触信号和神经突形态发生的分子改变。值得注意的是，杂合突变体新皮层中I型（而非II型）PKA调节亚基及PKA催化亚基选择性升高。功能上，这些改变伴随PKA底物磷酸化降低、皮质兴奋性神经元刺激依赖性PKA活性减弱，以及皮质神经元新生树突发育受损。

Conclusion: 本研究构建了一个临床相关、遗传精确的小鼠模型，为理解超罕见PTV如何促进精神疾病发病机制提供了机制性见解，并可作为测试合理治疗方案的平台。

Abstract: Rare genetic variants associated with psychiatric disorders are thought to have larger individual effect sizes and direct biological roles, offering a unique opportunity for understanding causal relationships between genetic architecture and disease phenotypes. A recent whole-exome sequencing meta-analysis identified an increased burden of ultra-rare protein-truncating variants (PTVs) in AKAP11 in patients with bipolar disorder and schizophrenia. AKAP11 encodes an A-kinase anchoring protein that mediates the association of protein kinase A (PKA) and its substrates. How ultra-rare AKAP11 variants contribute to psychiatric disorder pathogenesis remains unknown. Here, using CRISPR-Cas9 genome editing, we introduced into the mouse Akap11 locus an ultra-rare PTV identified in a human patient with bipolar disorder. This ultra-rare PTV reduced Akap11 mRNA and protein levels without producing stable novel mRNA isoforms, leading to Akap11 haploinsufficiency. Male heterozygous mice exhibited behavioral abnormalities consistent with anxiety- and depression-related phenotypes. Transcriptomic and proteomic profiling of the neocortex revealed molecular changes involving synaptic organization, synaptic signaling, and neurite morphogenesis. Notably, the heterozygous mutant showed selective elevation in type I, but not type II, PKA regulatory subunits, as well as PKA catalytic subunits, in the neocortex. Functionally, these changes were accompanied by reduced phosphorylation of PKA substrates, attenuated stimulus-dependent PKA activity in cortical excitatory neurons, and impaired nascent dendritic development in cortical neurons. Together, our study presents a clinically relevant and genetically precise mouse model that provides mechanistic insight into how ultra-rare PTVs may contribute to psychiatric disorder pathogenesis, and a platform for testing rational therapeutics.

</details>


### [128] [Predictive perception via simultaneous learning and inference](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.24.754081v1)
*Enan, M.,Senden, M.,Janik, P.,Vidal, Y.,Auksztulewicz, R.,de Martino, F.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该论文提出SLIM模型：将环境状态建模为离散，从而使贝叶斯滤波精确化；其信念形状不受限制，学习内容简化为一个由局部规则在线更新的转移矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然世界的状态空间是连续的、高维的，精确贝叶斯推断难以实现。变分方法通过限制后验形式来近似，采样方法用有限样本表示后验，但二者都是在后验上做近似而非状态空间。作者希望在近似位置做出不同选择。

Method: 将环境状态建模为离散，使贝叶斯滤波精确，信念形状不受约束，所需学习内容缩减为一个转移矩阵，并由局部规则在线更新。SLIM将精确滤波与门控Hebbian规则结合，从推断状态而非观测状态学习转移；并给出层次化实例化以复现局部与全局预测误差效应。代价是状态需预先指定。

Result: 模拟中，SLIM在有感觉噪声下恢复环境动态，并在动态变化时自适应，仅当传感器变得无信息时误差才增长；层次实例化复现了局部与全局预测误差效应；应用于两项噪声下的听觉决策实验，复现了期望的行为特征，并提供试次水平的期望与惊讶度量。

Conclusion: 在小离散状态空间上的精确推断配合单一局部Hebbian规则，足以解释这些现象，无需显式优化目标，也无需对后验进行参数化近似。

Abstract: Perception has been proposed to involve an inferential process that combines noisy sensory evidence with prior expectations to estimate the latent states of the environment. Exact Bayesian inference is intractable in the continuous, high-dimensional state spaces of the natural world. Variational schemes address this by restricting the form of the posterior, whereas sampling schemes represent the posterior with a finite set of samples. In both cases the approximation concerns the posterior rather than the state space over which it is defined. Here we place the approximation elsewhere. Rather than restricting the form of the posterior, we restrict the state space, modeling environmental states as discrete and thereby making Bayesian filtering exact. Beliefs are then unconstrained in shape and what has to be learned can be reduced to a transition matrix updated online by a local rule. The cost is that the states, rather than the distribution, must be specified in advance. The Simultaneous Learning and Inference Model (SLIM) combines this exact filtering with a gated Hebbian rule that learns transitions from inferred rather than observed states. In simulations SLIM recovers environmental dynamics under sensory noise and adapts when those dynamics change, with error growing only once the sensor becomes uninformative. A hierarchical instantiation reproduces local and global prediction error effects. Applied to two auditory decision-making experiments under noise, SLIM reproduces behavioral signatures of expectation and supplies trial-level measures of expectation and surprise. Exact inference over a small discrete state space with a single local Hebbian rule is therefore sufficient to account for these phenomena, without an explicit optimization objective and without a parametric approximation to the posterior.

</details>


### [129] [Imaging mitral/tufted glomeruli in the mouse olfactory bulb using the genetically encoded voltage indicator ArcLight](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.22.753601v1)
*Leong, L. M.,Lloyd, E.,Storace, D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究利用基因编码电压指示器ArcLight在小鼠嗅球中进行活体成像，成功解析了僧帽/丛状细胞glomerular输出的兴奋性与抑制性信号、浓度响应异质性及呼吸耦合动态，并验证多种新型GEVI的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嗅球中每种嗅觉受体类型映射到特定glomerulus，其输出由僧帽/丛状细胞轴突与复杂突触网络共同塑造。既往双光子Ca2+成像显示glomerular输出在气味浓度信息处理上具有异质性，但其电压动态尚不清楚，因此需要直接测量电压信号的方法。

Method: 使用基因编码电压指示器（GEVI）ArcLight，在麻醉小鼠体内对僧帽/丛状细胞glomerulus输出进行活体成像；开展跨数周的重复成像以评估表达稳定性；并在宽浓度范围内检测气味诱发信号，同时比较多种新型GEVI在落射荧光成像中的表现。

Result: ArcLight能够分辨兴奋性和抑制性气味诱发信号；其表达在多周成像中保持稳定，并可在较大浓度范围内捕捉气味特异性和浓度特异性的激活模式；可解析异质的浓度-响应敏感性和呼吸耦合动态；多种新型GEVI同样可用于落射荧光成像测量气味诱发信号。

Conclusion: ArcLight及其他新兴GEVI可作为解析小鼠嗅球中感觉信息编码与转换机制的有力工具。

Abstract: In the mammalian olfactory bulb, each olfactory receptor neuron type maps to receptor-specific channels called glomeruli. These input axons interact with the apical dendrites of mitral/tufted cells, which are projection neurons that send axons to the olfactory cortex. Therefore, each glomerulus reflects the input-output relationship for a different olfactory receptor type, which is transformed by a complex synaptic network. While prior 2-photon Ca2+ imaging experiments have shown that the glomerular output is heterogeneous with respect to how odor concentration information is processed, the nature of voltage dynamics remains unclear. Therefore, we used the genetically encoded voltage indicator (GEVI) ArcLight to image the glomerular output in vivo in anesthetized mice. We found that ArcLight could resolve both excitatory and suppressive odor-evoked signals from mitral/tufted glomeruli. ArcLight expression remained stable over multi-week imaging sessions and successfully captured odor- and concentration-specific patterns of activation across a large concentration range. Importantly, this approach allowed us to resolve heterogeneous concentration-response sensitivities and respiratory-coupled dynamics. We also demonstrate that several newer GEVIs can similarly measure odor-evoked signals using epifluorescence imaging. Our study establishes ArcLight and other emerging GEVIs as powerful tools for dissecting how sensory information is encoded and transformed through the mouse olfactory bulb.

</details>


### [130] [Mapping Mesolimbic Dopamine Projections to Subcortical Circuits Underlying Adaptive Behavior: A Human 7T Diffusion Tractography Atlas](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.22.753583v1)
*Elliott, B. L.,Mopuru, R.,Hoffman, L. J.,Leong, J. K.,Volkow, N. D.,Olson, I. R.,Murty, V. P.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究利用高分辨率7T扩散磁共振成像（N=173）重建了人类中脑边缘多巴胺系统的七条双侧VTA-皮层下通路，揭示了VTA-海马通路的长轴与亚区组织以及VTA-NAc的上下地形，并发现通路微结构与延迟折扣的局部关联，发布了开放的概率图谱MesoConnect。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中脑边缘多巴胺系统参与显著性、学习、记忆与决策等多种适应性行为，但其支撑这些功能的结构组织在人类中尚未被完整描绘。

Method: 采用高分辨率7T扩散磁共振成像（样本量至N=173），重建七条双侧VTA-皮层下通路，涵盖直接中脑边缘投射与海马-VTA（HPC-VTA）环路调控回路；进行投射终点分析、神经突密度协变分析与逐节点微结构-行为关联分析。

Result: 投射终点分析揭示了VTA-海马通路的长轴与亚区组织及VTA-NAc的上-下地形；神经突密度在中脑边缘通路间呈协变，而在弓状束中无此现象，支持回路水平组织；逐节点分析发现通路微结构与延迟折扣在VTA-海马、VTA-NAc和VTA-杏仁核通路上存在局部关联。

Conclusion: 研究将人类中脑边缘系统解析为支撑适应性行为的多条独立通路；发布概率图谱MesoConnect并提供教程，便于在独立数据集中重建与研究相关回路。

Abstract: The mesolimbic dopamine system supports broad aspects of adaptive behavior including salience, learning, memory, and decision making, yet the structural organization supporting these functions remains incompletely mapped in humans. Using high-resolution 7T diffusion magnetic resonance imaging (up to N=173), we reconstructed seven bilateral ventral tegmental area (VTA)-subcortical pathways spanning direct mesolimbic projections and the hippocampus-VTA (HPC-VTA) loop regulatory circuit. Projection-endpoint analyses revealed long-axis and subfield organization of VTA-hippocampal pathways and superior-inferior VTA-nucleus accumbens (NAc) topography. Consistent with the HPC-VTA loop framework, neurite density covaried across mesolimbic pathways but not the arcuate fasciculus, demonstrating circuit-level organization. Nodewise analyses identified localized associations between pathway microstructure and delay discounting across VTA-hippocampal, VTA-NAc, and VTA-amygdala pathways. Together, these findings parse the human mesolimbic system into distinct pathways supporting adaptive behavior. The probabilistic atlas, MesoConnect, is freely available with tutorials to facilitate reconstruction and investigation of circuits in independent datasets.

</details>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Structured Bayesian Modeling of Dynamic Receptive1 Fields in Salamander Retinal Ganglion Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30731)
*Alokesh Manna*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文将动态感受野估计建模为高维贝叶斯问题，用空间高斯马尔可夫随机场与时间自回归过程的泊松模型实现时空平滑连贯，在蝾螈视网膜神经节细胞数据上得到连贯曲面与三类时间响应表型，并在模拟中优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感受野估计需从少量试次中为每像素每时间窗估计系数，属高维问题，需正则化；但LASSO等稀疏正则化在各时间点独立选择像素，缺乏空间连贯性与时间平滑性约束，即使真实响应平滑演化，估计结果仍可能碎裂或不连续，无法刻画应有的演化模式。

Method: 将动态感受野估计表述为高维贝叶斯问题：泊松模型融合空间上的高斯马尔可夫随机场与时间上的自回归过程，使估计场在时空上平滑连贯；对每个神经元独立拟合。随后以空间平均时间响应概括各神经元曲面，并用基于模型的功能聚类进行聚类，以BIC选择簇数。另开展已知真值的模拟研究。

Result: 在155个蝾螈视网膜神经节细胞记录上，模型恢复出连贯曲面，而像素级Poisson-LASSO对比给出碎裂结果；以空间平均时间响应聚类，BIC选择三类均衡的时间响应表型（85、32、38个神经元），而对原始曲面聚类则得到退化分组。模拟研究中同样模式得到验证，模型在恢复与估计精度上优于未正则化泊松GLM、LASSO和弹性网，但LASSO在假阳性控制上更好。

Conclusion: 该模型可有效实现时空连贯的动态感受野估计，并支持基于功能聚类的群体表型分型；其贡献包括逐神经元感受野识别、与LASSO的对比以及功能聚类群体分型，尽管在假阳性控制方面不及LASSO。

Abstract: Neurons in the visual system are selective for specific spatial and temporal stimulus features, described by their \emph{receptive field}. Estimating one means a coefficient per pixel per time bin from few trials -- a high-dimensional problem requiring regularization. Sparse regularizers such as the LASSO handle the dimension but select pixels independently at each time point, with nothing to keep the region coherent in space or smooth in time; it can fragment or reorganize discontinuously even when the true response evolves smoothly, a failure since this evolving pattern is what a receptive-field estimate should capture. We formulate dynamic receptive-field estimation as a high-dimensional Bayesian problem: a Poisson model combining a Gaussian Markov random field in space with an autoregressive process in time, so the estimated field is smooth and coherent across space and time. On recordings from $155$ salamander retinal ganglion cells, fitting this model independently per neuron recovers a coherent surface, where a pixel-level Poisson-LASSO comparison instead returns a fragmented one. Summarizing each neuron's surface by its space-averaged temporal response and clustering these curves with a model-based functional-clustering procedure, BIC selects three balanced temporal-response phenotypes ($85$, $32$, $38$ neurons), against a degenerate grouping from clustering the raw surfaces. A simulation study with known ground truth confirms the same pattern, with the model beating an unregularized Poisson GLM, LASSO, and the elastic net on recovery and estimation accuracy, though LASSO controls false positives better. The per-neuron field identification, its contrast with LASSO, and the functional-clustering population typing constitute this paper's contribution.

</details>


### [132] [Landscape Limits of Quantum-Inspired Evolutionary Optimization across 256 continuous functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30938)
*Rishi Govind,Ferdin Sagai Don Bosco,Kasturi Venkata Srikanth,Aman Mittal,Abhishek Singh,Aditya Singh,Abhishek Chopra*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文对量子启发式进化优化（QIEO）进行大规模基准评测，用508个连续函数覆盖11类地形特征，对比3种QIEO变体、2种GA编码与CMA-ES，明确其竞争条件与独立变量表示的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QIEO以量子比特幅度对表示设计变量、每代向单一精英旋转，更新廉价、几乎无参数、易于并行，因而在工程、设计与规划中被广泛采用，并被认为相对遗传算法具有显著优势。但其将设计变量视为独立概率分量，理论上无法利用局部曲率、各向异性与变量耦合，这一根本性问题缺乏系统性验证，因此需要严谨的基准研究厘清其真实能力边界。

Method: 从既有文献中选取256个（实为508个：256个unshifted + 252个shifted，4个无法shift）连续函数，覆盖连续性、可微性、可分离性、可扩展性、模态、凸性、条件数、对称性、最大维度、维度依赖性与变量耦合模式共11类地形特征；使用3种QIEO变体、2种GA编码以及Hansen的协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）进行求解，并从计算成本、求解精度及对各地形特征的特化表现三个维度评估结果。

Result: 结果识别出QIEO具备竞争力的具体条件，厘清了其独立变量表示在何处成为性能瓶颈，并判定特定QIEO变体是否在特定地形特征上具有优势；同时与GA编码和CMA-ES形成横向对照，量化了QIEO相对经典方法的定性判断与计算优势是否成立。

Conclusion: QIEO的适用性具有明显条件依赖性：其近乎无参数、低成本的幅度旋转更新在部分地形上有效，但独立概率分量的建模方式限制了其对曲率、各向异性与变量耦合的利用，性能在相应特征上受限；研究给出了变体选择与问题匹配的实证依据，而非笼统宣称其优于GA。

Abstract: Quantum-inspired evolutionary optimization (QIEO) represents design variables as a set of qubits and searches a continuous, multi-dimensional landscape through rotation of the qubit's amplitude pair. Every generation rotates those amplitudes toward a single elite, which corresponds to that generation's best. The update is cheap, almost parameter-free, and well-suited for massive parallel implementation, which has encouraged its adoption in engineering, design, and planning applications. However, there are critical issues with this formulation, principally, the treatment of design variables as independent probability components which make it incapable of exploiting local curvature, anisotropy, or variable coupling. Despite this, QIEO is believed to hold promise, and has been used extensively to solve real-world problems, with significant qualitative and computational advantage over its classical counterpart, Genetic Algorithm (GA).
  A collection of 256 (actually 508; 256 unshifted + 252 shifted, 4 could not be shifted) continuous function are selected from the prior works, in such a way that they represent eleven landscape characteristics, namely continuity, differentiability, separability, scalability, modality, convexity, conditioning, symmetry, maximum dimensionality, dimension dependency, and the coupling pattern of the design variables. These functions are then solved by three QIEO variants, two GA encodings and Hansen's Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES).
  The results are evaluated in terms of computational cost, solution precision, and specialization across landscape characteristics. They identify the conditions under which QIEO provides competitive performance, clarify where its independent-variable representation becomes limiting, and establish whether particular QIEO variants offer advantages for specific landscape characteristics.

</details>
